## 117. Stage 2B/2C Myriad scale-up — bidirectional mid-layer disruption + §111.5b "reverse null" overturned (2026-05-09) [finding][infra] #finding #infra

### 117.1 Setup

DGX→quark Tailscale→Myriad SSH chain newly opened (笔记 §117.6 infra). Stage 2B forward (24 strong-tier tasks) + Stage 2C reverse (15 reverse-tier tasks) run on Myriad A100 80GB compute node `node-v00a-001` / `node-u00a-001`. ~30min wallclock each (V100 estimate had been 24h — A100 80GB ~48× speedup). Output dirs:
- `results/mechanistic/stage2b_curated_b1_cls_myriad/`
- `results/mechanistic/stage2c_reverse_curated_b1_cls_myriad/`

Both rsync'd back to DGX via tar-pipe through SSH chain.

### 117.2 Headline finding — bidirectional disruption (overturns §111.5b)

§111.5b reported "reverse null at all layers" based on **N=1 single task** (task 0 reverse direction): overlap→source = 0.51 (baseline) at all 36 layers, no patch effect. Concluded "asymmetric encoding".

**Scale-up to N=15 reverse tasks reveals different picture**:

| Layer | Forward overlap→tgt (N=24) | Reverse overlap→tgt (N=15) | Forward LD→tgt | Reverse LD→tgt |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| L0  | 0.98 | 0.88 | 0.9 | 2.4 |
| L5  | 0.97 | 0.89 | 1.3 | 2.6 |
| **L11** | 0.91 | **0.82** | 3.9 | **6.7** |
| **L17** | **0.86** | **0.81** | **6.0** | **6.9** |
| L23 | 0.93 | 0.87 | 2.2 | 2.8 |
| L29 | 0.95 | 0.93 | 2.5 | 1.1 |
| L35 | 1.00 | 1.00 | 0.0 | 0.0 |

Reverse direction shows **mid-layer L11-L17 disruption magnitude similar to forward** (Δoverlap→tgt: forward -0.143 ± 0.217 / reverse -0.193 ± 0.256 at L17). Welch's t @ L17 forward vs reverse p=0.535 (not distinguishable — consistent with bidirectional mechanism, but failure to reject ≠ proof of equality).

### 117.3 Statistical significance (paired t-test + Holm-Bonferroni, `scripts/analysis/stage2_layer_significance.py`)

Per-task paired test layer L_n vs L35 baseline. One-sided H1: disruption (overlap < baseline OR LD > baseline). Holm correction across 6 tested layers.

| Direction × Metric | Holm-significant layers | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| Forward × overlap→tgt | **L17** (p_Holm=0.011 \*\*) | L11 marginal (p_Holm=0.092) |
| Forward × LD→tgt | **L17** (p_Holm=0.024 \*) | L11 marginal (p_Holm=0.080) |
| Reverse × overlap→tgt | **L11 + L17** (p_Holm=0.044 \* / 0.033 \*) | strongest signal |
| Reverse × LD→tgt | None Holm-significant | L17 raw p=0.014 marginal Holm 0.084 (N=15 power-limited) |

**L17 robust mid-layer mechanism layer** confirmed across 2 directions × 2 metrics (3 of 4 Holm-significant). L11 emerges as secondary in reverse but marginal in forward.

### 117.4 §111 task-0 finding re-interpretation

§111 reported "L11 flips 93% match in forward task 0" — that's **task-specific outlier**, not population pattern. 24-task forward average shows L11 overlap→tgt=0.908 (Δ=-0.093 from baseline), much weaker than task-0's near-flip. Distribution has long tail (some tasks fully flip, some don't disrupt). §111.5 "Stage 2A logit_shift L17 peak" is **consistent with N=24 finding** — both Stage 2A (5-task aggregate) and Stage 2B (24-task aggregate) point to L17 as primary.

Marked: §111 task-0 single-case study should NOT be cited as paper §5 representative finding. Use 24-task L17 aggregate + ±1σ band + Holm p-value instead.

### 117.5 Selection-bias caveat — 2x2 control launched

Forward 24 tasks ≠ reverse 15 tasks. They're curated by direction-specific composite score (`scripts/mechanistic/curate_mirage_tasks.py`):
- `composite = mirage_score × (1 + divergence)` where `mirage_score = (src_neg - tgt_neg) + (tgt_aff - src_aff)`
- Strong-tier: composite ≥ 1.0 (forward-easy mirage)
- Reverse-tier: composite ≤ -1.5 (reverse-easy mirage)

Composite is **task-text-only** (no patching leak), but plausibly correlates with patching effect size — strong-tier may be high-effect-size subset. Bidirectional finding could reflect mechanism universality OR task curation correlation.

**2x2 control launched 2026-05-09 morning** (commit `9d67387`):
- Cell A (existing): forward × strong (24) — done
- Cell B (existing): reverse × reverse (15) — done
- Cell C (new): forward × reverse-tier (15) — qsub 335339, qw
- Cell D (new): reverse × strong-tier (24) — qsub 335340, qw

Decision tree post-2x2:
- If C+D show similar L17 disruption → mechanism universal (paper §5 confident bidirectional claim)
- If C+D null/weak → mechanism is task-class-specific, paper §5 must caveat or scope to curated subsets

### 117.6 Infrastructure — DGX-driven Myriad workflow established

DGX→quark Tailscale→quark Cisco AnyConnect→Myriad SSH chain verified working (B-81 umbrella + new 7th class B-81g UTF-8 ASCII codec fix). Watcher cron `scripts/maintenance/glm/myriad_watcher.py` installed (5min ntfy on state change). DGX never directly reaches UCL gateway (IP-restricted) — quark Windows is mandatory bastion. Documented in `docs/reference/COMPUTE_INFRASTRUCTURE.md` + `memory/reference_dgx_to_ucl_chain.md`.

### 117.7 Open questions for paper §5 prose update

1. **Effect size**: L17 forward overlap drop -0.143 ± 0.217 (~14% match degradation). Reviewer: "is 14% drop strong evidence?" Need to contrast with random-injection control (zero effect baseline).
2. **Task heterogeneity**: std band ±0.22 ≈ 1.5× effect size at L17. Some tasks no disrupt, some near-flip. Need median+IQR alternative reporting OR per-task scatter alongside aggregate.
3. **Bidirectional vs asymmetric framing**: only commit after 2x2 cells C/D resolve. If bidirectional confirms, lit anchor is Q5 Gemini DR "bidirectional modality fusion". If asymmetric returns (cells C/D null), revert to §111.5b style "image-grounded encoding asymmetry" framing.
4. **L11 vs L17 hierarchy**: Stage 2A peaked L17, Stage 2B forward L17 strongest, Stage 2B reverse both L11+L17. Possible "L11 = mirage emergence onset, L17 = mirage commitment / decision lock-in" two-stage interpretation. Speculative — paper section needs careful wording.

### 117.8 Pending follow-up actions

- [ ] Wait for cells C/D (~30 min A100 each, qw queued)
- [ ] Once cells C/D done: re-run `scripts/analysis/stage2_layer_significance.py` with all 4 cells
- [ ] If 2x2 confirms universal → update `paper_planning §5` + `paper_drafts/section5*.md` to bidirectional framing
- [ ] If 2x2 shows selection bias → caveat language in paper §5 + 笔记 §117 update
- [ ] Regenerate forward + reverse figures with corrected `(forward)` / `(reverse)` title labels (commit `c8e76bd` script fix already in repo, just needs re-plot)
- [ ] Update `project_paper_hook.md` memory: replace "§111.5b asymmetric encoding" with "117.x bidirectional mid-layer mechanism (provisional, 2x2 pending)"


## 118. Cell F cross-site replication on reddit (2026-05-09) [finding] #finding

**Cell F (forward × reddit × strong-tier N=24)** completed 2026-05-09 15:17 BST on Myriad Bagpuss queue (A100, 42 min wall — 10× faster than V100 estimate). Pulled to DGX via DGX→quark→Myriad cat-base64 chain (5 files, byte-exact match).

### Per-layer Holm-corrected significance (overlap_to_target metric)

| Layer | overlap→tgt mean | Δ vs L35 | t-stat | p_raw | **p_Holm** | Reject H0 |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|:---:|
| L0  | 0.885 | -0.115 | -2.48 | 0.010 | **0.031 ✓** | yes |
| L5  | 0.937 | -0.063 | -1.95 | 0.032 | 0.063 | no |
| **L11** | **0.747** | **-0.253** | **-4.02** | **<0.001** | **0.002 ✓✓** | yes |
| **L17** | **0.772** | **-0.228** | **-3.62** | **<0.001** | **0.004 ✓✓** | yes |
| L23 | 0.847 | -0.153 | -2.74 | 0.006 | **0.023 ✓** | yes |
| L29 | 0.937 | -0.063 | -1.54 | 0.069 | 0.069 | no |

### Cross-site replication confirmed

| Site | L17 p_Holm (overlap→tgt) | L11 p_Holm | L17 LD→tgt mean | L17 LD→tgt median (G8 sparse) |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| classifieds (Cell A, fwd × strong N=24) | 0.011 ✓ | not significant | 6-8 (varies) | **0** (sparse) |
| **reddit (Cell F, fwd × strong N=24)** | **0.004 ✓✓ (3× tighter)** | **0.002 ✓✓ (NEW)** | 8.000 | **0** (sparse) |

**Key finding**: L11 + L17 mid-layer mechanism replicates on reddit with 2-3× tighter Holm p-values than cls. **Mechanism is NOT cls-specific** — Qwen3-VL-4B mid-layer mirage mechanism is observed on at least 2 VWA sites.

### Implications for paper

- **§8.1 external validity caveat loosens**: "phantom mechanism in Qwen3-VL family on VWA-style tasks (cls + reddit, 2 sites)" — was effectively "1 site evidence" before.
- **§5 mechanism narrative**: cross-site replication is a strong reviewer-rebuttal point. Cell F prose: "the mid-layer disruption signature observed on classifieds (Cell A, p_Holm=0.011 at L17) replicates on reddit (Cell F, p_Holm=0.004 at L17 and 0.002 at L11), strengthening the cross-site mechanism claim."
- **G8 sparse-mechanism caveat preserved**: median LD→tgt = 0 at L17 on reddit, mirroring cls. Mean (8.0) carried by ~25% high-salience-image task subset.
- **Cell G pending** (reverse × reddit × reverse-tier N=15): when done, will complete the 2x2 cross-site validation matrix (forward+reverse × reddit, mirroring cls Cells A+B).

### Caveats

- Cell F is forward × strong only — reddit reverse (Cell G) needed to confirm 2x2 symmetry on cross-site.
- L11 + L23 also Holm-significant on reddit but not on cls — possible site-specific layer recruitment, or cls had more sparse signal that didn't reach Holm. Disclose in §5 as "cls Cell A + reddit Cell F together flag L11/L17/L23 region; the per-site Holm-significant subset is non-identical and discussed as exploratory."
- Sample size N=24 still modest — 16-cell rerun version of Cell F would tighten further.

### Pending follow-up

- [ ] Pull Cell G (reverse × reddit × reverse N=15) when ETA ~20:00 BST today completes.
- [ ] Re-run `stage2_layer_significance.py` with cells A/B/C/D/F/G (6 cells) → write final mechanism §5 prose.
- [ ] Update `_status/cells/cell_*.md` for cellf_* entries.
- [ ] Regenerate `paper_drafts/section5_mechanism_reddit.md` with cross-site reddit numbers explicit (currently uses cls Cell A as anchor).
- [ ] Push 14 + N audit commits + 笔记 entry.

## 120. Cell G reddit reverse + 4-cell bidirectional mid-layer mechanism (2026-05-09) [finding] #finding

**Cell G (reverse × reddit × reverse-tier N=15)** completed 2026-05-09 20:10 BST on Myriad V100 (job 336424, name `cellg_rev_`). Pulled to DGX via auto_pull_myriad_cell.sh (5 files, 21s wall-clock). Together with §118's Cell F (forward × reddit × strong-tier N=24), reddit 现有完整 forward+reverse 2x1 (2x2 reddit control 仍未跑).

### Cell G layer-resolved Holm-corrected significance (overlap_to_target metric)

| Layer | overlap→tgt mean | Δ vs L35 | t-stat | p_raw | **p_Holm** | Reject H0 |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|:---:|
| L0  | 0.817 | -0.183 | -2.07 | 0.029 | 0.086 | no |
| L5  | 0.799 | -0.201 | -2.47 | 0.013 | 0.054 | no |
| **L11** | **0.757** | **-0.243** | **-2.63** | **0.010** | **0.049 ✓** | yes |
| **L17** | **0.729** | **-0.271** | **-2.89** | **0.006** | **0.036 ✓** | yes |
| L23 | 0.873 | -0.127 | -1.64 | 0.062 | 0.124 | no |
| L29 | 0.896 | -0.104 | -1.61 | 0.064 | 0.124 | no |

### 4-cell mid-layer mechanism summary (cls Cells A+B + reddit Cells F+G)

| Cell | site × dir × tier × N | L11 p_Holm | L17 p_Holm |
|---|---|---:|---:|
| Cell A | cls × fwd × strong × 24 | 0.092 ✗ | 0.011 ✓ |
| Cell B | cls × rev × reverse × 15 | 0.044 ✓ | 0.033 ✓ |
| Cell F | reddit × fwd × strong × 24 | **0.002 ✓✓** | **0.004 ✓✓** |
| Cell G | reddit × rev × reverse × 15 | 0.049 ✓ | 0.036 ✓ |

- **L17 mid-layer mechanism**: Holm-sig in **all 4 cells** — cross-site (cls + reddit) × cross-direction (forward + reverse) replication.
- **L11 mid-layer mechanism**: Holm-sig in 3/4 cells (cls fwd is the only miss; reddit fwd is the strongest at p=0.002).
- **Reddit > cls magnitude**: reddit forward Cell F shows ~3× tighter p_Holm than cls forward Cell A (likely site-specific signal-to-noise: reddit text payload more informative per token, cls more visual-grounding-dependent).

### Bidirectional symmetry per site (Welch's t @ L17 overlap_to_target)

| Site | Forward Δ ± std (N) | Reverse Δ ± std (N) | Welch p (two-sided) | Bidirectional? |
|---|---|---|---|:---:|
| cls | -0.143 ± 0.217 (24) | -0.193 ± 0.256 (15) | 0.535 | yes |
| reddit | -0.228 ± 0.308 (24) | -0.271 ± 0.363 (15) | 0.705 | yes |

Forward and reverse magnitudes are statistically indistinguishable on **both** sites — consistent with a bidirectional mid-layer information channel rather than a one-directional flow (P-SoM mirage info ↔ SoM full-multimodal hidden states are mutually substitutable mid-layer).

### Implications for paper

- **§5 mechanism narrative upgraded**: was "mid-layer disruption signature on cls only (1 site, 1 direction Holm-sig)"; now "**bidirectional mid-layer mechanism replicated across 2 sites × 2 directions** (4/4 cells L17 Holm-sig, 3/4 cells L11 Holm-sig)". Reviewer-rebuttal worthy.
- **Q5 lit anchor (bidirectional fusion)**: Cell A+B+F+G empirical witness aligns with paper_planning §3.2 Q5 6/6 Gemini-DR finding — mid-layer 是 fusion locus, 不是 single-modality dominance.
- **Caveats preserved**:
  - 2x2 control on reddit (Cells C-r fwd × reverse-tier, D-r rev × strong-tier) **未跑** — selection-bias artifact 仍 possible. cls 已有 Cells C+D,reddit 没. Disclose §5 paragraph.
  - Reverse-direction tasks (15) curated by REVERSE composite score, forward by FORWARD composite — task subsets differ. cls 已用 2x2 控制此 confound, reddit 缺.
- **§118 typo correction**: §118's cross-site comparison table mistakenly had cls Cell A L17 = 0.011 paired with reddit Cell F L17 = 0.004 in the same table column header (`overlap→tgt`); both numbers are correct individually. The "**3× tighter**" framing stands (cls 0.011 vs reddit 0.004 ≈ 2.75×).

## 121. Cell C+D 2x2 selection-bias control (cls) — partial asymmetry confirmed (2026-05-09) [finding] #finding

**Cell C (forward × reverse-tier N=15)** + **Cell D (reverse × strong-tier N=24)** completed earlier 2026-05-09 on Myriad (qsub 335339/335340 launched §117.5). Both pulled to DGX. Closes the cls 2x2 selection-bias control matrix announced in §117.5.

### 2x2 cls layer-resolved Holm-sig (overlap_to_target @ L11/L17)

| Cell | direction × tier × N | L11 p_Holm | L17 p_Holm | Holm-sig? |
|---|---|---:|---:|:---:|
| **Cell A** | forward × **strong-tier** × 24 | 0.092 | **0.011** | L17 ✓ |
| **Cell B** | reverse × **reverse-tier** × 15 | **0.044** | **0.033** | L11+L17 ✓ |
| **Cell C** | forward × **reverse-tier** × 15 | 0.257 | 0.257 | **✗ NULL** |
| **Cell D** | reverse × **strong-tier** × 24 | **0.008** | **0.010** | L11+L17 ✓ |

(LD_to_target metric tracks: Cell C L11/L17 p_Holm = 0.201/0.201 ✗; Cell D L11/L17 p_Holm = 0.037*/0.037* ✓ — same conclusion.)

### Asymmetry interpretation

The "diagonal" cells A+B fired (matched direction × tier). The off-diagonal cells reveal direction-tier interaction:

- **Cell C NULL** = forward patching on reverse-curated tasks does **NOT** produce mid-layer disruption.
- **Cell D Holm-sig** = reverse patching on strong-curated tasks **does** produce mid-layer disruption (matches Cell B magnitude qualitatively).

Implication: **strong-tier tasks (composite ≥ 1.0)** carry mirage signal usable in BOTH directions; **reverse-tier tasks (composite ≤ -1.5)** carry mirage signal usable in REVERSE direction only.

The composite curation score (`scripts/mechanistic/curate_mirage_tasks.py`):
```
composite = mirage_score × (1 + divergence)
mirage_score = (src_neg - tgt_neg) + (tgt_aff - src_aff)
```
is symmetric by sign reversal, but its **forward and reverse sub-pools partition the task population non-symmetrically** — reverse-tier tasks are a strict direction-locked subset, strong-tier tasks are a "mirage-strong-in-both-directions" subset.

### Implications for paper

- **§5 mechanism prose** can claim "**bidirectional mid-layer mechanism on strong-curated tasks** (Cells A+D both Holm-sig L17)" without selection-bias caveat. This is the cleanest paper claim.
- **Beyond strong-tier**, mechanism is **direction-tier-conditional** — must caveat. Cell C NULL means we cannot generalize "forward direction always disrupts" to all-task population without further controls.
- **Bidirectional symmetry remains valid in the strong-tier subspace**: Cell A (fwd × strong) Δ=-0.143, Cell D (rev × strong) Δ=??? (need to extract from /tmp/cell_cd Welch). Cross-direction Welch on 2x2 diagonal mismatch (Cell C vs Cell D L17 overlap): p=0.282 — magnitudes not distinguishable but C is null so this is power-limited.
- **Reviewer-rebuttal** answer to "is this just task curation correlation?": "No — strong-curated tasks (24+24=48 forward/reverse) show mechanism in BOTH directions; only reverse-curated tasks are direction-locked (15 reverse-only)."

### Caveats

- Cell C N=15 is small — power to detect a real but smaller forward effect on reverse-tier is limited. p_Holm=0.257 is "not Holm-sig" not "proven null". 95% CI for L11 Δ = [-0.235, -0.012] technically excludes 0 (raw p=0.043), but Holm correction across 6 layers + N=15 inflates penalty.
- Reddit 2x2 (Cells C-r + D-r) **not run** — paper §5 must restrict the "strong-tier bidirectional mechanism" claim to cls until reddit 2x2 closes (or accept reddit data as 2-cell evidence with cls 4-cell as the bidirectional anchor).
- Composite score itself is correlation-based heuristic — strong vs reverse partition is empirical, not theoretically justified.

### Files generated

- `results/mechanistic/layer_significance_cellcd_cls2x2_20260509.md` (Cell C+D analysis)
- Existing: `results/mechanistic/stage2b_2x2_fwd_revtasks_myriad/` (Cell C raw data) + `results/mechanistic/stage2c_2x2_rev_strongtasks_myriad/` (Cell D raw data)

### Pending follow-up

- [ ] Update `paper_drafts/section5_mechanism_reddit.md` "selection-bias caveat" paragraph to reflect 2x2 result: NOT a generic selection bias — it's a direction-tier-conditional mechanism, with strong-tier showing bidirectional and reverse-tier showing direction-locked.
- [ ] Reddit 2x2 (C-r + D-r) — Myriad qsub when free slot. Otherwise §5 caveat: "the 2x2 control was conducted on cls only; reddit cross-site evidence is 2-cell (Cells F+G), one direction per task subset".
- [ ] Update §117.7 #3 ("bidirectional vs asymmetric framing") — verdict: bidirectional **on strong-tier**, direction-locked on reverse-tier. Hybrid framing replaces the binary choice.


## 122. Cell E random-injection negative control — content-specificity confirmed (2026-05-09) [finding] #finding

**Cell E (forward × strong-tier × random-injection control N=24)** completed earlier 2026-05-09 on Myriad. Same 24 strong-tier tasks as Cell A, same forward direction, same source/target mode pair (`som → phantom_som`), but **the patched activations are sampled from a random control source** rather than the genuine SoM-with-image source. Negative-control purpose: rule out that the mid-layer disruption observed in Cells A/B/C/D/F/G is a **generic perturbation effect** (any random replacement at mid-layer breaks output) rather than a **content-specific transfer** (the mirage hidden states carry information about the SoM source).

### Cell E layer-resolved aggregates (N=24, raw — no Holm needed because L35 baseline itself broken)

| Layer | overlap→tgt mean | LD→tgt mean | overlap→src mean |
|---|---:|---:|---:|
| L0  | 0.028 | 17.21 | 0.032 |
| L5  | 0.084 | 9.71 | 0.027 |
| L11 | 0.029 | 32.79 | 0.017 |
| L17 | 0.040 | 29.62 | 0.015 |
| L23 | 0.026 | 36.25 | 0.010 |
| L29 | 0.044 | 22.75 | 0.024 |
| L35 | 0.034 | 28.88 | 0.019 |

### Contrast with Cell A (real-patching positive control, same 24 tasks)

| Layer | Cell A overlap→tgt (real) | Cell E overlap→tgt (random) | Diff |
|---|---:|---:|---:|
| L35 (baseline) | **1.000** | **0.034** | -0.966 |
| L17 (peak disrupt) | 0.857 | 0.040 | -0.817 |
| L11 | 0.908 | 0.029 | -0.879 |

### Three signature differences vs Cells A-G

1. **L35 baseline broken**: Cell A's no-patch baseline is overlap→tgt = 1.000 (target output perfectly reproduces itself). Cell E's L35 baseline is **0.034** — random patching at the very last layer already destroys generation. → **The Holm-paired-vs-L35 test framework does not even apply to Cell E**; the baseline is not a baseline.

2. **Flat across layers, no mid-layer peak**: Cell A drops 1.000 → 0.857 specifically at L17 (-14%, then partial recovery at L23/L29 back to 0.93/0.95). Cell E is flat at 0.03-0.08 across all 7 sampled layers — no L11/L17 peak, no L23+ recovery, no L0 robustness either. → **Random injection is layer-agnostic destructive**; mid-layer is not privileged.

3. **Magnitude is generic break, not content transfer**: Cell A's L17 disruption = -14% from baseline, residual signal preserved (0.857 still high). Cell E's overlap is uniformly ~3-8% (residual is at random-bigram level, not content-preserving). → **Cell E shows what "model-collapse-from-bad-injection" looks like; Cells A-G's mid-layer signature is structurally different**.

### Implications for paper

- **§5 mechanism content-specificity claim survives review**: Cell E is the answer to "could your L17 disruption just be 'patching breaks model'?" — No. Random patching breaks model uniformly across all layers and far more catastrophically than Cell A's L17 drop. The Cell A/B/F/G mid-layer signature **carries information specific to the SoM-with-image source**.
- **§5 should report a Cell A vs Cell E "real vs random" contrast figure or table**, ideally with both curves on the same y-axis. This is a stronger reviewer rebuttal than 4-cell Holm tables alone.
- **Q5 lit anchor (bidirectional fusion)** strengthened: bidirectional info channel (Cells A+B+F+G) + content-specific (vs Cell E random control) = mid-layer is acting as a **content-bearing fusion locus**, consistent with multimodal fusion literature.

### Caveats

- Cell E uses **random injection** but the manifest's `random_inject` field shows `None` (script schema predates F18+F19 audit fix that added `random_inject` provenance to incremental JSON). Confirmed Cell E IS the random-injection variant by directory naming (`stage2b_celle_random_cls_strong_myriad`) + L35 baseline ≠ 1.000 (only random-injection runs break L35) + same 24 task IDs as Cell A. **Provenance gap**: re-run Cell E post-F18+F19 to lock manifest if paper-grade rerun cycle resumes.
- N=24 single random seed — replicating with multiple random seeds would tighten the "random injection invariance across draws" framing. Lower priority (the all-layer-flat signature is robust).
- Cell E only run on **cls forward strong-tier**. Reddit random control (Cell E-r) **not run** — paper §5 must restrict the negative control claim to cls or accept cls Cell E as proxy for both sites (random injection mechanism is not site-specific; this is a defensible shortcut).

### Files generated

- `results/mechanistic/stage2b_celle_random_cls_strong_myriad/` (5 files raw data; `patching_heterogeneity_curves.png` extra from earlier per-task variance analysis)

|---|---|:---:|---|
| A | cls × fwd × strong × 24 | ✓ (0.011) | §117 |
| B | cls × rev × reverse × 15 | ✓ (0.033) | §117 |
| C | cls × fwd × **reverse-tier** × 15 | ✗ NULL (0.257) | §121 |
| D | cls × rev × **strong-tier** × 24 | ✓ (0.010) | §121 |
| E | cls × fwd × strong × 24 × **random** | n/a (no real baseline; flat all-layer) | §122 |
| F | reddit × fwd × strong × 24 | ✓ (0.004) | §118 |

**Pending cells (not yet executed)**:
- C-r (reddit fwd × reverse-tier 15) — reddit 2x2 control

## 123. Reddit 2x2 + random control closure — site-asymmetric mid-layer mechanism (2026-05-10) [finding] #finding

**Cell C-r (reddit fwd × reverse-tier N=15)** + **Cell D-r (reddit rev × strong-tier N=24)** + **Cell E-r (reddit fwd × strong × random N=24)** all completed 2026-05-10 on Myriad Bran queue (A100, 30-50 min wall each, 8h 50min queue wait from 2026-05-09 21:13 → 2026-05-10 06:03 first start). Auto-pulled via cron `myriad_watcher` GONE_HOOKS dispatch (Cell E-r needed manual trigger because watcher cron 5min lag exceeded conversation window). Closes the reddit 4-cell selection-bias + content-specificity controls.

### Reddit 2x2 + random Holm results

| Cell | site × dir × tier × N | L11 p_Holm | L17 p_Holm | Holm-sig L17? |
|---|---|---:|---:|:---:|
| **F**  (existing §118) | reddit × fwd × **strong-tier** × 24 | **0.002 ✓✓** | **0.004 ✓✓** | yes |
| **G**  (existing §120) | reddit × rev × **reverse-tier** × 15 | 0.049 ✓ | **0.036 ✓** | yes |
| **C-r** (NEW) | reddit × fwd × **reverse-tier** × 15 | **0.012 ✓** | **0.012 ✓** | yes |
| **D-r** (NEW) | reddit × rev × **strong-tier** × 24 | 0.116 ✗ | **0.041 ✓** | yes |
| **E-r** (NEW) | reddit × fwd × strong × 24 × **random** | n/a (baseline broken) | overlap→tgt all-layer mean ~0.01-0.09 (vs L35=0.021) | n/a |

**4/4 reddit cells L17 Holm-sig**. Random Cell E-r matches cls Cell E qualitatively (uniformly destructive, no L17 selectivity).

### Site-asymmetric mid-layer mechanism (8-cell unified table)

| Cell | site | dir | tier | N | L17 p_Holm | L17 Δ |
|---|---|---|---|---:|---:|---:|
| A   | cls    | fwd | strong  | 24 | 0.011 ✓     | -0.143 |
| B   | cls    | rev | reverse | 15 | 0.033 ✓     | -0.193 |
| C   | cls    | fwd | reverse | 15 | **0.257 ✗** | -0.103 |
| D   | cls    | rev | strong  | 24 | 0.010 ✓     | -0.193 |
| F   | reddit | fwd | strong  | 24 | **0.004 ✓✓** | -0.228 |
| G   | reddit | rev | reverse | 15 | 0.036 ✓     | -0.271 |
| C-r | reddit | fwd | reverse | 15 | **0.012 ✓**  | -0.320 |
| D-r | reddit | rev | strong  | 24 | 0.041 ✓     | -0.155 |

**Key cross-site contrast**:
- **cls: 3/4 Holm-sig, 1 NULL (Cell C)** — mid-layer mechanism is **direction-tier conditional**: forward direction × reverse-tier tasks fail to disrupt mid-layer.
- **reddit: 4/4 Holm-sig** — mid-layer mechanism is **universal** across all (direction, tier) combinations.

### Site-asymmetry interpretation (paper §5 reframe)

Original hypothesis (per §121 cls 2x2): "selection bias is partial — strong-tier bidirectional, reverse-tier direction-locked". Reddit data **falsifies the universality** of this conditional pattern:

- **Reddit C-r magnitude** (Δ -0.320 at L17, p_Holm=0.012) is **larger** than cls Cell A magnitude (Δ -0.143, p_Holm=0.011) — i.e. on reddit even the off-diagonal (forward × reverse-tier) shows STRONGER mid-layer disruption than cls's diagonal (forward × strong-tier).
- **Reddit D-r magnitude** (Δ -0.155 at L17) is comparable to cls Cell A but smaller than cls Cell D (-0.193) — reddit reverse direction × strong-tier shows weaker than cls counterpart.

Two candidate explanations (paper §5 should pick one or disclose both):

1. **Site-specific text density** — reddit text payload (post titles + comment threads) is denser than cls visual product listings. mid-layer mechanism in 4-billion-parameter Qwen3-VL is **less reliant on visual cues on reddit**, so the mechanism activates broadly across task subsets regardless of curation direction. cls relies more on visual grounding, so the mechanism is **direction-tier modulated** by which task subset matches the patching direction's information flow.
2. **Composite-score validity site-dependent** — `mirage_score = (src_neg - tgt_neg) + (tgt_aff - src_aff)` is computed from text-only features. On reddit text dominates, so composite captures real direction-asymmetric mirage signal; the score's strong/reverse partition is well-aligned with the actual patching effect. On cls visual features matter more, composite under-captures, so partition is poorly aligned with patching effect — Cell C's NULL emerges from this mismatch (forward direction fails on tasks whose "reverse-tier" label is text-based but actual visual pattern is forward-aligned).

### Implications for paper §5

- **Bidirectional + cross-site claim survives strongly**: 7/8 cells L17 Holm-sig (88%), with magnitudes -0.10 to -0.32. Welch's cross-direction symmetry on cls (p=0.535) and reddit (p=0.705) confirms forward/reverse magnitudes statistically indistinguishable.
- **Selection-bias caveat changed shape**: paper §5 should NOT claim "strong-tier supports both directions; reverse-tier is direction-locked" as a universal property — that's cls-specific. Cleaner framing: "**reddit shows a fully universal mid-layer mechanism; cls shows a direction-tier conditional mechanism**". Site-asymmetric framing replaces the old cls-only direction-tier conditional framing.
- **Content-specificity confirmed on both sites**: cls Cell E + reddit Cell E-r both show all-layer ~0.02-0.09 random output (vs real patching baseline 1.000 → 0.85-0.91 selective L17 dip). Random injection cannot be the mechanism behind Cells A-D/F-G/C-r/D-r mid-layer disruption.
- **Reddit becomes the cleaner anchor for paper §5 main figure**: 4/4 universal mechanism is a stronger reviewer-rebuttal point than 3/4 conditional (no NULL cell to explain). cls remains as **cross-site site-asymmetry evidence** showing that mechanism's universality is itself site-modulated.

### 124.1 Stage 3 mechanism attribution — H-text + H-prompt cells (cls + reddit, 4 cells + 1 pending)

**Goal**: isolate whether the **[SOM_MARKS] text** alone, or the **SoM prompt** alone, suffices for the L11/L17 mid-layer disruption observed in Cells A/B/F/G (Stage 2, all phantom_som target).

- Hp_cls (cls × fwd × strong × **target=phantom_prompt**, N=24)
- Ht_red (reddit × fwd × strong × **target=phantom_text**, N=24)
- Hp_red (reddit × fwd × strong × **target=phantom_prompt**, N=24)

**Holm-sig L11/L17 results** (overlap_to_target):

| Cell | target | site | L11 p_Holm | L17 p_Holm | L11 mean | L17 mean |
|---|---|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| Ht_cls | phantom_text | cls | 0.002 ✓✓ | 0.002 ✓✓ | 0.743 | 0.745 |
| Hp_cls | phantom_prompt | cls | 0.009 ✓✓ | 0.011 ✓ | 0.756 | 0.777 |

### 124.2 BUG — phantom_prompt target was P-SoM repeat (CRITICAL)

`run_stage2b_continuation_pilot.py:273-276` hardcoded `som_marks_text` as text payload for ALL target_modes including `phantom_prompt`. But P-prompt definition = SoM prompt + **AXTree** text (no marks). So H-prompt-red first run (340265) produced **byte-identical patched_text to Cell F** for all 24 reddit tasks at L11 — fully verified by md5.

**Fix**: introduce `text_payload_for(mode)` lambda mapping:
- `som / phantom_som / phantom_text` → som_marks_text
- `phantom_prompt / dom / phantom_dom` → obs_text (AXTree)
- `vision` → ""

**Affected past data**: ONLY Stage 3 H-prompt-cls (340263 qdel'd before complete) + H-prompt-red (340265 invalid 24-task). All other Stage 2/3 cells (A/B/C/D/E/F/G/Cr/Dr/Er + Ht_cls/Ht_red) used target=phantom_som or phantom_text which were unaffected.

**Re-launched** as 342330 + 342331 with fixed script (uploaded via SSH base64 chain at 13:43, jobs started 13:53+, finished 16:10/18:15). Sanity check: post-fix H-prompt-cls L11 per-task 9/24 incidental match with Cell A (chance level, not byte-identical). Bug fully resolved.

### 124.4 Framing correction wave — three user critiques, each load-bearing

**Critique 1 (transfer correlation Spearman robust check)**: my earlier "Cell A L11 ρ=-0.851 strong fusion" verdict was Pearson-artifact. Sorted by Δ_to_target, trim top-3 + bottom-3 → Cell F flipped from ρ=+0.329 catastrophic to ρ=-0.485 modest fusion. Across all 12 cells **Spearman is POSITIVE everywhere** (0.02 to 0.76), meaning rank-wise: tasks with larger Δ_target also have larger Δ_source (i.e., patched output drifts AWAY from source as target shifts). **Robust verdict: no clean fusion at mid-layer, just disruption**. Earlier "site-asymmetric mechanism" framing from §123 was Pearson-noise-driven.

**Critique 2 ([SOM_MARKS] vs AXTree is FORMAT not INFO)**: cls task 1 step 2 empirical comparison shows AXTree (73 lines, 4188 chars, 70 elements with `[N]` tags) and [SOM_MARKS] (70 marks, 4169 chars) have **1.00× character ratio + identical element content** — labels, URLs, role attrs all preserved. The ONLY difference is hierarchy indentation (tab-prefixed parent-child structure) stripped from marks block. Text-axis is **structural format (hierarchical vs flat)**, NOT lean/rich information bottleneck.

This invalidates the entire "P-text has thinner context" mechanistic framing from earlier work. **Memory file written**: `memory/project_phantom_space_axes_format_not_information.md` documenting the correction + 6 rules for future framing.

**Critique 3 (existing failure-mode/SR/action-distribution data is pre-§116 contaminated)**: `docs/analysis/phantom_paper/phantom_dom_vs_som_diagnostic.md` § 4 failure-mode buckets, `axis_effect_size_report.md` cascade decomposition, `som_vs_phantom_som_diagnostic.md` — all derived from pre-§116 baseline runs with early-stop bug present. Cannot use to verify H1+H2 framework predictions. **Stage 2 patching raw data unaffected** (single-step page-snapshot inference, not multi-step trajectory derived). 16-cell paper-grade rerun (post-§116) needed for valid behavioral verification.

### 124.5 Mechanism framing v3 (post-critique synthesis) — "elicitation diversity" not "fusion locus"

Combined synthesis of three critiques: phantom space 3 arms are **non-substitutable policy elicitations** of the same Qwen3-VL-4B model, not separable mid-layer representations. Two axes:
- **H1 Format-axis** (AXTree hierarchical vs [SOM_MARKS] flat): triggers different **training-distribution-induced priors** (browser-agent SFT for hierarchical AXTree; multimodal grounding + tool-calling SFT for numbered flat list).
- **H2 Prompt-axis** (DOM descriptive vs SoM referential): triggers different **task-conditional decision priors** across Q5 4 sub-dims (commitment / verbosity / register / sequencing).

4 cells = 2x2 (training-prior × decision-prior) elicitation grid. Each cell evokes a distinct functional regime from same model. Mid-layer hidden states are nearly identical (cosine 0.998+ same-prompt, AUROC=1.0 mode-classification trivially separable from input-encoding alone); divergence happens in downstream computation policy. Drop-one routing value (1.7-3.3pp) + Jaccard 0.29-0.49 emerge from policy-level elicitation diversity, NOT representation-level orthogonality.

**P-SoM HERO status**: per `meta_phantom_lift.md` DerSimonian-Laird random-effect, P-SoM drop-in pooled +2.34pp [1.30, 3.37] with **I²=0% (zero heterogeneity)** vs P-text (+2.44pp [0.32, 4.56], I²=71%) — P-SoM is statistically the **most across-cell-consistent** routing arm, not mechanism-privileged.

**Falsifiable predictions per H1+H2 v3**:
1. Action distribution differs per axis ✓ (axis_effect_size_report cascade — pre-§116 only, but pattern likely robust)
2. Mid-layer representation similar same-prompt ✓ (Stage 1 linear probe trivial / Stage 4 partial PCA cosine 0.998)
3. Patching disrupts no transfer ✓ (Stage 2/3 Spearman positive 12/12 robust)
4. Each cell drop-one routing value ✓ (phantom_lift.md meta-analysis)
5. ⏳ Per-cell failure modes reflect regime mismatch (P-text more early-finish than P-SoM in cls 51.5% vs 44.5% — pre-§116 data partially confirms; needs 16-cell rerun)

| pair | L11 cos | L17 cos | L23 cos | axis |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---|
| P-prompt ↔ P-SoM | 0.9987 | 0.9984 | 0.9980 | text-axis (same SoM prompt, marks-vs-AXTree) |
| P-prompt ↔ SoM | 0.9667 | 0.9583 | 0.9708 | image-axis (no-image vs with-image) |
| P-SoM ↔ SoM | 0.9693 | 0.9615 | 0.9733 | image-axis |

**Image-axis 3.0-3.3% gap vs text-axis 0.13% — 25× asymmetry at mid-layer cosine geometry**, matching SR-level image-axis dominance (axis_effect_size_report finish_rate / step_count / click_frac). 3-layer triangulation: SR-behavioral + mid-layer cosine + patching disruption magnitude all show same image-axis dominance.

### 124.10 Cell H-d-cls 2x2 additivity verdict (added 2026-05-10 22:50 BST after data landed)

Cell H-d-cls (344623) finished 22:50 BST on Bran A100, auto-pulled. **2x2 additivity test**:

| Metric | Layer | Predicted (Ht_cls + Hp_cls − A) | Observed Hd_cls | Diff | Verdict |
|---|---|---:|---:|---:|---|
| overlap | L11 | -0.4092 | -0.3317 | +0.0775 | sub-additive (small) |
| **overlap** | **L17** | **-0.3342** | **-0.3092** | **+0.0250** | ✅ **ADDITIVE (~5% of main)** |
| LD | L11 | +10.75 | +11.92 | +1.17 | ✅ additive |
| LD | L17 | +8.83 | +11.21 | +2.37 | super-additive (small) |

**Key reading on canonical L17 overlap metric**: interaction term (+0.025) is 5% of main effects sum (-0.50), well within "approximately additive" range. Other layer/metric combinations show small (5-15% of main) interaction terms, dominantly sub- or super-additive depending on combination.

**Cell H-d-cls (DOM target) shows largest disruption of all 4 cells** at L17 overlap: -0.31 vs P-SoM -0.14 / P-text -0.26 / P-prompt -0.22. Monotone gradient: target shared axes with source ↓ disruption ↑. Bare DOM (no SoM prompt, no marks) most disrupted.

**Implication for paper §5 v3 elicitation-diversity framing**: each axis activates independent (training-prior, decision-posture) dimensions; effects compose approximately linearly in mid-layer disruption magnitude. **Factorial elicitation grid hypothesis confirmed at L17 overlap level** — dominantly additive 2x2 with small interaction terms (vs fusion-locus theory which would predict either saturation or strong synergy).

Manual computation 22:55 yielded above table. Lesson: monitor post-loop bash should be simple stat-only (no embedded python eval) — leave compute to manual / dedicated analysis run.

### 125.2 Method 4.2 PCA cosine gap — paper-grade 6-mode discrimination

`scripts/analysis/stage4_pca_cosine_gap.py`. Three metrics per (mode pair, layer):
- Cosine gap = 1 − cos(mean_A, mean_B)
- AUROC via projection onto (mean_A − mean_B) direction
- Per-(mode, layer) PCA top-10 variance explained

**Findings**:
- **All 540 (15 pairs × 36 non-trivial layers) AUROC = 1.000** (only DOM↔P-prompt L36 = 0.998). 6 phantom-space modes form linearly separable clusters in residual stream.
- **Three-tier axis hierarchy validates paper §5 v3 elicitation-diversity framing**:
  - image-axis (vs SoM/Vision): peak cosine gap **0.06** at L4–L17
  - text-axis ([SOM_MARKS] vs AXTree): peak **0.025** at L23
  - prompt-axis (SoM-prompt vs DOM-prompt alone): peak **0.007** at L36
  - Magnitude ratio image:text:prompt ≈ 10:4:1.
- **P-SoM identity** (paper §5 HERO arm): P-SoM's closest mode at every layer 4–36 is **P-text** (L17 cosine 0.0028 vs P-SoM↔SoM 0.0412 = 14.7× more distant). Refutes "P-SoM = SoM minus image"; supports "P-SoM = P-text + SoM-prompt tweak". Format-axis anchored memory (`project_phantom_space_axes_format_not_information.md`) directly validated by mechanism-level data.
- **Disruption locus convergence**: P-SoM↔SoM peak L17 = 0.0412 matches Stage 2 patching disruption locus exactly. Two independent methods (interventional patching Cell A–H + observational PCA cosine gap) converge.

### 125.3 Method 4.2 robustness suite — 5/5 paper-grade defense pass

`scripts/analysis/stage4_robustness.py`:
- **Test A (label permutation)**: Real AUROC 1.000 vs perm mean 0.629 ± 0.038, p=0.005, **9.8σ above noise**. AUROC=1.000 NOT achievable from random labels (perm baseline = 0.63 in high-dim, not 0.5).
- **Test B (per-task consistency)**: 100% of 24 tasks show positive cosine gap for all 6 inspected pairs at L17, std/mean ≤ 10%. Not driven by outliers.
- **Test C (per-step invariance)**: P-SoM↔SoM L17 step 2 = 0.0414 vs step 5 = 0.0411. Mechanism step-invariant.
- **Test D (silhouette score)**: L17 = 0.471, L23–L36 = 0.51–0.52. ≥0.5 = "strong clustering structure" (standard criterion). Mid-output layers form well-separated mode clusters.
- **Test E (bootstrap 95% CI, n=1000 task resample)**: P-SoM↔SoM L17 = 0.0413 [0.0403, 0.0422], width 4.6% of mean. All key pairs exclude zero by >>3σ.

### 125.4 Method 4.4 v1 null was calibration bug, NOT mechanism asymmetry

v1 smoke (`run_stage4_method44_steering.py`, 4 task-steps, α ∈ {0.5, 1, 2, 5}, L17): 0/4 switch toward P-SoM at all α. I initially framed this as "multimodal multi-step asymmetry vs Wu et al. tool calling 93%".

**Diag** (`diag_stage4_method44_layer_check.py`, α=50 + 5 conditions including random control): hook IS firing (random direction at α=50 → output collapses to "."), direction IS causally meaningful (real direction at α=50 shifts output to "We are on search results for red Toyota" — matches P-SoM baseline content). v1 null was just α too small relative to mid-layer hidden state magnitude.

### 125.5 Method 4.4 v2 sweep — layer × α dose-response

`run_stage4_method44_v2_sweep.py` (smoke 2 tasks × 2 steps × 6 layers × 5 α = 128 generations, **full 24-task version running on DGX 2026-05-11 evening, ~2h**).

Smoke results:
- **Direction norms grow 15× across depth**: L11=4.48 → L34=68.24. Same α scaled = wildly different effective shift.
- **L33 α=10 sweet spot**: 50% shift rate + 25% JSON valid. Highest cell.
- **Mid-layer (L11, L17) max shift = 25% at α=2–5, JSON 100% valid** → readable but not effectively steerable at small α.
- **Late-layer (L33, L34) high shift but breaks JSON envelope** → over-steers.
- **L23 = 0% shift across all α**: peculiar dead zone, possible cosine-gap peak layer for text-axis pairs but mean-diff direction doesn't transfer to generation.

**Verdict**: 50% << Wu et al.'s 93% on tool calling, but matches Ma & Rui Qwen3-family pattern (probe works + causal patching uneven).

### 125.7 Ma & Rui 2026 probe-vs-causal distinction — paper §5 framing gift

`docs/literature/wheres_the_plan_paper_note.md` (arXiv:2605.07984, 2026-05-08, Ma & Rui). Rhyme couplet planning task:
- **Linear probe finds planning-compatible representation at newline across all model families** (Qwen3, Gemma 3, Llama 3.1, 270M–70B)
- **Activation patching shows only Gemma-3-27B causally uses newline as planning site** (corrupt-rhyme rate 67% [57, 75] at L33). Qwen3-32B (1%) and Llama-3.1-70B (2%) keep using original rhyme word position.

**Probe-causality dissociation = paper-grade framework for my Method 4.2 vs 4.4 tension**:
- Method 4.2 AUROC=1.000 = **planning-compatible representation** (Ma & Rui terminology)
- Method 4.4 v2 L33 α=10 = 50% shift = **partially causally active planning site**
- Stage 2/3 Cell A-H L17 patching disruption = **replacement-patching causal evidence** (Ma & Rui's protocol applied)

**My Qwen3-VL-4B sits in Qwen3 family pattern**: probe works, causal patching weaker than Gemma. Matches Ma & Rui's Table 6.3 finding. Updated `paper.bib`: added `maRui2026planning` entry.

(2) 你 ablation 里有跑过 L17 mid-layer vs L34 late-layer 对比吗 — 我 mid-layer max 25% shift; (3) SAE direction (你录音里建议方向) 是不是 Qwen3-VL-4B 自训路径合理?"

`★ Insight`:
- v1 null framing 错: 不是 multimodal mechanism asymmetry, 是 α + layer calibration. 一定不能 ship 给 reviewer.
- v2 layer specialization 真 finding: mid-layer L17 = discrimination locus (paper §5 v3 ✓), late-layer L33 = generation locus (Wu et al. + Ma & Rui Gemma anchor).
- Wu et al. + Ma & Rui simultaneous arrival 5/8 is **paper-grade gift** — 2 strong anchors land at exactly the moment I needed them, framing my paper §5 from "probe-only mechanism" to "3-method evidence stack positioned in established probe-causal dissociation landscape".

### 126.1 Hermes 文献副驾 first run (5/6-5/11 window, paper-grade signal/noise)

设置: WeChat-side Hermes agent + 我给的 prompt (`实验笔记 §125` 时未写, 在 ChatGPT/Claude 协作设计阶段). 关键词白名单 4 层 L0-L4 (从 phantom routing space mechanism anchor 到 broad AI awareness), narrative mapping 强制 (每条命中必须挂 P79 5 narrative 之一), score 3 严格 (≤3 篇 / 期, 没货就标 0 不凑数), 双轨输出 (vault digest + 微信 short push).

First run 结果:
- 5 天窗口 (2026-05-06 至 2026-05-11), 200 arxiv submissions + 29 GitHub repos (stars>50, topic:agents/llm/vlm/web-agent, pushed past 3 days)
- 筛 11 hits (筛除率 95%): 1 篇 score 3 + 5 篇 score 2 + 5 个 score 1
- **0 个 false positive** (agent 主动 self-audit "本期没明显 FP")
- Narrative mapping 严格: 每条都写 "P79 关联" 一句话, 没有 keyword-only 撞名词
- arxiv ID 5 篇全准确, 跟 paper.bib 现有 entry 对得上

**Score 3 命中**: Wu et al. 2026 "Tool Calling is Linearly Readable and Steerable" (2605.07990v1) — 我笔记 §19 (4-09) 已 grok, paper.bib placeholder 已 1+ month. Hermes correctly surfaces as score 3 + 标 "validates P79 §5 假说: 模型内部 encode 了 routing decision".

**Score 2 命中** (5 篇):
1. Peale et al. 2026 "Flexible Routing via Uncertainty Decomposition" (2605.07805v1) — paper §6 routing 直击, uncertainty decomp + regret bound
2. **Ma & Rui 2026 "Where's the Plan?"** (2605.07984v1) — paper §5 probe-vs-causal vocabulary
3. "Safe, or Simply Incapable?" phone-use agent safety (2605.07630v1) — paper §8 cross-capability

Item 2 (Ma & Rui) + Item 4 (HDMI) 已 user 在 Hermes 端拉给我 → 已在 §125.7 整合. **NEW: Item 1 (Peale) + Item 4 (HDMI) + Item 5 (Lin & Liu) 是这次额外捡到的**.

**Tool dynamics**: WeChat 端 Hermes 直接通过 web agent 拿 arxiv listing + LLM 评分, **每 3 天 cron 触发**, 双轨 (vault `auto_digest/2026-MM-DD-digest.md` 写历史 + 微信 short summary 推通知). 完全 hands-off 后, 周一/周四 8AM BST 自动出 digest.

### 126.3 HDMI completeness × selectivity → harmonic mean reframe (paper-grade narrative flip)

**Before** (Method 4.4 v2 smoke 用 raw shift rate):
- L33 α=10: 50% shift toward P-SoM → "sweet spot"
- 但 JSON 仅 25% valid — high shift + high envelope-breakage

**After** (HDMI H-mean reliability framework adopted):
- Reliability = 2·c·s/(c+s), c=completeness (shift rate), s=selectivity (JSON valid rate)
- **L17 α=5 H-mean 0.44** (c=29% + s=100%) ← actual sweet spot
- L33 α=10 H-mean 0.23 (c=57% + s=25%) — over-steer JSON 破

**为啥这个 reframe 重要**:
1. Mid-layer L17 (Stage 2/3 patching disruption locus + Stage 4 PCA cosine gap peak + Method 4.4 v2 H-mean sweet spot) **三方收敛**, 不需要"mid 是 readable 但 late 是 steerable" 的 layer specialization 故事
2. paper §5 narrative 更 clean: 3 methods 全 converge on L17, 不需要解释为啥不同 layer
3. Reviewer 看到 raw 50% vs 25% 会问 "你 reported "winner" 但 JSON 都破了, 这是 success 吗?" — HDMI H-mean 直接 preempt 这个 attack
4. 跟 Wu et al. tool calling 93% 的 reliability framework 是 cross-paper alignable (Wu et al. 在 single-token decision, selectivity 自动 1.0; 我们 multi-token JSON, selectivity 是真约束)

**实操更新**:
- `run_stage4_method44_v2_sweep.py` aggregator 加 H-mean column (commit `5daa4e8`)
- NEW `scripts/analysis/reaggregate_method44_v2_hmean.py`: idempotent re-aggregator. 加载 JSON, 重算 H-mean, 写 md. 给 v2 full sweep 完成后用 (running process 持 stale module in memory, 直接重跑覆盖 md)
- `mechanism/plan.md` §5.3 dashboard 已用 H-mean 数字

### 126.4 Lin & Liu 5-step identification protocol 采纳进 paper §5

`mechanism/plan.md` §4 新建 "Identification protocol" subsection, 严格按 Lin & Liu 2026 disclosure norm 5 步:

1. **Causal claim**: "Mid-layer L17 hidden state at last-token position is the causally active planning site for phantom routing space mode selection in Qwen3-VL-4B web agents."
2. **Identification strategy**: triangulation of probe (Method 4.2) + replacement patching (Stage 2/3 Cell A-H) + additive steering (Method 4.4 v2)
3. **Enumerate assumptions A1-A5** (table format):
   - A1: L17 last-token mediates action (vs earlier obs token)
   - A2: Mean-diff approximates causal axis (Wu et al. hypothesis)
   - A3: 24 strong-tier tasks generalize
   - A4: Qwen3-VL-4B mechanism transfers to other VLMs
   - A5: Replacement patching faithfully simulates natural read
4. **Stress-test**: Cell E random-injection control rules out non-specific disruption → A5 strongest stressed
5. **Validation ≠ identification**: Method 4.2 AUROC 1.000 = validation; Stage 2/3 + Method 4.4 v2 = identification attempts. Reviewer should not conflate.

**为啥这个 protocol 关键**: paper §5 reviewer 看到 "AUROC 1.000" 会立刻问 "你 claim 是 causal 还是 descriptive?" Lin & Liu 给我**正式 disclosure 模板**, 直接 preempt 这个问题. 我们之前的 honest-framing 已在做 #5 (separate validation), 现在升级为完整 5-step protocol.

### 126.5 Mechanism workspace folder split (commit `5daa4e8`)

Paper §5 mechanism story growth 已经 paper-within-paper:
- Wu / Ma&Rui / HDMI / Lin&Liu / Peale 5 anchors
- Method 4.2 (probe) + 4.4 v2 (steering) + 4.5 (SAE/LA-HDMI future) 3 methods
- Stage 2/3 Cell A-H (10 cells replacement patching) + Stage 4 (288 examples) data
- Identification protocol + per-task variability + cross-site replication 多 open Q

混在 `paper_planning.md` 里 mechanism content 散在 §2/§2.5/§3 多 sections (>500 lines mechanism-related), 维护不灵活. 决定 split:

```
docs/checkpoints/mechanism/
├── README.md   (49 行) — 1-page elevator + status snapshot + cross-ref 索引
└── plan.md     (209 行) — 9 sections: theory / lit / methods / identification protocol / findings / open Qs / advisor sync / roadmap / §1+§6 边界
```

**关键决定**: NOT 复制 `paper_planning.md` §2 内容. paper_planning §2 保 canonical (full Zoom 1-4 narrative), mechanism/plan.md 是 working substrate (summary + identification table + dashboard + open Qs + roadmap). 三 source 互不复制, 各司其职:

| Source | 角色 |
|---|---|
| `paper_planning.md` §2 | Strategic narrative (Zoom 1-4 full) — stable |
| `mechanism/plan.md` | Working cockpit (dashboard + protocol + Qs + roadmap) — frequent update |
| `paper_drafts/section5_*.md` | Final prose — codex round output |
| `实验笔记.md` (chronicle) | Time-order history — append-only |

**Update protocol** (写在 README): plan.md 改 trigger = theory shift / new identification assumption / advisor decision / method strategy. 200 行内单 file 维护, > 400 行 OR §5 dashboard > 100 行就 split → findings_dashboard.md.

### 127.8 `/stress` skill + auto-trigger rule [infra]

**Background**: 用户在 §127.2 + cross-family question 中两次 catch 我没 surface 的 paper-grade gap (axis-2 mechanism / cross-family coverage). Forward-progress mode 系统性漏 reviewer-defense questions. 写 `/stress` skill 是结构性 fix.

**v1**: PRA-10 checklist runner (10 items: cross-model / cross-site / per-task variance / axis decomposition / output-level / negative control / effect size / layer resolved / mechanism necessary-vs-sufficient / bibkey). 太 mechanical.

**v2 reframe** (用户 feedback): **hostile top-tier reviewer persona** (NeurIPS/ICML/ACL main/ICLR), 阅读 paper drafts + evidence + plan, **强力批评**, 找 honest gaps, 给 distance-to-top-tier framing. Not checklist runner. Specific claim-by-claim attack with line/data citation. Default hostile-but-fair; "be brutal" → reviewer-3-skeptical mode; "be gentle" → refuse (gentle 没 value).

**v3 language patch**: 用户 feedback "全英文头大" → SKILL.md `Language (中文为主双语)` section: primary 中文, 保留 English 用于 exact quote / bibkey / layer index / 统计术语 / file path / magnitude.

**Auto-trigger** (CLAUDE.md "Hostile reviewer audit (`/stress`)" section): 5 milestone signals (done / 收尾 / paper-grade / commit prose / push paper / advisor sync) 之前 mandate /stress run unless explicit "skip stress".

**Files**:
- `.claude/skills/stress/SKILL.md` (gitignored skill source)
- `.claude/CLAUDE.md` "Hostile reviewer audit" section (gitignored)
- `docs/checkpoints/process/stress_skill_replica.md` (git-tracked replica for cross-machine recovery)
- `docs/checkpoints/process/README.md` (restoration instructions)

### 127.9 `/stress` 第一次实战 — W1 + W2 attacks [finding]

第一次 invocation 用 default hostile-but-fair, 跑整 paper §1-§8 + plan §1-§7 + Exp 1/3/per-task data. 6 weak claims + 7 honest gaps. 今晚 attack 2 个最高 leverage:

**W2 (axis-2 per-task variance missing)** → **defused** (commit 4cfc12f)
→ 见 `docs/checkpoints/mechanism/results/axis2_per_task_fragility.md` + `fig_axis2_per_task_fragility.png`.
- 24 task × 2 site, P-text↔P-SoM cosine gap @ L23
- cls: mean=median=0.0131, IQR [0.012, 0.014], **100% tasks > 0.010**
- reddit: mean=median=0.0120, IQR [0.011, 0.013], **100% tasks > 0.010**
- Zero outliers, no right-skew. Axis-2 signal **broad and uniform**, NOT aggregate artifact.
- 跟 H1 dichotomy 11% strict per-task fragile **形成对比**: H1 是 binary layer-comparison 问题易 fragile; axis-2 是 continuous mode-pair distance, 即使 magnitude 小也 robust per-task.
- Paper §5.7 加 per-task uniformity paragraph (48/48 tasks > 0.010 threshold).

**W1 (single-mode hero CI crosses 0)** → **partial sustain + drop-one defuse** (commit 35784b9)
→ 见 `docs/checkpoints/mechanism/results/hero_claim_bootstrap_ci.md`.
- Bootstrap B=10000, seed=42, task-level resample N=210 reddit / 234 cls
- **Single-mode P-SoM > SoM reddit**: point +3.33pp, CI **[-0.95, +7.62] crosses 0**, P(>0)=0.914
  → /stress prediction 正确, 不能 claim "exceeds", 用 "competitive parity within 2σ"
- **Drop-one P-SoM oracle reddit**: point +3.33pp, CI **[+0.95, +6.19] strict-positive**, P(Δ>0)=0.998
  → Hero status 在 oracle level defended
- Drop-one SoM reddit: +1.90pp [+0.48, +3.81], P=0.980
- Cls sanity: P-SoM vs SoM -6.84pp [-12.39, -1.28] P=0.005 strict-negative ✓ (SoM dominates cls)
- §1 prose rewrite: **lead with drop-one CI** [+0.95, +6.19] strict-positive; head-to-head 显式标 "competitive parity within 2σ", **不 claim exceeds**. /stress W1 hedge 正式化 为 statistical disclosure.

## 128. Exp 5 axis-2 prompt-only patching lands + watcher silent-miss bug 根因+patch (2026-05-12 late) [finding][bug][infra] #finding #bug #infra

**Two land 同步 (Major experiment + Bug postmortem chronicle triggers)**:

### §128.1 Exp 5 cellhprompt cls + red 数据 land (axis-2 causal evidence)

Myriad 359511 (cls) + 359512 (red) 在 21:42 + 21:54 UTC finish, **被 watcher silent-miss bug 漏了** (见 §128.2), 手动 `auto_pull_myriad_cell.sh` 拉成功 (5 files each, ~1.5 MB / cell, sentinel_OK_PILOT).

**Setup**: source=`phantom_som` (no image, flat `[SOM_MARKS]`, SoM prompt), target=`phantom_text` (no image, flat `[SOM_MARKS]`, DOM prompt). 与既有 H-text cell 的差: source 从 `som` (with image) → `phantom_som` (no image). 因此差分 isolate **axis-2 prompt-family only** (image + text-format 都 held constant).

**主结果**: axis-2 patching (P-SoM→P-text) 在 L11-L17 显示清晰 mid-layer causal effect, 两 site cross-replicate:

| Site | Cell (axes) | overlap→tgt L17 | LD→tgt L17 |
|---|---|---:|---:|
| cls | H-text (image+prompt) | 0.75 | 9.2 |
| cls | cellhprompt (**prompt only**) | 0.79 | 8.5 |
| red | H-text (image+prompt) | 0.76 | 8.6 |
| red | cellhprompt (**prompt only**) | **0.70** | 8.8 |

**Axis-2 alone captures ~80-125% of combined image+prompt patching displacement**, reddit 甚至 *stronger* (0.70 < 0.76).

**Cosine ⫨ Causal disjoint** — **两层 disjoint** (magnitude + layer, user 2026-05-12 抓到):

| Axis | Cosine peak (layer) | Patching causal peak (layer) | Layer-aligned? |
|---|---|---|---|
| Image | 0.041 @ L17 | ~0.04-0.05 @ L11-L17 | ✓ |
| Text-format | 0.029 @ L23 | (H-d 待 reanalyze) | ? |
| **Prompt-family** | **0.011 @ L23** | **~0.20-0.30 @ L11-L17** | **✗ misaligned** |

**Magnitude disjoint**: prompt-family geometric magnitude **最小** (0.011), causal patching weight **最大** (0.20-0.30) → 4:3:1 cosine 比例 ≠ causal 比例.

**Layer disjoint** (new!): prompt-family cosine peak L23 但 patching peak L11-L17 → **signature layer ≠ decision layer**. 在 L23 patching (cosine peak) overlap→tgt 仅 0.96 / 0.89, 几乎无 displacement; 在 L11/L17 patching (causal peak) overlap→tgt 0.70-0.80, 大 displacement.

**Interpretation** (paper-grade reframe):
- L23 = "signature layer" — representation stabilized, *what prompt was given* readable
- L11-L17 = "decision routing layer" — upstream signal that downstream consumes
- Activation patching path-dependent: upstream patch propagates; downstream patch leaves upstream unchanged so subsequent re-encoding nullifies effect
- 这是 mechinterp standard finding (Wang et al. 2023 IOI: feature encoded ≠ feature used)

**Refined argument**: residual-stream cosine separation is necessary but not sufficient for causal mechanism; prompt-family is "dispatchable" — small geometric perturbation **at the decision layer** produces large output displacement.

**Paper-grade implications**:
- 3-axis mechanism stack 现在全部有 causal evidence (axis-1 H-d, axis-2 cellhprompt, axis-image residual)
- **/stress W2 / 老批评 "你只有 axis-1 mechanism"** 完全 defused
- Paper §1 hero framing "prompt tunes commit" 现有 causal patching 背书 (不只是行为相关性)

→ 见 `docs/checkpoints/mechanism/results/exp5_axis2_causal_patching.md`

### §128.2 Watcher silent-miss bug 根因 + 修复

**Symptom**: cron `myriad_watcher.py` 每 5 min run, state.json 显示 `{}` (空), Exp 5 两 cells 完全 invisible. `auto_pull_359*.log` 从未生成 → GONE_HOOK 没触发.

**Diagnostic chain**:
1. `ssh_fail_count` 不存在 → SSH chain 正常工作 (没有 timeout/error 路径触发)
2. `myriad_watcher.log` 自 May 9 起 0 字节 → watcher 持续 run 但 nothing printed
3. State.json mtime 22:05 (fresh, just now)
4. Manual SSH chain qstat (PowerShell key syntax) 显示 jobs 早 finish, .err 文件 38KB on Myriad

**根因**: `ssh_chain()` 函数 (line 156-172) 处理 inner ssh (quark→myriad) silent-fail 失败 — Cisco VPN drop / 内层 ssh hang 时, **outer ssh (DGX→quark) 仍然 returncode=0 且 stdout=空** (因 PowerShell on Windows 没 propagate inner exit code). `parse_qstat("")` → `{}`, 写入 state.json. 整个 job 生命周期 invisible.

**Patch** (`scripts/maintenance/glm/myriad_watcher.py`, syntax-validated):
1. `_qstat_with_sentinel()` wrapper: 加 `&& echo __QSTAT_OK__` 到 qstat 命令; stdout 不含 sentinel → 视为 None (chain failure), preserve old_state
2. Double-probe guard: 当 `old_state` 非空但 `new_state` 空时, 立即第 2 次 ssh_chain probe; 第 2 次仍空才接受; 第 2 次 failed → ntfy high-priority + preserve old_state

**Effect**: 未来 Cisco VPN drop 期间, watcher 会保留旧 state, GONE_HOOKS 不会因 silent-fail miss; 用户会从 ntfy 知道异常.

**Lesson**: SSH chain through Windows PowerShell intermediate **不能 trust returncode**. 任何 production-grade ssh chain script 都需要 stdout sentinel pattern. 类比 docker exec 没 -t 时 PTY 不传 exit code 的老坑.

(commit `3d61dde` push 完)

### §128.4 Codex methodology audit (v1 directive vs v2 lean) + Stage 4 catastrophic bugs (2026-05-12 late night) [finding][bug][infra] #finding #bug #infra

**Trigger**: user 提议 "让 codex 审 mechanical 方法论, 上次 audit 是 claim-level 还是 methodology-level?" → 真实是 partial coverage → 启动专用 methodology audit. 同时 user 抓到 prompt 设计 over-directive 问题 → meta-experiment v1 vs v2.

#### Prompt-design meta-experiment (v1 directive vs v2 lean)
- v1 prompt: persona + 8 bug categories + ~30 specific bullet questions → codex 把 token budget 花在逐项回答, ran out **mid-exploration**, 没生成 final structured verdict (8141 lines 多为 raw code exploration)
- v2 prompt: persona + scope + output format. STOP. → codex 自由 explore + synthesize → 完整 6-section 报告 (6484 lines), **6 paper-grade methodology bugs, 4 HIGH severity**

**Empirical confirmation**: lean prompt 是结构上 better cross-AI audit pattern. Enumeration = "checked the listed = audit complete" false-confidence + list-shaped blind spot. → memory rule `feedback_lean_audit_prompts.md` 新增 + /stress skill v4 删 15 attack lines + v5 always-chain.

#### 6 methodology bugs surfaced (v2 lean)

**Bug 1 (HIGH) — Stage 4 task selection ignores manifest tier**:
- `sorted(archive_dir.glob(...))` lexicographic, 没 `--tier` filter
- Spot-verified: cls 24 strong + 15 reverse, reddit 47 strong + 48 reverse (mixed). Lex-first-24 必然 contaminate (cls_task_4 / _10 = reverse, sort 早)
- Paper 全篇 "24 strong-tier" 实际是 mixed
- Fix: `--tier {strong,reverse,all}` arg + manifest filter, default strong

**Bug 2 (HIGH, CATASTROPHIC) — Stage 4 SOM_MARKS lossy regex**:
- `re.compile(r"^\[\d+\]\s+\w+").findall` 只 keep `[N] role`, **drop labels + drop options**
- Spot-verified on cls_task_1 step_002: obs 4188 chars, OLD output **38 chars 3 lines** vs NEW production 4169 chars 72 lines. **71/72 marks dropped**
- 所有 Stage 4 NPZ (Method 4.2 cosine, Exp 1 三轴 hierarchy, Exp 3 logit lens KL) 都在 3-line text 上算的, 而 agent + Stage 2/3 patching 用 72-line production payload → **不同 text payload 完全 different hidden states**
- Fix: `build_som_marks` 现 import `p79.experiment.som._extract_text_marks`, byte-identical with Stage 2B production

**Bug 3 (HIGH for AUROC wording) — Method 4.2 AUROC in-sample**:
- 旧: 在所有 examples 上 fit `direction = c1 - c2`, 然后**同样的 examples** 评 AUROC → 必然 inflated
- "AUROC 1.0" 不是 held-out decodability
- Fix: leave-one-task-out CV. 输出 schema 加 `auroc_in_sample` + `auroc_lototask`. Smoke test on legacy buggy NPZ: lototask = in_sample = 1.000 (separability 由 prompt + image dominated, 不是 text payload — itself a finding)

**Bug 4 (MEDIUM-HIGH) — Layer indexing off-by-one between methods**:
- Patching hooks expose 36 block outputs, `L0` = block 0 output (NOT embedding)
- Hidden-state extraction saves 37 layers, `L0` = embedding (`L1..L36` = block outputs)
- Paper "same L17 locus" cross-method 实际是 off-by-one
- Status: 文档化, **数值 unchanged**, defer code rename to follow-up

**Bug 5 (MEDIUM) — Logit lens model revision unpinned**:
- HiddenStateExtractor pins `ebb281ec...`, logit lens script 用 default
- Cache state 不同 → KL magnitudes 不可复现
- Fix: `MODEL_REVISION` 常量 + 传 `revision=` 给 `AutoTokenizer` + `Qwen3VLForConditionalGeneration`

**Bug 6 (MEDIUM) — Holm-Bonferroni baseline 用 L35 (also patched)**:
- 测 "L_n vs L35 patched", 不是 "L_n vs unpatched"
- Tests layer specificity, 不是 causal displacement
- 实证: L35 patched overlap→target ≈ 1.00 (final-block patching empirically equiv to unpatched, 因为只剩 final_norm + lm_head 处理) → 数值 ≈ 不变
- Status: 文档 note 加, **数值 unchanged**, defer

#### 损坏 scope clarification (重要 — 比初看小)

**仅 affect Stage 4 NPZ pipeline** (Method 4.2, Exp 1, Exp 3, layer_axis_emergence). **不影响**:
- Stage 2/3 activation patching (Cell A-H/D-G/H-t/H-p/H-d, Exp 5 cellhprompt cls + red, cellhprm_*_rand pending) — 用 Stage 2B 直读 archive_subset, 用 production `_extract_text_marks`
- §1 hero claims (drop-one CI / SR) — 用 episode summaries
- §4 empirical tables — 用 episode summaries (11.90→12.38 已修)

→ 因果 patching evidence 完整, 只 Stage 4 几何 evidence 待 v2 verification.

### §128.5 V2 NPZ lands — **Method 4.2 三轴 hierarchy claim 完全 invalidated** (2026-05-13 late night) [finding][bug] #finding #bug

**Trigger**: Myriad 359736 stage4mm_cls_v2 + 359737 stage4mm_red_v2 完成 (~50 min runtime), Tier 1 file-marker monitors fire → Method 4.2 v2 auto-run → numbers land.

**Headline (Bug 2 propagation to §5.7 measured)**:

| Mode pair | v1 (buggy 3-line NPZ) peak | v2 (fixed 72-line NPZ) peak | 变化 |
|---|---|---|---|
| **DOM ↔ P-text** (axis-1 anchor) | **L23 0.0254** | **L36 0.0047** | **-81% magnitude + boundary shift** |
| P-prompt ↔ P-SoM (axis-1) | L23 0.0292 | L36 0.0048 | -84% |
| P-text ↔ P-prompt | L23 0.0288 | L36 0.0081 | -72% |
| **P-text ↔ P-SoM** (axis-2 anchor) | L23 0.0114 | L36 0.0088 | -23% (was small) |
| **P-SoM ↔ SoM** (image axis anchor) | **L17 0.0412** | **L36 0.0416** | magnitude similar, peak L17→L36 |
| DOM ↔ Vision (image) | L04 0.0653 | L04 0.0670 | unchanged |
| DOM ↔ P-prompt | L36 0.0067 | L36 0.0068 | unchanged |

**三轴 hierarchy 比例 v1 vs v2**:
- v1 framed: image 0.041 > text-format 0.029 > prompt-family 0.011, 4:3:1 ratio
- v2 reality: image (0.042) ≫ axis-2 (0.009) ≈ axis-1 (0.005); image **5-10x** axis-1/axis-2; **axis-1 现 ≤ axis-2** (rank reversed)
- "axis-1 ≈ 3x axis-2" 关系**消失**

**§5.7 paper grade implication**:
1. **"Three quantitatively distinct axes at 4:3:1"** — **retracted**
2. **"text-format peaks at L23 with magnitude 0.029"** — wrong (v1 artifact, v2 is 0.005 monotone-rise to L36 boundary)
3. **"image axis localized at L17"** — wrong (v2 peaks L36 boundary; the L04 image-axis peak for AXTree-vs-Vision still holds, that's the **real** Mirage signature)
4. **AUROC 1.000 held-out lototask** — survives (Bug 3 fix: 6 modes 仍 perfectly linearly separable, just with sub-permille mean-diff for non-image axes)

**新 cleaner §5 mechanism story** (replaces "three-axis hierarchy"):
> "Three claim layers, distinct evidence types: (1) Linear readability — all 6 modes lototask AUROC 1.000 in residual stream; (2) Geometric magnitude — dominated by image axis (~0.04-0.07), with text-format and prompt-family producing sub-permille separation; (3) Causal patching effect at L11-L17 — 20-30% target displacement under final-token replacement. The disjoint between sub-permille geometric magnitude and 20-30% causal patching effect is the paper-grade-novel finding: residual-stream cosine UNDERESTIMATES causal influence by orders of magnitude. Wang et al. 2023 IOI 'feature encoded ≠ feature used' framework applied to multimodal web agents."

**Paper §5 surgery 完成 inline tonight**:
- §5.1 ¶4 (four-claim summary) — claim 2 重写 ("text-axis sibling" → "linear readability + image-axis dominance"), claim 3 加 "feature encoded ≠ used" framing
- §5.1 Evidence Status table — v2 numbers + 359719 random injection result (Gaussian destroys output, codex Bug 6 confirmed)
- §5.7 — 全段重写 with revision note acknowledging prior 4:3:1 hierarchy claim was v1 NPZ artifact. New table from v2 cls + reddit data.

**仍未做 (post-v2 follow-up)**:
- Exp 1 axis-2 layer profile (`stage4_axis2_layer_profile.py`) re-run on v2 NPZ
- Exp 3 logit lens KL re-run on v2 NPZ (qualitative direction likely survives, magnitudes change)
- §5.5 image-axis peak-layer dichotomy verify (some pairs may have flipped L04 ↔ L36)
- §5.7 per-task fragility 检 v2 axis-2 数值 distribution
- §5.6 four-defense stack — defense #4 ("cross-site Mirage geometry") 重 verify

**Cross-site replication**: cls v2 + reddit v2 patterns align — image axis cos 0.045/0.042 both sites, axis-1 0.005/0.003 both, axis-2 0.009/0.009 both. Bug 2 fix consequence identical across sites (expected, same code change).

**Lesson** (process-level):
1. **Comment "same as Stage 2B logic" 不是 sufficient verification** — 注释撒谎 (Stage 4 build_som_marks 自称 "copy of Stage 2B" 但实际不同 regex). 跨 script soreness check 应该 byte-compare actual output, 不信注释.
2. **Cross-AI audit catches what comment-trust misses** — codex v2 lean prompt 没看任何 comment, 直接 byte-diff code → spot bug 2. Claude /stress 在同代码上 (有 conversation context, 习惯 trust comments) 漏了.
3. **Lean prompt design 是 cross-AI audit value 的 prerequisite** — v1 directive prompt 让 codex 跟 list 走, 漏 bug 2; v2 lean 让 codex 自由 explore 抓到 6 个 bug
4. **"Bug 2 dropped 71/72 marks" 是 numerical 1000+ % artifact** — 之前所有 §5 几何 evidence (Method 4.2 + Exp 1 + Exp 3 + layer_axis_emergence) 都 invalidated. **Stage 2/3 + Exp 5 patching evidence (uses archive 直读)** 不 affected → **paper 仍有 causal mechanism evidence base**, 只 §5.7 framing 需重写

→ 见 `docs/checkpoints/mechanism/results/method42_v1_vs_v2_comparison.md` (canonical v1-vs-v2 数据 record) + commits 即将 land

### §129.1 Watcher GONE_HOOKS prefix dispatch bug [bug] (commit `af2299f`)

**Symptom 00:15 BST**: 359769 (cellhprm_red_tsh, task-shuffle) finish → auto_pull 触发 → 数据写入 `stage3_cellhprompt_red_fwd_ptext_myriad/` (即 359512 原 cellhprm_red 目录) → 原数据被 OVERWRITE 销毁本地. 359720 (red_rand) 在 00:15 早就受同 bug 影响, 二次 dispatch 00:19 才正确路由. cls_tsh (359768) 在跑, 完成后会同 bug 覆盖 cls 原 359511 数据.

**根因**: `_dispatch_gone_hook` 用 dict insertion-order iteration + `name.startswith(prefix)`. `cellhprm_red` 在 `cellhprm_red_tsh` 之前插入 → shorter prefix consumed longer-name event → wrong remote_dir dispatched.

**Fix**: sorted longest-prefix-first + exact-or-boundary match (`full_name[len(prefix):len(prefix)+1] == "_"`). 4 个 cellhprm variant routing 验证 全过.

**Recovery**: 直接 invoke auto_pull 用正确 REMOTE_BASENAME re-pull from Myriad (intact side). 40s 完成 2 sites × 2 datasets. Myriad-side data 没受影响.

**Production verify**: 02:05 BST cls_tsh (359768) 完成, watcher 新 fix 在线 → 自动正确路由到 `_taskshuf_myriad/`. 不需要 manual recovery.

### 130.3 /stress v5 redesign (persona shift)

User feedback: 之前 /stress 输出 6 findings 中 2/6 是 mechanical fact-check (数字对不对类), 不是 paper-grade methodology error. 用户要的是 "顶刊 reviewer 自己实施过这方法, 找 principled errors + 出 box thinking", 不是 prose polish 或 fact-check.

v5 主要 changes (`.claude/skills/stress/SKILL.md`):
1. **Persona shift**: 从 "generic reviewer 读 200+ paper" → "implementer who built activation patching / mean-diff steering / logit lens themselves, debugs own grad student's pipeline regularly"
2. **Reading order REVERSED**: scripts FIRST (mandatory step 1: `stage4_pca_cosine_gap.py` etc.), prose SECOND. Most paper-grade bugs are code↔prose mismatches, visible only by reading code.
3. **Out-of-box HARD constraint**: ≥1 of 3 attacks must be something a typical reviewer first-read MISSES. Stats-major undergrad test — if they'd catch it, downgrade.
4. **Explicit NOT-this-skill list**: NOT fact-checker / NOT prose editor / NOT checklist runner / NOT citation auditor — route to dedicated skills.
5. **Mode B pre-flight smoke test** (added 2026-05-13): `echo "READY" | codex exec` 失败则 skip 链, 不假装 cross-AI. 避免今日实证的 "codex exit 0 但 empty output 仍 fake diff" 失败模式.
6. **Mode B scope split** (added 2026-05-13): Claude 读 scripts A+B, codex 读 scripts C+D. Cross-AI 真复用而不是重叠.

`.claude/skills/codex-stress/prompt_template.md` v3: same implementer persona + write-first-explore-second 强制顺序 + ≤ 600 words 强 budget + assigned scope (不 explore).

### 130.4 Codex output cutoff diagnosis + `-o` fix (the real fix)

实证 4 次 codex fire 这次 session: 1 完整 (smoke test "READY"), 3 partial/empty (audit prompts mid-stream cutoff). 诊断:

| Factor | Interactive (用户日常) | `codex exec < file > out` (我用) | 影响 |
|---|---|---|---|
| stdin | TTY 长开 | 文件读完即 EOF | codex 可能把 EOF 当 "session 结束信号", 跳过 structured output |
| stdout buffer | line-buffered | block-buffered to file | 异常退出末 flush 丢失, `tokens used` footer 不写 |
| isatty | true | false | 部分 codex 版本走简略 codepath |

**Real fix**: `-o, --output-last-message <FILE>` 旗标. codex 把 final assistant message atomic 写指定文件, 跟 stdout truncation 完全解耦. Smoke verified 工作 (haiku test 完美返回 final message file, full log 同时有 banner + reasoning + tokens used). v5 SKILL.md Mode B procedure 已 patch 用 `-o` + fallback chain (检查 `-o` file 非空, 否则去 stdout trace 找 partial critique).

Fallback alternatives if `-o` 不够: (a) `--json` JSONL events; (b) stdin trick `(cat file && sleep 7200) | codex`; (c) `tee` 替代 `>`. 都比 prompt narrowing 更 native fix.

### 130.5 V2 retraction audit findings (Mode A + B combined)

跑了一次 /stress v5 on v2 retraction scope. Claude 抓 6 attacks (4 OOB / 2 principled), Codex 抓 1 OOB + agree 1 confounder. Diff:

**Claude only catches** (codex scope 不在 analysis script):
- ⚡ OOB1: Logit lens `lm_head(mean_h)` → KL of decoded-means 不是 per-task KL averaged. Jensen + softmax non-linearity → "amplification 8-44×" 可能 averaging artifact (`stage4_logit_lens_axis2.py:114`)
- ⚡ OOB2: bf16 dtype coercion of sub-permille mean-diff. lm_head input forced bf16 (~3 decimal precision); KL 4th decimal 是 noise (`stage4_logit_lens_axis2.py:86`)
- ⚡ Principled: V1 N=48 / V2 N=24 sample size halving confound; -81% cosine collapse 跟 regex bug fix 不可 disentangle
- ⚡ Principled: AUROC direction = (c1-c2)/||c1-c2|| 跟 cosine 测同一方向, AUROC 1.0 + cos 0.005 ≠ "modes well-separated" 而是 "within-mode variance ⊥ inter-mode direction" (`stage4_pca_cosine_gap.py:88`)
- ⚡ Gap: Tuned lens (Belrose 2023) 未 cite, vanilla logit lens on mid-layer 有 known artifact prior work
- ⚡ Gap: Bootstrap CI on cosine peak layer 缺失

**Codex only catches** (scope: extraction + extractor):
- ⚡ OOB: `run_stage4_multimode_extract.py:227-228` silently tolerates per-mode failures (logger.error 但不 raise, 只在 ALL tasks fail 才 raise). Current v2 NPZ luck-balanced, 但 protocol 允许 silent imbalance.
- ⚡ Agreement: V1→V2 实际 ≥3 changes (regex + tier filter + revision), 不只 Bug 2. Plan.md attribute 单 cause 不可识别 — 合并 Claude N halving = **4 simultaneous confounders**.

**Defuse plan**: 单 highest-leverage = re-run logit lens per-task KL (2h GPU, written + fired `scripts/analysis/stage4_logit_lens_per_task.py`). 写完输出 ratio per-task / of-means; ratio ≈ 1 terminology-fix, ratio >> 1 mechanism 反而被低估, ratio << 1 hero claim 死. 等 land 决定 §1.2 重写策略.

## 132. Pre-launch codex stress on 16-cell rerun design → 6 paper-grade design fixes + scope reframe 24cond/4cell (2026-05-13 afternoon-evening) [design][finding][infra] #design #finding #infra

### 132.1 Trigger: pre-run codex hostile audit

User explicitly asked "让 codex stress 一下 baseline 设计，在我 pre run 前的", spot-check decision-point invocation. Skip Claude /stress (user directive: "claude你不用 stress"), go straight Mode B cold-read codex audit on 16-cell paper-grade rerun design (pre-launch window, post-codex-pipeline-audit / post-codex-viz-audit / post-codex-retraction-audit — design layer only, complementary not duplicative).

Prompt (`docs/checkpoints/codex_prompts/codex_stress_16cell_design_2026-05-13.md`): lean (no enumeration, no attack-vector list), scope = design layer (preregistration / paper-prose / advisor decisions / launch orchestrator / framing rule R1-R5), explicit skip of code/data pipeline already covered by prior audits. 60min budget.

Codex finished in ~5 min, 315K tokens used (2.4MB output). Output: `docs/checkpoints/codex_outputs/codex_stress_16cell_design_2026-05-13.md`.

### 132.2 Verdict: 6 paper-grade HIGH severity design flaws (must fix before launch)

| # | Flaw | Severity | Defuse |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Queue only launches phantom, no DOM/SoM/Vision baseline siblings → drop-one CI compares fresh phantom vs archived pre-fix baselines or nothing | HIGH | scope reframe (24 conditions including baseline) |
| 2 | K_h1 / K_h3 internally contradictory (gate per H1/H3/R5 vs transparency per §4 audit B9) | HIGH | repropagate K-of-N transparency-only to H1/H3/R5/§6 |
| 3 | `preregistration_decision_test.py:29-32` tests "P-SoM ≥ best DOM/SoM/Vision" not "drop-one > 0" — direct prereg ↔ script mismatch | HIGH | rewrite decision script to drop-one |
| 4 | Paper §3 excludes P-prompt as "intentionally excluded" but prereg H3 axis-2 REQUIRES P-prompt | HIGH | rewrite §3.4 to include P-prompt as 4th cell; commit to 6-mode |
| 5 | `advisor_sync_5_5_followup.md` says "3 sites × 3 phantom" (= 18) but calls it 16, OSF manifest repeats 16/18 ambiguity | HIGH | scope canonical to 24 conditions / 4 cells |
| 6 | Smoke-gate B7 "first 10 ep must show ≥1 success" → outcome-dependent stopping biases low-SR cells upward | HIGH | revise to outcome-independent (auth + artifact + evaluator parseability only) |

Probable concerns: TOST δ unit ambiguity (SR pp vs cost %); Router H7/H8 paper-1-vs-paper-2 unresolved; Heterogeneity I²>75% "do not pool" rule conflicts with H1(i) pooled meta gate.

### 132.3 Strategic decision: scope reframe 16-cell phantom-only → Phase 1a 24-condition / 4-cell + Phase 1b shop deferred

User-decided scope (workshop-first publication strategy, advisor 5/5 R3 framing additive expansion not reopen):

- **Phase 1a (workshop-targeted)**: 24 operational conditions = 2 sites (cls, red) × 2 models (B0, B1) × 6 modes (DOM, SoM, Vision, P-text, P-prompt, P-SoM). Statistical: 4 (site, model) cells, pooled DerSimonian-Laird meta + TOST primary gate, K-of-N transparency consistency.
- **Phase 1b (main paper deferred)**: +12 conditions = shop × B0+B1 × 6 modes. Feeds R3 → R1 / Option D framing decision post-workshop submission.

Rationale:
- All current data is pre-fix; full fresh rerun required regardless of scope choice
- Workshop = "stake territory" with narrow R3 hook (cls+red 4th routing arm); main paper = "full evidence stack" with shop replication test
- Cross-site asymmetry claim is main-paper territory not workshop (N=2 sites is inherently 1-bit observation; need N≥3 for site-class adaptive structural claim)
- Phase A bug worry is wash (all sites have bug surface pre-fix; shop marginal ops risk = Magento FPC + B1 low SR, not different-class bug)
- Phased commit theory: information cost low (data is going to run anyway), commitment cost high (paper §1 prose hard to change post-write) → commit late, collect early

### 132.4 K-of-N transparency-only reclassification

Pre-data reclassification 2026-05-13, locked in prereg §4 audit B9 row + propagated to H1/H3/R5/§6/§3 family declaration. Rationale:

- Power analysis (`docs/analysis/cross_sites/power_analysis.md`, pre-data) shows K-of-N family power at observed 1-3pp effect sizes < 10%; calibrated only for ≥7pp effects
- N=4 cells × per-cell power ≈ 0.30 at 1.5pp effect → P(≥3 of 4 sig) ≈ 8%, K-as-gate dysfunctional
- N=4 also too small for K-of-N to have statistical leverage (only 5 distinct outcome states: 0/4 - 4/4)
- Primary gate = pooled DerSimonian-Laird random-effects meta on N=4 cells + TOST equivalence at δ=1.0pp
- K-of-N retained as transparency consistency check in §4 per-cell table

Reclassification timestamp matters: must be pre-data-unblinding (this commit predates Phase 1a launch) so reviewer cannot accuse "weakened gate after seeing data".

### 132.5 Terminology disambiguation: "condition" vs "cell"

Codex Flaw 5 root cause = 4 different definitions of "cell" across prereg / decision script / advisor sync / OSF manifest. Locked terminology:

- **condition** = 1 (site, model, mode) operational unit (Phase 1a N=24)
- **cell** = 1 (site, model) statistical stratification unit (Phase 1a N=4)
- Drop-one is per-cell using all 6 modes within that (site, model); pooled meta inputs are 4 cells; K-of-N family is 4 cells per H sub-family
- Memory locked: `feedback_condition_vs_cell_terminology.md` + `feedback_kofn_transparency_only.md`

### 132.6 Smoke-gate B7 revised (outcome-independent)

Prior text: "first 10 episodes must show ≥1 success or ≥1 N/A" → outcome-dependent stopping. For true 5-10% SR cell (B1 shop expected, B1 cls capability-modulated reversal possible), P(0 successes in first 10) ≈ 35-60%, would invalidly restart → systematic upward bias on low-SR cells = reviewer 1-shot kill ammunition.

Revised: smoke gate = auth `logged_in=True` on all 10 AND ≥9/10 produced complete artifact bundle AND evaluator returned parseable verdict (success/failure/ua_match all OK; **success rate itself NOT checked**). Failures halt for auth refresh / artifact pipeline debug, not for low SR.

## 132. Myriad 366792 v2 split sweep land — Method 4.4 A5 counter-claim succeeds, §5.3 paper-altering downgrade (2026-05-13 evening) [finding][design] #finding #design

### 132.1 Job lifecycle

| | 363196 | 366792 |
|---|---|---|
| qsub | 13:35:53 (h_rt=4h) | 16:42:14 (h_rt=2h override) |
| qw wait | ~3h, qdel'd | 2h12min |
| run | n/a (qdel'd) | 18:59 → 20:27 = 1h26min on V100-PCIE 32GB |
| outcome | swap pivot | DONE marker land 20:27:49 |

User pivot at 16:42: qdel 363196 + re-qsub with `-l h_rt=2:0:0` override. h_rt 4h→2h trade aimed at faster short-queue. Net wait actually similar (~2h either way) but new fresh-fairshare prior 0→3.06.

### 132.2 Hero numbers — A5 counter-claim succeeds

| | Held-out (8 tasks, paper-grade headline) | In-sample (16 tasks, reviewer comparison) |
|---|---:|---:|
| Best cell | L33 α=20 | L11 α=20 (different cell!) |
| H-mean | **0.12** | 0.29 |
| Completeness | 7% (1/15 paired cells) | 17% (5/30) |
| Selectivity | 100% | 100% |
| Non-zero cells (out of 30) | 1 | 9 |

**Generalization gap = +0.16**. Reviewer-3 transferability threshold 0.10 exceeded. Best peak migrates L11 (in-sample) → L33 (held-out) — not just magnitude decay, *peak layer disagreement*.

**A5 counter-claim (§5.8) succeeds**: "0.33 H-mean ceiling is in-sample artifact" — verified. Method 4.4 mean-difference direction fits the training cohort and **does not transfer** at paper-grade margin. Original "evidence ceiling not validation" hedge was insufficient.

### 132.3 §5.3 prose reframe — probe-causal-steering trichotomy

Original framing: probe-causal *dissociation* (cosine ≤ 0.01 readable + patching 0.20-0.35 causal + steering 0.33 ceiling). Revised: probe-causal-steering *trichotomy*:

1. **Probe-level (Method 4.2)**: mode AUROC 1.000 at all layers ✓
2. **Causal use (§5.4 patching, per-task paired)**: 0.20-0.35 displacement at L11-L17 ✓ transfers
3. **Single-direction steering (Method 4.4 mean-diff)**: 0.12 held-out ✗ does NOT transfer

Why §5.4 patching transfers but §5.3 steering does not: **patching uses per-task paired source-target activations** (per-task counterfactual). **Steering uses population mean-difference direction** (mode-mean, NOT per-task). The mid-layer mechanism is real, but is encoded per-task — no single fixed direction captures it. SAE feature steering / LA-HDMI adaptive direction needed for transferable steering.

§5 mechanism story now actually CLEARER: patching tests *presence*, steering tests *transferability of population direction*. Patching positive + steering held-out negative is a coherent + paper-grade reading (Wang 2023 IOI feature *used* vs feature *encoded*).

  - §5.3 prose downgrade — held-out paper-grade headline, in-sample reviewer-comparison-only
  - §5.8 A5 counter-claim outcome — succeeds (rejection)
  - §5.9 discussion limit — "held-out collapse" replacing "0.33 ceiling"
