4. cron itself not running (DGX-side health)

Not blocking — manual SCP worked. Need to debug cron Mode B chain post-paper-update.

### 132.6 Paper §5 story — actually stronger post-A5

Pre-Commit-D framing: "Method 4.4 0.33 ceiling indicates probe-causal dissociation". Implicit claim: there IS a transferable direction but steering hits ceiling due to layer-α tradeoff.

Post-Commit-D framing: "Method 4.4 0.12 held-out shows direction does NOT transfer; mid-layer mechanism is real but task-specific." Stronger claim about mechanism (patching robust under held-out paired evaluation) + honest claim about steering (cohort-fit, not transferable). Cleaner narrative: pretraining-shortcut mechanism is per-task encoded, not a single population direction. SAE / LA-HDMI per-input adaptive steering becomes well-motivated future work, not "future work that might improve a ceiling".

→ 见 `results/mechanistic/stage4_multimode_b1_cls/method44_v2_sweep.json` (763KB, 30 train cells + 15 eval cells) + `docs/checkpoints/stage4_method44_v2_results.md` (full sweep tables) + commits 8ab1455 (Commit D pipeline) + (this commit) prose updates.

### 133.2 v6 changes (`.claude/skills/stress/SKILL.md` 243 → 390 lines)

1. **Scope calibration table** — spot-check (2-3 files, ≤600w) / milestone (5-7, ≤1200w) / **pre-fire (8-12, ≤2400w)** / submission (10-15, uncapped). State scope at top of every output.
2. **Bilingual exemplar + FAIL CHECK** — GOOD vs BAD output examples; post-output scan, 整段英文 attack prose → redo.
3. **Sibling-script propagation check (NEW)** — after Bug N spotted in 1 script, grep all siblings using same primitive BEFORE declaring scope complete. Hard rule.
4. **Mode A → Mode B context handoff** — Claude writes `audit_scope.md` (read list + top findings + cross-validate targets); codex prompt references it. Structural complementary coverage.
5. **Persona rotation menu** — 4 personas (mechinterp implementer / ML systems engineer / stats methodologist / reproducibility auditor); pick by Claude's persona blind spot.
6. **Fix-verification mandate** — codex inline fix → py_compile + git diff --check; data-altering NOT auto-applied.
7. **Retrospective hook** — within 7 days, append §N.retro to chronicle: did finding F<N> surface real bug? Spec drift signal.

## 135. Decision "3A" — estimand-first prereg rewrite + pre_run/ folder de-redundancy (2026-05-14 下午) [design][infra] #design #infra

### 135.1 Trigger: followup.md 阈值重思 + 2 轮 codex cross-think

User 让重思 `followup.md` Part 2 的 3 个阈值 (K_h1=0.75 / K_h3=0.67 / TOST δ=1.0pp)，基于 bug-fix-pre archive 数据，并让 codex 交叉思考。两轮 codex cross-think:
- `threshold_rethink_FINAL_2026-05-14.md` — 阈值校准 (codex 跟 Claude 数值几乎完全一致；codex 增量价值在 LOO sensitivity: P-text LOO-fragile, δ=1.0pp superiority LOO p≈0.044-0.046 压线)
- `prereg_structure_review_FINAL_2026-05-14.md` — prereg 整体结构 (codex + Claude 独立逐条 overlap: H1 over-specified / stale 16-cell / DL lock 过早 / K 坍缩 / H7-H8 scope clutter; codex 增量: estimand 问题 + H2(a) 内部矛盾 + §5.X 移出)

核心认知 (双方收敛): `followup.md` Part 2 是**双重 stale** — scope 还是 16-cell, role 还把 K-of-N 当 gate。但更深的问题是 prereg **过度把 inferential statistics 套在不需要它的量上**。

### 135.2 Decision "3A" (student-decided after cross-think)

User 决定 **3A** = Decision 2 → Option 3 + Decision 1 → Option A:

**3 = H2(a) by-construction**: P-SoM cost ≈ DOM 是 by-construction token-accounting 属性 (regex-filtered AXTree subset, 无 image token), 用 falsification check 验证 (median cost ratio > 1.20× 任一 condition → falsified), **不是 sampling-theory gate**. 对近确定性的 token-count 量跑 TOST 是 category error.

**A = fixed-effects estimand**: 4 个 (site,model) cell 是**研究 design 不是 population sample** → estimand = "FE inverse-variance 加权平均 over 这 4 个 planned cells"，**没有 τ²** → 没 DerSimonian-Laird, 没 REML, 没 Hartung-Knapp. **这溶解了 codex /stress v6 F1/F2** (DL τ² biased at k=4 + Wald CI anti-conservative) — 因为 k=4 fragility 本质是 τ²-estimation artifact, τ² 不在 estimand 里就没有 fragility.

**统一根因**: 两个决定都往 "少推断、多 by-design/by-construction" 走 — 当一个量是 design-fixed 或近确定性的, 不要假装它是 noisy-statistical-hypothesis. 这跟项目自己的文档哲学一致 (CLAUDE.md "不硬编码会变的东西").

### 135.3 Prereg rewrite (preregistration.md, decision "3A" propagation)

- **H1 简化成单一检验**: one-sided FE superiority test (H0: θ_FE ≤ +1.0pp at α=0.05). 之前 H1(i) pooled-meta-≠0 + magnitude≥1pp 两个 sub-condition 被 superiority test 逻辑蕴含 → 折叠进单一 test. Estimand 段显式 locked.
- **H2(a) reframe**: from "PRIMARY gate ≥3/4 cells ±10%" → by-construction property + falsification check (>1.20× cost ratio threshold). 退出 PRIMARY family m-count.
- **H3(i)/(ii)**: DL random-effects → FE inverse-variance pooling (跟 H1 一致). Archive caveat 注明 P-text I²=71% + LOO-fragile.
- **R1-R5 framing rule**: "H2(a) cost holds" → "H2(a) by-construction property not falsified"; R4 = H2(a) falsified; R5 = 单一 superiority test fail. Heterogeneity-conditional rule reframe: I²>75% 不 block FE pooling (FE 永远 well-defined), 而是把 hook cap 在 R3.
- **§3 family declaration**: PRIMARY family m=1 (just H1 single test); H2(a) 退出 m-count; TRANSPARENCY 改成 "report n-of-4 count, NO threshold".
- **§4 locked choices**: B8 row → FE estimator (DL/REML 退出, τ²/I² descriptive only + RE 作 appendix sensitivity); K_h1/K_h3 两行并成单 "K-of-N transparency count, no threshold" 行; B9 K-of-N row → 退休百分比阈值; δ row 注明 archive LOO-borderline (δ=1.0pp 是地板).
- **Scope-strip**: H7/H8 router + §5.X mechanism layer-selection disclosure 加 ⚠️ DEFERRED banner (logical Appendix B/C, not part of this DOI claim).
- **Stale sweep**: 16-cell→24-condition, H1-H6→H1-H3 gating, 8→9 lock decisions, OSF 16-cell→24-condition, reading-order 更新.
- **新增 §2.4 + §2.5**: in-doc 4-cell power acknowledgment + crisp H1 PASS/FAIL decision flow (8-step, 含 I²>75% heterogeneity cap).
- **§6(b)**: OSF workflow 改 reference `osf_lock_manifest.md §3` 不重列.
- **§6 9-decision list**: (1)(2)(3) 重写反映 3A (estimand=FE / K-of-N transparency-count / δ=1.0pp FE superiority); (8) Router → DEFERRED.
- **Appendix A**: 加 2026-05-14 decision "3A" 完整条目 + pending advisor sync witness 行.

### 135.4 pre_run/ 文件夹 de-redundancy

User 问 `docs/checkpoints/pre_run/` 是否有冗余. 评估 12 个文件:
- **真冗余 1**: `pre_rerun_audit.md` (678 行) — stale aggregator, 把 ~6 个 doc 内容 copy 一遍, 标题还 "16-Cell", 引用已删除的 `PAPER_STRATEGY_OPEN_QUESTIONS.md`, 早于 queue Gate 1-7 + /stress v6. **→ slim 成 ~55 行 pointer index** (本 session 完成).
- **真冗余 2**: model/dataset identity 事实在 `locked_versions.md` + `model_card.md` + `dataset_card.md` triplicated, 已 drift (B0 name 矛盾). **→ deferred** (locked_versions 设 SoT 需先确认 B0 model name — config `api_name: qwen.qwen3-vl-235b-a22b` vs cards `qwen3-omni-235b-thinking`, 事实问题待 user 确认).
- **轻微 overlap**: `osf_lock_manifest.md §3` ↔ `preregistration.md §6(b)` — 已修 (§6(b) 改 reference).
- **不冗余**: evaluator_change_protocol + reeval_audit_protocol 是设计成一对的 (Protocol A/B); negative_results / release_redaction / ethics 各单一用途.

冗余根因 = 文档分层错位: spec 类 (preregistration / locked_versions / osf_lock_manifest) 跟 derived 类 (pre_rerun_audit / topvenue_constraints / model_card) 混在一起, 派生视图 copy 了 spec 内容 → spec 一改就 stale. 正确做法 = derived 视图 reference 而非 copy.

## 136. /stress + /codex-stress mechanistic 轴隔离审查 → SoM `[OPTIONS]` confound 根因 + canonical builder 修复 (2026-05-14 下午) [bug][finding][infra] #bug #finding #infra

codex 读 substrate (`som.py` / `activation_patching.py` / `axis2_layer_profile.py`):
- **C1 [P0]** 连"production-aligned"的 `build_som_marks` 都不是 production-identical — 生产 SoM 还有 `_options_map` 第二遍恢复 `[OPTIONS]`/`[DROPDOWN OPTIONS]` 行，机制版省掉了。

### 136.4 真正的问题 (user 引导厘清) — axis-1-collinear confound

User pushback: dropdown 处理是文档化 bug fix (B-06 / §51 `_inject_select_options`)，做 fix 时 phantom modes 还不存在。厘清后真正的问题：

- `_extract_text_marks` 只 keep `[数字]` 行 → `[OPTIONS]` (字母) 被 drop。生产 builder 有第二遍 `_options_map` 找回；机制 `build_som_marks` 没有。
- 实测 archive: cls **47/71** + reddit **144/177** observation 含 dropdown 标记。
- `text_payload_for` 让 options-presence 和 text-format **共线**: AXTree modes (dom/phantom_prompt) 保留 options，marks modes (som/phantom_som/phantom_text) 丢失 → **axis-1 (text-format) pairs 被污染** (混了"一边有 options 文本一边没有"的人为缺失)；axis-2 + image-axis 干净 (options 对称)。
- 最可疑的受害者: reddit axis-1 logit-lens "3.95× surprise" (DOM↔P-text，reddit 81% 有 dropdown)。
- **定性: phantom-design 疏忽，不是 B-06 回归** — phantom modes 后于 B-06 fix 设计。

### 136.5 干净修复 (本 session 完成)

- `p79/experiment/som.py` 新增 canonical `build_som_text_from_obs_text(obs_text, max_marks)` —— `_extract_text_marks` + `_options_map` 恢复 + `[SOM_MARKS]` wrapper，single source of truth。
- `_build_som_result` (生产路径) refactor 成调用它 (byte-identical lift)。
- 9 个机制脚本的本地 `build_som_marks` / `_build_som_marks_text` / `fmt_som_standard` 全部 delegate 到 canonical。
- 验证: `py_compile` × 10 文件全 clean；`git diff --check` 无 whitespace；功能 smoke 确认 `[DROPDOWN OPTIONS]` 恢复 (旧版 47 行无 options → 新版 48 行含)。
- master bug catalog 新增 **B-82** (§136 section)。

**Re-extraction required (未做，follow-up compute)**: 所有 v2 NPZ (cls + reddit) + Method 4.4 sweep + format-variation 输出都是 options-less builder 产的 → axis-1 机制 claim paper-grade 前必须重抽。

### 136.6 对 advisor sync 的结论

- Method 4.2 + logit lens **轴隔离逻辑**干净，但 substrate (SoM text) 有 B-82 confound，axis-1 findings 需重抽后复核

### 137.4 Mechanism 转 SAE — Qwen-Scope 不覆盖 Qwen3-VL (调研实测)
学长: mechanism 部分 **SAE (sparse autoencoder) 是重点**，但 **不一定有 Qwen3-VL 的 pretrained SAE**。
**调研结果 (2026-05-14, web search)**: Qwen 官方 2026-05-01 开源 **Qwen-Scope** SAE 套件 (arXiv:2605.11887)，但 **只覆盖 text-only Qwen3/3.5 语言模型** (Qwen3-1.7B/8B/30B-A3B + Qwen3.5-2B/9B/27B/35B-A3B，14 组 SAE，训练数据是 text-only residual-stream activations)，**无任何 VL 变体，连项目用的 4B/235B 尺寸都没有**。
→ **结论**: off-the-shelf Qwen3-VL SAE **确实不存在** (印证学长担心)。选项: (a) 自训 VLM SAE — 有 tooling (LMMs-Lab "SAE Made Easy" + VLA-SAE arXiv:2603.19183 先例; Qwen-Scope 可作 methodology 模板); (b) 退守现有 activation patching + layer probe + logit lens (§133/§136); (c) phantom space 的 axis-1/axis-2 都是 text-only 输入 → text-domain SAE 思路对这两轴可能仍 applicable，只 image-axis 需 vision pathway。

### 139.1 Trigger

### 139.2 Claude /stress — 6 findings (读 13 文件)

读 som.py / 两个 agent / 两个 backend wrapper / config.py / base+4 configs / conditions / modules / runner 关键段 / 3 queue 脚本：
- **F1 [OOB]** axis-1 输入截断/cap 不对称：dom/phantom_prompt 被 agent 端 `max_obs_chars` 截断；som/phantom_* 的 `[SOM_MARKS]` 从未截断 AXTree 抽（`max_marks=200`）→ 两路看到页面不同子集，污染主轴。
- **F2 [OOB]** `model.revision` dead-config：runner 只传 backend 子配置，`local_qwen.py` 不含 revision key → codex C8 的 provenance 修复从未接上线（B-82 双胞胎）。
- **F3 [OOB]** GLM fallback 是 B0 独有 parse-error 救生网，不在已披露的"解码/max_tokens"非对称清单里，影响 SR 非 cost。
- **F4** B1 `max_new_tokens=384` 卡在 agent 自己文档的 parse-safe floor (~400) 以下。
- **F5** reference image 仍注入 phantom no-image 模式。F6 B0/B1 token 计数方法不同（API usage vs 本地 tokenizer）。
- Strong（扛得住）：6-mode `max_steps:30` 全一致；phantom_prompt 的 obs label 随 text-format 轴共变（2×2 干净）；B0/B1 mode dispatch 字典结构对称。

### 139.3 codex Mode B — 6 findings，把 axis-1 图景改写

codex 读 `vwa_wrapper.py` + `analysis.py`：
- **C1 [OOB]** "raw AXTree" 不 raw — `_to_p79_obs` 在 mode dispatch 前调 `_inject_select_options`/`_inject_css_dropdown_options` 注入 `[OPTIONS]` 行（B-82 那些行的源头）。
- **C2** viewport 过滤 `current_viewport_only=True` 硬编码、不进 `condition_meta.json`，且**纠正了 Claude F1**：marks 来自 viewport-only（非整页）AXTree。

- **C6** `cost_efficiency_ratio` 仍 raw-success 基准。

### 139.4 整合 9-bug 清单 + 诚实校准

互审整合 → master_bug_catalog §139（B-83 FIXED + B-84~B-90 open 表）。两处**诚实校准**：
1. **viewport 硬编码不是 between-mode bug** —— 所有 mode 同源于一份 viewport-filtered AXTree，我审计中途把它 bundle 进 P0 是夸大；降为 P2 transparency。
2. **Bug 1 严重度 gated on 实测** —— 我把它定 P0 靠的是推断（`dom_size_threshold:12000`），不是测量。实测（task #64，57435 个 `observation_dom.txt`）：>12000 字符截断仅 **0.207%** step 触发，>200-mark cap 仅 **0.028%** 触发。viewport filter 才是真正输入边界（median 3306 / p99 7656）。→ Bug 1 从 P0 降级；user 拍板**直接删两个冗余 cap**（axis-1 by-construction 干净，无需 caveat），不做"截断前移"重构。

中途证伪：一度怀疑"B0=12000 / B1=8000 跨模型 truncation 不对称" —— 实际 wrapper 默认值两边都 12000，agent 里的 8000 是 dead default。

archived phantom_text run 与 Phase 1a configs 都用 `observation_mode: phantom_dom`（legacy alias）。但 `run_registry.canonical_mode()` 把 `phantom_dom` + `phantom_text` 都归一为 `P-text`，`_entry_to_cell` 加载时即 canonicalize → `cell.mode` 永远 canonical，raw alias 不泄漏。主 Phase 1a 行为聚合器 `aggregate_sr_fp_per_mode.py`（用 `PAPER_MODES` + `get_cells()`）全程在 canonical 空间运作 → **安全**。Minor 观察（非 fire blocker）：少数机制分析脚本（`stage4_pca_cosine_gap.py` / `stage4_robustness.py` 等）硬编码 `phantom_text` 作 mode key，但它们读 NPZ（机制管线），是否受影响取决于 NPZ extraction label，与 Phase 1a 行为 fire 无关。

### 139.8 FP-architecture 重构 — post-hoc adjustment → 上游根因修复（user-directed 2026-05-14）

User 在 B-85 拍板环节把问题升维:"eval_fp 必要吗？边界过于难确定，WA + 6 站点每个 edge case 单独算" → "能不能 bug fix 而非 post 修"。Claude code-read VWA evaluator + codex 两轮 cross-research(`b85_fp_filter_FINAL` + `vwa_eval_fp_search_FINAL`)→ 确立**整个 `compute_adjusted_success` 后处理层可退役**,换成上游根因修复。

**根因(精确)**:`StringEvaluator` 拿 `last_action["answer"]`(`evaluators.py:212-213`);agent 没真 finish → VWA `run.py:425-427` + P79 runner `runner/main.py:1426-1432` 都补 fake stop `answer=""` → 空 `pred` 进 LLM judge(`llm_fuzzy_match` / `llm_ua_match`)→ GPT-4o-mini 把空答案判 correct/same → FP。确定性 string 法对空 pred 正确返回 0,**只有两个 LLM judge 路径 FP**。na_fp + string_match eval_fp **同一根因**。

**文献佐证(codex)**:WebArena-Verified 把 N/A 评测归为 "evaluation mechanism issue";PAE 报 WebArena ~50% "成功"是 evaluator FP;WONDERBREAD 直接过滤 impossible task。VWA upstream 无修复。→ 反而利好 paper:修一个文献公认的评测器可靠性问题,可引用。

**4 块重构**:
1. **B-91 na_fp + string_match eval_fp 🛠️ FIXED**(本 session):`helper_functions.py` 两个 LLM judge 顶部加 `if not pred or not pred.strip(): return 0.0` 确定性 guard。VWA submodule commit `f0c835b` (branch `p79-patches`)。py_compile + guard 逻辑验证。比旧 post-hoc filter 更正确(多抓"finish 了但 answer 空")。
2. **program_html eval_fp 分支 → 删 🛠️ DONE(2026-05-14,task #75)**:`has_effective_action` 启发式无可扩展边界;污染由 `RESET_BEFORE` 上游协议防。user 指示"彻底"修 → `has_effective_action` **整个移除**,跨 8 文件:`analysis.py`(分支+param+batch caller)/ `runner/main.py`(`_has_eff` 计算+写入)/ `types.py`(dataclass 字段)/ `schema_migrations/v2.py`(catalog 条目)/ `analyze_cross_representation.py` + `analyze_reason_diagnostics.py`(sibling 重实现)/ `rederive_episode_summary.py`(`_rederive_one` 5-tuple→4-tuple)。py_compile ×7 clean;smoke 确认 program_html+~finished 不再降级、string_match+na_fp 仍正常、kwarg 移除报 TypeError。已验证无 strict from-dict 反序列化 → 删 schema 字段安全。B-85 由此 SUPERSEDED(研究:13 个会被降级的 episode 里仅 4 个真 click-only,8 个是 narrow filter 意外抓对的 stale comment FP)。
3. **N/A 任务 → 移出 primary SR 🛠️ DONE(2026-05-14,task #76)**:task-load 时排除(`task.exclude_na_tasks: true` 默认,`tasks.py::load_tasks` + `_is_na_task`,`config.py` 加默认)。全 6 站点统计:73 个 N/A 任务全是 `string_match`+`fuzzy_match:"N/A"`(cls 10 / red 5 / shop 31 / wa-shop 19 / wa-admin 6 / wa-red 2 = 1390 的 5.3%)—— 一条统一 config 规则,无 per-site edge case。prereg §4 加"N/A task exclusion"行 + Appendix A 2026-05-14 条目。引 WebArena-Verified / WONDERBREAD。⚠️ 下游 `EXPECTED_N` sweep 待做(`run_registry` / `active_processes` / `glm_cell_autoupdate` 的 cron DONE-gate / `power_analysis` 等硬编码 234/210/466)→ 并入 piece 4。

### 140.3 与并发 session 的 B-83/B-84 对齐（commit `d14b530`）— 并发 working-tree 危险实证
Gemma Tier 1 代码是照 **pre-B-83/B-84** 的 Qwen agent 抄的；并发 session 的 commit `a4e30fc` 同时 land 了 B-83/B-84（§139）。读 §139 才发现 Gemma 等于带了两个刚被修掉的 paper-grade bug：
- **B-84 对齐**：Qwen agent 删了 `max_obs_chars` 截断（axis-1 page-coverage 不对称）。Gemma agent 还带着 → 删掉 + `local_gemma.py` 删 dead `max_obs_chars` key。
- **B-83 对齐**：runner 的 B-83 逻辑 `if _model_revision and backend_cfg.get("revision") is None: backend_cfg["revision"] = _model_revision` —— 会把顶层（Qwen-specific）`model.revision` 转发给任何 `revision is None` 的 backend。`local_gemma` 原配 `revision: null` → 会被误转发 Qwen SHA `ebb281ec` → Gemma 加载崩。修复：`revision: null → "main"`（非 null placeholder，runner 不再覆写）；gemma agent 加 revision-unset warning（never silent）。模拟 runner B-83 逻辑验证：`local_gemma` 经转发后仍 `"main"`，None-revision backend 才会拿到 Qwen SHA。
- **经验**：多 session 共享一个 working tree 时，"照现有代码抄"有时间窗风险 —— 被抄的代码可能正在被并发修。对策：写完后必读最新 chronicle（§139）交叉核对、grep 相关 commit diff。

### §139.retro (due 2026-05-21): /stress audit retrospective
- B-83 [P0]: 真 bug? — **YES**，codex C8 修复接线断裂，provenance claim 名存实亡。compute saved = 避免 OSF lock 一个假的 SHA 证明。
- B-84 [P1]: 实证后 P0→P1，是否 premature? — 截断确实只 0.2% 触发，但删 cap 成本极低且让 axis-1 by-construction 干净，**不算 premature optimization**（是 cheap correctness win）。**关键教训：severity 不能靠推断，要实测**——我一度把 B-84 定 P0 靠 `dom_size_threshold` 推断。
- C1/C4 [codex]: vwa_wrapper + analysis.py 侧 —— Claude scope 结构性够不到，Mode B scope split 价值兑现（codex 抓的 3 项 Claude 读不到的文件）。
- Spec drift 建议 v7：severity 标注必须区分"实测"vs"推断"，推断的 P0 要标 `[P0?实测待定]` 并立刻建 gate task。

### 138.8 Compute 决定 — Phase 1 实验跑 A100 独立 docker + Gemma3-VL pin (2026-05-14)
**重要决定**: Phase 1 paper-grade 实验 (B0 / B1 / Gemma3-VL clean run) 跑在 **UCL Condenser A100 VM 的独立 dockerized VWA stack** 上 —— 不再像之前那样用 DGX 经 Tailscale 访问 quark 的 docker。
- **解决了** memory `reference_compute_resources.md` Tier 0 标的 VWA-reach blocker (A100 不能直连 quark docker) —— 选 "self-host VWA on VM" 而非 "Tailscale-to-quark"。A100 成 site-dependent paper-grade 跑的自足平台 (单租户 / 无队列 / 40GB / 自带 VWA docker)。
- **改变**: DGX 作为 "VWA bridge" 的角色被取代; B0 XOR B1 同站规则仍成立 (A100 VM 上单 docker stack / 单 account), 但跨机 (DGX+quark) contention 消失。
- **Gemma3-VL pin = `google/gemma-3-4b-it`**: 4B 量级对齐 B1 → matched-capability cross-family control (直接答 reviewer "是否 Qwen-specific"); bf16 unquant 装 A100 40GB (memory 确认 Qwen-4B 在 40GB 有 ~25GB headroom), 对齐 B1 的 `quantization: none` 无量化 confound。
- **遗留 infra prereq (Phase 1 真正起点, 实测 2026-05-14 SSH)**: (1) A100 venv `~/venvs/p79` 当前只有 torch, 缺 transformers / qwen_vl_utils → 需 `pip install -e ".[analysis,dev]"`; (2) A100 VM 上 VWA docker stack bring-up; (3) shopping base_url config 需适配 local docker (旧 quirk "非 localhost", local 化后重核)。
- **Gemma 接入风险** (codex + claude cross-review, `codex_outputs/gemma3vl_integration_crossreview_2026-05-14.md`): 4 个隐藏污染向量 — GLM parse-repair 不对称 / `model.revision` 未被 backend 转发 / `queue_chain.sh` collision 逻辑 B0-vs-B1 写死 / `StepRecordV2` 无 `model_family` 字段。两模型独立估算均 = 3-5 focused days。

### 140.11 A100 docker bring-up 实战 — 4 个 paper-grade-blocker bug 串发 (2026-05-15) [bug][infra]
继 §140.10。今天把 A100 dockers 起到 paper-grade fire 状态过程中**连续踩 4 个互不相关的 bug**，全是生态侧不可预期的事，paper-grade infra 不可能靠 dry-run 提前发现。归档保 future-me / 协作者 reference。

**Bug 1: HF dataset 全 404**
- `webarena/{Shopping,Reddit,Wikipedia,Classifieds}` HF dataset 仓库 RepositoryNotFound (2026-05-14 verified)。`setup_vwa.sh` 4 个 download 函数全死。
- Fix (commit `dba04fe`)：改用 **CMU metis 镜像** (`metis.lti.cs.cmu.edu/webarena-images/`) 拉 tarballs，archive.org 拉 classifieds zip。镜像无 CDN，速度 1-10MB/s。
- Lesson: VWA upstream 假设 HF datasets 永久存在，是脆弱依赖。镜像必须能 `wget -c` resume 的 HTTP。

**Bug 2: Docker 29.x + containerd-snapshotter 解压死锁 (jykoh/classifieds:latest)**
- A100 安装 docker 29.1.3，默认 `containerd-snapshotter=true`。`docker pull jykoh/classifieds:latest` 始终卡在 layer `91eb47e4f196c` 的 extraction 阶段，**`ec57d9f250af Extracting 700+ seconds` 后死活不前进**。
- 现象：`docker pull` PID `STAT=Sl WCHAN=futex_wait_queue_me`，0% CPU 0% mem = blocked on futex / 不是网络问题（同时拉 alpine:latest 秒成）。
- 根因：dockerd journal 报 `failed to cleanup "extract-XXX sha256:91eb47e4...": NotFound: snapshot does not exist` + `Cancel with lease, leased resources will remain until expiration` —— containerd-snapshotter 内部 extraction state corrupt + 留下 8h gc lease 锁血。`ctr -n moby leases ls` 看到 5 个 stale lease，删后仍 hang。
- 不是单 layer 损坏 —— `docker manifest inspect` 验证 manifest 正常单平台 (linux/amd64) 6+ layers 最大 104MB 压缩。换 `ctr pull` 也卡在同一位置 (STAT=S WCHAN=pipe_read)。
- Workaround（最终方案）：**DGX 上 `docker save jykoh/classifieds:latest` → stream over ssh → `docker load` on A100** —— `docker save/load` 跳过 containerd extraction pipeline，直接走 image store import。DGX 已有 image 77.4GB（之前 cls 实验留的）= lucky 一手。
- ⚠️ "永远不要在 docker compose --build 跑解压时 kill 它" —— kill 留下不可释放的 lease。要等结束 / `systemctl restart docker`（lease 持久化，restart 也清不掉，要 `ctr leases rm`）。

**Bug 3: DGX home 有 disk quota，77GB save 不下**
- `df -h ~` 显示 519GB free 但实际 user quota 远低于。`docker save -o /home/jiaming/jykoh_cls.tar` 5min 后写 `disk quota exceeded`，tar 删除。中间步骤设计（save → rsync → load）破产。
- 转 stream 方案：`docker save | ssh condense-a100 'docker load'` 不落 DGX 盘。代价：SSH chain 断了不可 resume。

**Bug 4: `pip install -r external/visualwebarena/requirements.txt` 是毒药**
- 跑 preflight 报 `browser_env` import 失败（缺 PYTHONPATH/editable install）→ 修补 `pip install -e external/visualwebarena/` → 还缺 `beartype` → 一念之差跑了 `pip install -r requirements.txt`。
- 后果：VWA pin 的 2 年前老版本**强制降级整 venv** —— transformers 5.8.1 → 4.34.0、torch 2.11.0+cu128 → 2.0.1+cu117、playwright 1.59 → 1.37。**Gemma3-VL T5 smoke 之前 PASS 的状态作废**（Gemma3 类不存在于 transformers 4.34）。
- 恢复（实战）：`pip uninstall` 老 nvidia-cu11 系列 → `pip install --force-reinstall --no-deps torch==2.11.0+cu128 torchvision==0.26.0+cu128` → cu12 NVIDIA libs 一组（cudnn / cublas / cudart / cupti / cufft / curand / cusolver / cusparse / nvrtc / nvtx / nvjitlink / cusparselt / nccl 2.28.9 / triton）force-reinstall —— 因为 pip 的 force-reinstall 不级联 deps，cu12 包 metadata 标记"已存在"但 .so 文件可能在 cu11 卸载时丢失，必须显式每一个 force-reinstall。
- ✅ 最终 verify：`torch=2.11.0+cu128 cuda=True / transformers=5.8.1 / Gemma3 OK`。
- ⚠️ Lesson **强制写入**：以后装 VWA 包 必须用 `pip install -e external/visualwebarena/ --no-deps` 只装 webarena 包元数据，依赖让 p79 venv 自己控制（p79 pyproject.toml 是真实 source-of-truth）。VWA 的 requirements.txt 是 2 年前的快照，对 p79 的 modern stack 是 destructive。

**A100 paper-grade fire-readiness 当前 70% 绿**：
- ✅ `.venv` 符号链 / playwright + chromium / torch+cu128 + Gemma3 / B0 proxy 可达 / vwa-reddit:9999 endpoint 200 / 6 站 env 配齐含 SHOPPING_ADMIN
- 🔄 cls image stream (background `blsceq9cr`) / wave-2 (shop+wiki 0%) / preflight 5 endpoints WARN（等容器）/ T13 cookies 等所有容器

### 140.12 A100 paper-grade fire-readiness 达成 — B2 dom cls smoke 端到端验证 (2026-05-15) [finding][infra]
**Milestone**: A100 这条线打到 **"paper-grade Phase 1a (cls + red) 随时 fire"** 状态。session goal 闭环。

**完成路径**：
- ✅ T11 (cls + red containers up) — cls 走 DGX-stream workaround 绕 docker 29 bug; reddit native load OK
- ✅ T12 (A100-local site env) — localhost endpoints + SHOPPING_ADMIN:7780

- ✅ T15 (preflight) — `Preflight completed successfully.` (cls + red endpoints PASS, playwright + chromium + torch + VWA evaluator 全 PASS)
- ✅ T16 (端到端 smoke) — B2 dom cls 3-task smoke 全跑完

**T16 smoke 关键 finding & fix**：
- 第一轮 smoke (max_tasks_per_site=3, RESET_BEFORE=0) → pipeline 完整运转,episode/condition/run/analysis summaries 全产出,**但 3 task 全 fail** with `FileNotFoundError: ./.auth/classifieds_state.json`
- 根因:`auto_login.py` 把 cookies 写到 CWD-relative `./.auth` —— 当时 CWD 是 `external/visualwebarena/`(脚本在那),所以 cookies 进 `external/visualwebarena/.auth/`;但 runner CWD 是 repo-root,期望 `./.auth/` = repo-root `.auth/`。DGX 那边 cookies 一直在 repo-root `.auth/`,A100 是首次 bring-up 才暴露这个 path 错配。
- Fix:`cp external/visualwebarena/.auth/*.json ~/workspace/p79/.auth/` 复制上一级。第二轮 smoke 全通,3 task × 30 steps 全 executed:trigger_distribution = action_failed×46 / page_unchanged×37 / no_progress×37(=4B model baseline 行为正常,reviewer cycle 检测全工作)。
- success_rate=0.0 是预期 —— 3 个随机 cls task,4B Gemma3-VL baseline SR 本来就很低。**T16 验证目标 "pipeline 端到端跑通" 完全达到**;Phase 1a 真正 paper-grade run 需要全 task set。

**A100 vs DGX 差异 (residual)**：
- **缺 reset 实现** (新建 T17) —— `scripts/maintenance/reset_vwa_sites.sh` 写死 DGX→quark→Windows PowerShell 路径,A100 自托管需平行实现 (cls 用 curl reset endpoint 已验证 0.1s OK, reddit 用 docker restart, shop 用 docker exec magento)。 **没 reset = 不能做正式 paper-grade Phase 1a 跑** (条件间 contamination 风险)。
- **wave-2 (shop + wiki) 还在下** ~10%,ETA 6h+;Phase 1b shop 需要,Phase 1a 不需要。
- **cookies 路径要决定**:repo-root `.auth/` 是 P79 convention(DGX 7 entries 都在那),A100 cookies 已 copy 上去。**长期 fix**:patch `auto_login.py` 用绝对路径或 ensure 在 repo-root CWD 跑(目前是 cd external/visualwebarena 后跑)。

### 142.8 B-116 + B-112 production smoke verification on DGX (2026-05-15 evening) [bug][infra]

User confirmed Phase 1a clean rerun 启动前需 smoke verify。Quark Docker 重启后 VWA cls/red/shop 三站从 DGX reachable (cls 357ms / red 4.3s / shop 7.8s warm-up)。Fire 1-task smoke on B1 dom classifieds with new 4096 cap;observed 5 production-path step records 后 kill 节省 GPU minute (full 30-step ETA ~2h on GB10)。

**Smoke verification — 5 step PASS** (run dir kept as forensic: `results/visualwebarena/phase1/B1_dom_classifieds_20260515_190653/`):

| Invariant | 预期 | 实测 (5 step) | Verdict |
|---|---|---|---|
| Output tokens range | < 100 typical, < 4096 cap | min=63, max=99, mean=79.0 | ✓ B-116 4096 cap correctly idle (cap is upper-bound not forced length) |
| Output tokens cap hit count | 0 | 0/5 (no 384 ⊂ no 4096) | ✓ no truncation observed |
| `image_meta` in dom mode step_record | None | `image_meta: None` | ✓ B-112 schema field present, dom-mode correctly null |
| `parse_valid` | all True | Counter({True: 5}) | ✓ no parse failure, no keyword fallback exercised |
| StepRecordV2 schema conform | all keys present | 5/5 records valid JSONL | ✓ B-99 / B-102 / B-112 schema unbroken |
| action_type diversity | type/scroll/click | type, scroll, click, type, type | ✓ no silent automation (B-114 keyword fallback removed; no scroll-by-substring path) |

**Runtime calibration (unrelated to fix, but logged for Phase 1a wall-time planning)**:
- B1 4B on DGX GB10 decode rate: **~2.6s / output token** (184s generate / 71 tokens) — ~10x slower than A100
- Per-step total: ~190s (184s generate + 6s env step)
- 30-step single-task ETA: ~2h
- Full Phase 1a single-baseline-single-site if serial-on-DGX: ~9 weeks
- Implication: Phase 1a paper-grade rerun should target Condense A100 (T0) for B1/B2 throughput, DGX retained for B0 (proxy is network-bound, not compute-bound)

**Production-path evidence for B-116 cap derivation theory** (codex Q2 said 0.017% B1 384-truncation rate from archived): smoke 5 step max output = 99 tokens,**well below 384** — even if cap had been 384 these 5 steps would not have truncated. Live evidence reinforces the codex archived-data quantification: max_new_tokens cap is over-engineered for typical agent thought+JSON envelope (< 100 tok), with only outlier "long reasoning" steps hitting the original 384 cap. Upgrading to 4096 disables the silent-truncation surface entirely without cost regression.

### 144.9 Commit C — paper §3.4.1 P-prompt disclosure (P2-1)

gemini G5 attack: "P-prompt graceful-degradation framing 跟 intentional 2x2 design 框架自相矛盾"。User clarification: contradiction IS the 2x2 experimental design — prompt-text ID-space disagreement 是 manipulated variable, 不是 confound。

§3.4.1 prose 改写 (line 84 + line 88):
- 原 (ii) 把 mismatch 框架成 "Modern multimodal LLMs handle disagreement by parser fallbacks... Section 4 reports whether P-prompt collapses or partially holds" → **事后辩护语气**, 暗示 graceful-degradation 期望
- 改后 (ii) "The prompt-text ID-space disagreement IS the manipulated variable, not a confound" + 明确 "the design point probes whether SoM prompt's behavioral effect generalizes off-distribution"
- 新加 **Disclosure** paragraph (响应 cross-AI audit, 2026-05-15): (a) action-parse-success rate 作 first-class metric (b) 不把 P-prompt SR collapse 解释为 against H3 (c) anchor structural claim 在 |P-prompt ∖ P-SoM| 而非 raw P-prompt SR → claim 对 collapse / partial-success 两种 outcome 都 robust

Statistical pre-commitment 防 post-hoc framework switching。§4 暂无 P-prompt 引用 (clean run 数据未 land), §1 引用 P-prompt 仅作 structural control mention 不涉 outcome framing, 无需改。

**§144 final tally — Commit C landed**: 13 fixes (B-131~B-143) + 5 advisor-parked + **2 paper-side disclosures (both landed)**。A1.1 fully closed。

## 146. /stress A1.2 v8 `p79/backends/` 3-AI cross-AI cycle + 10-fix Commit D+E + skill v7.4 双语 + 推荐修改 spec (2026-05-16) [infra][bug][design] #infra #bug #design

继 §144 (A1.1 v8) 之后, 对 `p79/backends/` (action_utils 在 A1.1 已覆盖, 余 7 file × 564 LOC) 跑 second-pass /stress。第一轮 A1.2 已 land F1-F6 (commits `9316c8a` / `2a05e9a` / `50e10b3`); 这次第二轮检查 post-A1.1 (B-131~B-143) 改动是否引入 backend wrapper contract drift + first-pass miss 的 sibling-propagation gap。

### 146.1 Cross-AI discovery (17 distinct findings, 9 OOB)

Mode A (Claude inline): **7 findings / 3 OOB** — F1-A\* max_new_tokens 512 stale sibling-propagation / F2-A stale comment vs B-136 / F3-A\* mock action 不对齐 / F4-A\* over_cap step_record (后查证已 B-140 完成) / F5-A stage_prefix triplicate / F6-A MockBackend backend_type 无 tag / F7-A\* PIL Resampling enum 版本依赖。

Mode B (codex `/codex-stress`, persona=reproducibility-auditor, ~3min): **5 findings / 3 OOB** — **P0-B1\*** multi-seed backend cache 冻结第一个 seed (paper-grade blocker for any future multi-seed validation) / **P0-B2\*** GLM fallback unseeded cross-baseline asymmetric / **P1-B3** decoding override cascade asymmetric (T/top_p only B0 honors) / **P1-B4\*** Gemma hidden Qwen dep (`gemma3vl_agent → qwen3vl_agent → qwen_vl_utils`) / **P1-B5** dom_mode B2 propagation 缺失。

Mode C (gemini `/gemini-stress`, persona=broad-reviewer, ~3min): **5 findings / 2 OOB** — **P0-C1\*** HeuristicDomBackend 裸构造破坏 prior /stress A1.2 F3 刚 land 的 2-arg 契约 (latent contract debt) / **P0-C2** `model_calls` 硬编码 1 掩盖 GLM fallback + future retry → paper §1 cost-fair claim 假 / **P1-C3** Planner/grounder stage_prefix backend-level prompt engineering 须 §4 disclose / **P1-C4\*** `api_key_env` config-override 构成 secret exfiltration 向量 / **P2-C5** mock divergence (跟 A F3 overlap)。

**Cross-AI agreement**: 2 overlap (F3-A=C5 mock div, F5-A≈C3 stage_prefix partial), 13 unique catches。**3 AI 强互补 ratio**: codex 全 reproducibility-auditor (seed/cache/cross-baseline), gemini 全 architecture (contract violation / security / disclosure), Claude 全 sibling-propagation (stale comment / cap / version pin)。

### 146.2 Skill v7.4 — 双语 + 推荐修改 spec land

User 反馈 round 1 bug table 排版纯英文 cognitive load 高 + 缺 "你打算怎么改" pre-empted section。Skill v7.4 强制:
1. **Blast Radius 列 MUST 双语**: 中文 prose 描述 cascade effect (机制 / 后果 / paper-grade impact) + English code references (file:line, function name, field name, specific number)。纯中文 OR 纯英文都违反 spec。
2. **推荐修改 section REQUIRED for all P0+P1**: 推荐方案 (具体改法, 不是"考虑加") / Effort (X min / X hours) / 风险 (regression vector 一句话) / 依赖 (prereq, e.g. advisor sync)。

Land 位置: `.claude/skills/stress/SKILL.md` v7.4 versioning entry + 输出 spec 强化。

### 146.3 Q&A 收口 decisions (4 user questions)

User 4 个 challenge 让 fix scope 大幅收口:
1. **GLM fallback** 本身是 advisor-pending item (B-86, P0-7), 目标整个 module retire (等官方 Qwen API tool_choice channel land)。所以 P0-B2 修法**改为 disable default 不修 seed**, 整个 module 即将退役。
2. **Heuristic** 是 Phase 2 router substrate (rule-based route 把 "不需要 LLM" 的 step 短路到零成本 backend), Phase 1a 不直接用 → 相关 bugs (P0-C1 contract / P1-B5 dom_mode B2 / P2-A5+C3 stage_prefix) 都**降权 Phase 2/3 启用前修**。
5. **Gemma → Qwen dep**: 工程债 (Gemma 后加, 复用 Qwen prompt classmethod 是 cheapest 对齐手段; `qwen_vl_utils` 实际不用但 Python 静态 import chain 强拉)。Phase 1a clean run 在 DGX 不爆但 Condenser A100 自托管 Gemma 时会 ImportError → 选 Option A proper 解耦。
6. **Planner/grounder triplicate**: 同段 14 行 prompt prefix 在 3 个 backend wrapper 复制粘贴, M4 two-stage (Phase 3) 启用时才触发 → Phase 1a dead code path, **Phase 3 启用前修**即可。

User 最后 challenge **retry 进 cost** 这个 P0-C2 finding: 区分了**network retry** (HTTP transport-level, 只 billed 一次 tokens, B-143 已通过 `latency_ms.total_minus_retry` 分离到 latency 维度) vs **model-call retry** (GLM fallback, 真 2 次模型 inference)。结论: P0-C2 in code 不需要修 — 移除 GLM fallback default 后 `model_calls=1` 硬编码就是真实值, 整个 module 退役时彻底删掉。**这是"by-decision dissolution"** — 上游决策让下游 bug 自动消失。

### 146.4 Commit D 落地: B-144 ~ B-150 (P0+P1 + P2-A2, 6 fixes + 1 cosmetic + 12 invariant test)

- **B-144** (Claude inline + codex B1) multi-seed backend cache: `runner/main.py` `_backends` cache key 从 `backend_id` 改成 `(backend_id, int(seed))` tuple → seed-loop 检测到 seed 切换强制重建 backend 把 seed 传入 agent_cfg。Test: `test_runner_backend_cache_keyed_by_backend_id_and_seed`
- **B-145** (codex B2) GLM fallback disable default + deprecation: `configs/exp_v2_base.yaml:160` `use_glm_fallback: true → false`; `proxy_api_agent.py` GLM 块加 "MARKED FOR FULL RETIRE" docstring + DeprecationWarning。等官方 Qwen API channel land 后整体删。
- **B-146** (codex B4) Gemma → Qwen 解耦: 新 `p79/agents/_shared_vl_utils.py` (轻量 deps, torch + logging only, no transformers / qwen_vl_utils) 抽出 6 个跨 baseline 函数 (`make_dom_prompt` / `make_som_prompt` / `make_vision_prompt` / `format_history` / `compute_confidence` / `wait_for_vram`); Gemma 直接 import 共享 module; Qwen classmethods 保留作 backward-compat delegate (proxy_api_agent + mechanistic scripts + tests/test_agents_prompt_parity 都不动)。byte-identical 验证: Qwen / Gemma / 共享 module 三方 prompts 完全相同。
- **B-147** (Claude A1) `api_proxy.py:28` max_new_tokens default 512 → 4096: 3-baseline wrapper layer 现 default 全 4096 对齐 agent layer (B-135), defense-in-depth 防 future config refactor regression。
- **B-148** (gemini C4) `api_proxy.py` `api_key_env` allowlist: `_ALLOWED_API_KEY_ENVS = {"PROXY_API_KEY", "DASHSCOPE_API_KEY", "GLM_API_KEY"}` + `_validate_api_key_env()` 在 backend init 时 raise ValueError 拒绝任何非 allowlist 值。防 yaml-injection secret exfiltration。
- **B-149** (Claude A3 + gemini C5 dual-AI catch) `api_proxy.py` mock action `click element_id=1 → scroll [0, 0.8]` 跟 local_qwen/local_gemma/MockBackend 对齐。
- **P2-A2** stale comment `local_qwen.py:27-28` realigned with B-136 strict-mode (注释从 "falls back to default + warns" 改成 "raises RuntimeError at agent init")。

Commit E 同 commit batch land P2 polish:
- **B-153** stale comment fix (上面 P2-A2 同一项, in test header)
- **B-154** (Claude A6) `factory.MockBackend.backend_type = f"mock_{backend_id}"` 替代裸 "mock", cross-baseline mock invariant test 现可分辨 baseline。
- **B-155** (Claude A7) PIL pin: `pyproject.toml` `pillow>=10.0,<12.0`; `image_utils.py` 顶部 import-time assert `PIL.__version__ >= (10, 0)` 防 venv 手动覆盖。

A1.2 fully closed。下一个 phase1_plan item: **A1.3 `p79/envs/`** (VWA 环境封装 / locator dispatch / observation standardization)。

## 147. /stress A1.3 v8 `p79/envs/` 3-AI cross-AI cycle + 6-fix Commit F + 3 by-design dissolved (2026-05-16) [infra][bug][design] #infra #bug #design

继 §146 (A1.2 v8) 之后, 对 `p79/envs/` (vwa_wrapper.py 939 LOC + locator_dispatch.py 316 LOC = 1255 LOC, 2 file) 跑 second-pass /stress。第一轮 A1.3 已 land F1-F6 + B-105/106/107 (commits `131949b` / `9fb9b38` / `b1a31a2`); 第二轮检查 post-A1.2 sibling drift + vwa_wrapper.py 大组件 audit。

### 147.1 Cross-AI discovery (21 raw → 19 distinct, 11 OOB)

Mode A (Claude inline): 7/4 OOB — F1 navigate_to f-string injection / F2 coord normalize boundary / F3 inject_options regex / F4 horizontal scroll / F5 locator telemetry / F6 silent swallow / F7 fuzzy threshold。
Mode B (codex repro-auditor, ~5min): 7/4 OOB — P1-B1 locator-route 新 tab loss / P1-B2 dialog handler 多 tab gap / P1-B3 asyncio passthrough / P1-B4 coord_type ignored / P2-B5 form snapshot truncation / P2-B6 select empty-value filter / P2-B7 locator telemetry (overlap F5)。
Mode C (gemini broad-reviewer, ~6min): 7/3 OOB — C1 invisible turn injection / C2 select stale coord / C3 asyncio hijack / C4 shadow DOM blindness / C5 hover/upload dead code / C6 P79Observation.image polymorphism / C7 select redundant env.step。

Cross-AI agreement: 1 dual-AI overlap (F5≈B7 telemetry), 18 unique。

### 147.2 User Q&A 收口 — 3 dissolved + 1 demoted

User challenge 让 3 个 finding 直接 dissolve + 1 个 demote:
1. **C1 invisible turn** DISSOLVED: user 指出 `_type_needs_enter` post-Enter `env.step(NONE)` 是 obs refresh 不是独立 step。Paper §3 step semantics per agent action, gemini misread。
2. **C2 select stale coord** DEMOTED P2: 笔记 §51/§54/§60/§61/§62 已 5 轮修过 select_option; 真正 attack 是 framework-level (所有 element_id action 都用 `_last_obs_nodes_info` snapshot, 非 select 特定)。
3. **F2 + B4 coord normalize** DISSOLVED: user 确认 `>1.0` heuristic 自动判断 pixel vs normalized 是 intentional 设计 — 模型 output 不可信任 `coordinate_type` 字段。Codex P1-B4 + Claude F2-A 都违背设计。
4. **F3 inject_options regex** DISSOLVED: re-audit VWA AXTree `processors.py:531` 行格式 `[N] role 'name' key: value` 不含 inline `[N]` brackets; 代码 line 868 已 double-guard `m.group(1) in injections` dict lookup → fire rate ~0。User "选择绕过" 就是 dict guard。

**By-decision dissolution 比 code fix 更高效** — cross-AI audit **无法替代 user-level design intent review**。

### 147.3 Commit F: B-156 ~ B-161 (6 P1 fixes + 8 tests)

- **B-156** (Claude F5 + codex B7 dual catch) locator-route telemetry → step_record: `StepRecordV2.locator_route_meta` + `vwa_wrapper.step()` 把 `_lr_result + action_kind` stamp 到 `info["locator_route_meta"]` + runner 写入 step_record。Phase 1a clean run 后 audit 跨 site Cluster 1 ON_TARGET 命中率。
- **B-157** (codex B1) locator-route 新 tab handling: click 前 capture `_num_tabs_before`, success 后检测 `len(pages) > _num_tabs_before` 切到 `pages[-1]` + `bring_to_front()` + `self._env.page = _new_page` (mirrors VWA `browser_env/actions.py:1417-1421`)。
- **B-158** (codex B2) dialog handler context-level: `_dialog_registered_page` → `_dialog_registered_context`; `ctx.on("page", lambda new_page: new_page.on("dialog", _on_dialog))` 让 listener 跟随 context lifetime, 多 tab dialog 自动接管。
- **B-159** (codex B3) asyncio loop raise loud: `_lazy_init()` + `reset()` 检测 running loop 时 `raise RuntimeError` with actionable message (subprocess 隔离建议)。替换 silent passthrough。
- **B-160** (Claude F1) navigate_to URL `json.dumps` escape: `create_playwright_action(f"page.goto({json.dumps(url)})")`。VWA 上游 exec path 安全化。
- **B-161** (gemini C4) shadow DOM elementFromPoint penetration: `locator_dispatch.py` 加 `_JS_SHADOW_DESCENT_FN` helper + `_pierceElementFromPoint(cx, cy)` 递归下钻 shadow root (depth ≤ 5); 3 个 `_JS_RESOLVE_*` 都改用; walk-up 加 `getRootNode().host` 跨 shadow boundary。Phase 1b cross-family / reddit-redesign 受惠。

**Test delta**: 177 → 185 (+8 new tests, 100% pass, 0 regression)。

### 147.4 §147 final tally

| Round | B-numbers | Items |
|---|---|---|
| Commit F (6 P1 fixes) | B-156 ~ B-161 | locator telemetry / 新 tab / dialog context / asyncio raise / url escape / shadow DOM |

| Demoted P2 latent | C2 | 1 item, framework-level |
| P2 polish defer | F4/F6/F7/C3/C5/C6/C7/B5/B6 | 9 items |

**Pattern**: §147 验证 second-pass /stress 价值 — `p79/envs/` 第一轮已修 6 finding, second-pass 仍发现 6 个新 P1 (4 OOB)。但**最大收口靠 user Q&A** — 3/4 我 (+ gemini) 误读 design intent。

A1.3 fully closed。下一个 phase1_plan item: **A1.4 `p79/experiment/`** (核心实验引擎, ~15-20 file, runner/main.py 单文件 ~1700 LOC), pre-fire scope (≥7/≥3 OOB)。

## 148. /stress A1.4a v8 `p79/experiment/` orchestrator core 3-AI cycle + 4-commit G1-G4 (2026-05-16) [infra][bug][design][finding] #infra #bug #design #finding

A1.4 (`p79/experiment/`) 按 split-large-scope rule 拆 3 chunks (A1.4a orchestrator / A1.4b data plane / A1.4c auxiliary), 本 § = **A1.4a only** (5 file / 2521 LOC: `runner/main.py` 1695 + `runner/helpers.py` 299 + `router.py` 149 + `conditions.py` 208 + `tasks.py` 170)。Pre-fire scope (≥7 findings / ≥3 OOB)。3-AI cycle 出 23 raw → 21 distinct (4 dual-catch), 经 user Q&A 收口 6 修 / 6 dissolve / 5 demote-defer / 4 paper-2 scope。

### 148.1 Cross-AI discovery (21 distinct findings, 13 OOB)

- **Mode A (Claude inline)** 8/4 OOB — F1 about:blank silent attribution (P0) / F2 fallback_finish reward override (P0, → D1 dual) / F3 error_category fall-through (P0) / F4 trajectory_incomplete empty answer (P0) / F5 phantom som_on=False (P1) / F6 multi-site task duplication (P1) / F7 zero-step error race watchdog (P1) / F8 cycle detection min_reps (P2)。
- **Mode B (codex reproducibility-auditor, ~5min)** 8/5 OOB — B1 partial-step crash → zero-step summary (P0) / B2 resume stale identity (P0) / B3 fallback_finish reward override (P0, → D1 dual) / B4 schema v2.0 drift (P1) / B5 shallow backend cfg copy nested mutation (P1) / B6 task order non-reproducible manifest (P1, → D3 dual) / B7 placeholder provenance gap (P1) / B8 analysis subprocess timeout invisible (P2)。
- **Mode C (gemini broad-reviewer, ~3min)** 7/4 OOB — C1 escalation monotonicity bypass (P1) / C2 Phase 2 metadata-state Frankenstein (P0, → F5 partial dual) / C3 trigger distribution inflation (P1) / C4 phase 3 synergy ceiling gap (P1) / C5 heuristic injection diagnostic_controls (P1) / C6 dom_size threshold cross-mode fragility (P2, → Claude gap dual) / C7 silent escalation reset (P2)。

Cross-AI 4 dual-catch: F2≡B3 (fallback_finish P0) / F5≈C2 (som_on, Claude phantom + gemini Phase 2 Frankenstein) / F6≈B6 (task ordering, Claude duplication + codex order non-reproducible) / Claude-gap≡C6 (dom_size cross-mode)。

### 148.2 User Q&A 收口 — 5 dissolved + 4 demoted + scope expand

User 6 个问题改 fix list:

1. **Q1 fallback_finish 机制?** → 解释 backend keyword-rescue 路径 + reward override 漏 guard, D1 确认 P0 fix
2. **Q2 phantom som_on=False?** → User confirm by-design ("phantom 整个基本和 dom 一个 cost"), **D2 整个 dissolve**: phantom_som=False is correct per paper §3 "no annotated image" boundary; Phase 2 routed Frankenstein defer to paper-2
3. **Q3 about:blank?** → "应该系统研究下" — F1 改成 **mini-investigation 路径** (write analysis script + open issue file, not direct patch)
4. **Q4 invalid_action 细分?** → **F3 scope expand** 4 倍 (5 → 10 + unknown_failure bucket, validate_action_detailed 上游 emit specific reasons)
5. **Q5 F4 不懂?** → 解释 max-steps timeout 空 answer SR=0 跨 baseline 偏差; user 默认接受 Path A disclosure
6. **Q6 dom_size_threshold 跨 mode?** → User: "obs_text 每个 mode 都是清洗后的 axtree, 长度基本一致"; 我 spot-check `results/visualwebarena/phase1/B1_3mode_classifieds_20260413/.../step_000` 实证 dom=3804 / som=3879 / vision=3805 字符, **ratio 1.00-1.02×**, 我 D4 + Finding 6 + gemini C6 整个错误 — memory file `project_phantom_space_axes_format_not_information.md` 早写 "1.00× chars" 实证, 但 3 AI 都套了"AXTree → flat 应该 -50%"的错误 mental model 没去看 artifacts。**D4 dissolve by empirical fact**, 同时新加 memory feedback rule "spot-check length/token claims before file finding"。

**User-layer audit value**: 5/21 finding 经 user Q&A 直接 dissolve/defer (24%), 2/21 demoted, 1 expanded scope。Cross-AI 三方 disjoint catch 率高 (only 4 dual-catch), 但 design-intent layer 仅 user 能仲裁 — 跟 §147 (4/19 dissolve, 21%) consistency。

### 148.3 Commit G1 — cross-AI memory infra + 3 quick-wins (commit `05ff344`)

3 paper-grade fixes (renumbered B-164~B-166 后 — collision avoidance, 见 §148.6):

- **B-164** (`p79/experiment/runner/main.py:165` _get_backend) `dict(backend_cfg)` → `copy.deepcopy(backend_cfg)`。Pre-fix: 即使 B-144 cache key (backend_id, seed) tuple 正确, nested `generation/model_kwargs/headers` 共享 ref → constructor side effect 跨 seed 污染。Source: codex B5 (OOB)。
- **B-165** (`runner/main.py:1562-1585`) reward override 加 `_real_finish` guard (`parse_valid AND not fallback_finish`)。Pre-fix: keyword-rescue 触发的 fallback finish 也走 score=0→1 override → 跨 baseline 不公平 (B1/B2 4B fallback rate 高于 B0 235B → B1/B2 SR 系统性虚高 1-3pp)。Source: Claude F2 + codex B3 dual catch。
- **B-166** (`runner/main.py:1545-1568` + episode_summary stamp) `trajectory_incomplete` telemetry。Max-steps timeout 无 explicit finish → fake stop action with empty answer → string_match SR=0 跨 baseline timeout-rate confound。Path A disclosure (SR canonical, 加 transparency field)。Source: Claude F4 (OOB)。

**Cross-AI memory infra**: 新建 `AGENTS.md` (codex CLI 约定) + `GEMINI.md` (gemini CLI 约定) 都是 1-page pointer 文件 → `.claude/CLAUDE.md`。`/stress` 之外 ad-hoc codex/gemini invocation 现在也 inherit 项目 context。`~/.codex/AGENTS.md` 实际 0-byte 空文件, 之前并没有 set up persistent memory (跟用户 perception 不一致)。

Tests: `tests/test_stress_a1_4a_g1_fixes.py` 6 invariants (deepcopy / fallback_finish guard / trajectory_incomplete x2 / AGENTS.md / GEMINI.md)。185 → 191 pass。

### 148.4 Commit G2 — invalid_action 7-category 细分 + unknown_failure bucket (commit `10b6e4c`)

- **B-167** (`p79/backends/action_utils.py` + `runner/main.py`) 上游 `validate_action_detailed` emit 5 sub-category reasons (invalid_schema_dict / invalid_action_type / invalid_element_id / invalid_coord / invalid_select_option), backward-compat 2-tuple `validate_action` wrapper 保留, 20+ callers 不动。`_normalize_error_category` 从 5 → 10 categories + `unknown_failure` future-proof bucket。`parse_action_text` Path 1 (clean JSON) 用 detailed reason 透传。Source: Claude F3 expanded scope per user Q&A。

Episode summary 新增 `unknown_failure_reasons: Dict[str, int]` Counter (filtered from step_records by `error_category == "unknown_failure"`) — paper-grade tripwire, 新 backend 加 novel failure_reason 时 catalog 自动收集。

Router-aware escalation policy (per-category target mode mapping: invalid_element_id → SoM / invalid_coord → vision) **defer to Phase 2 / paper-2 scope** (current Phase 1a router_on=False)。

Priority ordering fix (sed iteration): coord-present-but-malformed beats invalid_element_id (specific reason wins)。

Existing `tests/test_action_utils.py` 2 个 case 测试 `"invalid_action"` legacy reason → 改成 `"invalid_action_type"` / `"invalid_element_id"` specific reasons (intentional behavior change)。

Tests: `tests/test_stress_a1_4a_g2_fixes.py` 20 invariants。191 → 211 pass。

### 148.5 Commit G3 — JSONL/resume hardening (commit `1495a6e`)

**Paper-grade reproducibility refactor**。两个 same-episode JSONL-vs-summary divergence 关闭:

- **B-168** (`runner/main.py:618-684` except path) partial-step crash recovery。新 `ExperimentRunner._aggregate_partial_steps(step_records)` aggregate steps/tokens/cost/latency/retries from already-written JSONL rows; `_run_and_record_episode` except path 调 `read_jsonl_dedup` + `_aggregate_partial_steps`, summary 用真实数字而非 `steps=0,total_cost=0` 零步骤覆盖。Pre-fix: 12-step episode crash at step 13 → summary 写 `steps=0,total_cost=0` 但 JSONL 已有 12 行 — 同 episode 两个数据产品 truth split。Source: codex B1 (OOB)。
- **B-169** (`runner/main.py:367-432` resume gate) 新 `_validate_resume_identity(loaded, expected)` 校验 6-field tuple (schema_version, run_id, condition_id, seed, benchmark_site, task_id); mismatch → quarantine to `<episodes>/quarantine/<basename>.<ts>.json` + 落回 re-run。Pre-fix: output_root 复用 + run_id/seed 改了 → stale summary 静默 ingested into new aggregate。Source: codex B2 (OOB)。

Tests: `tests/test_stress_a1_4a_g3_fixes.py` 14 invariants (aggregate 4 + identity 7 + integration 3)。211 → 225 pass。

### 148.6 Commit G4 — chronicle + master_bug_catalog §149 + F1 mini-investigation + B-number collision resolution (commit `<TBD>`)

**B-number collision pattern** (第二次出现, 跟 §145 B-150/151/152 经验):

- 这是 cross-session parallel work 的预期 pattern, 不是 process bug; lesson: B-number reservation 不可靠, 落到 catalog 后才 canonical

**F1 about:blank mini-investigation** (deferred 直接 patch 改为系统研究):

## 149. Router design v1 → v2 via 3-AI cross-stress (2026-05-16) [design][infra] #design #infra

**Trigger**: user 指令 "使用 stress 进行 router 的设计思考，综合三方 ai 给出两种 router 的设计方案"。Phase 1 plan §C 已锁 router 双路线 (rule-based + learned)，但 preregistration §2 H9/H10 estimand 当前 `<pending>`，proposal 细节 (feature / split / baseline) 未 commit — 这次设计 session 把它推到 v2 pre-launch 锁定状态。

### 149.1 Workflow

1. 草拟 `router_proposals_v1.md` (P1 rule-based 4 layer + P2 learned 4520-dim TF-IDF+LR) 作 stress artifact
2. Mode A (Claude) self-stress → `router_stress_mode_a_2026-05-16.md` — 7 findings / 3 OOB
3. Mode B (codex) `/codex-stress` 并行 → `codex_outputs/router_design_FINAL_2026-05-16_084921.md` — 8 findings / 3 OOB / PASS verification
4. Mode C (gemini) `/gemini-stress` 并行 → `gemini_outputs/router_design_2026-05-16_084921.md` — 7 findings / 4 OOB / PASS (internal-retry-recovered)
5. 综合 22 findings (4 三家 / 4 两家 / 7 单家独家) → `router_proposals_v2.md` (defuses all P0 + most P1)

### 149.2 Cross-AI value 实证 — 7 unique findings 单 AI 漏

- **codex 独家 P0 (3)**: `adjusted_success` 已退役但 preregistration §359 还写 → label provenance drift; P1 伪代码 early-return bypass `RuleBasedRouter.decide()` 的 streak state update; `compute_axtree_complexity` 不存在 (只有 `build_page_state` line-count) → ImportError
- **gemini 独家 P0 (1)**: "Phantom Renaming" — P2 TF-IDF+LR 实质 = preregistration Appendix B H7 deferred paper-2 oracle router 改名 H10 塞回 paper-1 → "hollow contribution padding" reviewer attack。Defuse: P2 增加 explicit test-leak-free constraint (features = step-1-observable only, no post-run signals) — 这是 paper-1 P2 vs paper-2 H7 的 scientific distinction
- **gemini 独家 P1 (1)**: "Lazy Minimization reversal" — `paper_planning §2` 记录 B0 文本轴主导 / B1 图像轴主导, cross-model transfer 假设 shared distribution 与 paper 自身文献矛盾 → P2 cross-model 改 cross-site
- **Claude 独家 P0 (1) + P1 (2)**: estimand `<pending>` 但 downstream commit (HARKing); best-single-mode anchor fold-noisy at N=180; Tier-0 random uniform = strawman

### 150.5 Patterns

- **Cross-AI ROI 量化**: 3 AI 17 unique findings vs Claude alone 9 (codex 独家 8 / gemini 独家 1). Codex reproducibility-auditor lens 对 paper §3 evidence-layer 价值最大 (B1 net-saving 数字 + B2 estimand drift 都是 design-layer + mechinterp 看不见). Gemini 对 paper ↔ code mismatch 框架 "Schrödinger's Adjustment" 价值高于具体 P0 数量.
- **User Q&A 作为第 4 层 audit**: 用户 "make analysis 看看还缺什么" 直接 reframe scope — 把 "做不完" 的 3 个 new artifact 放进 issue tracker 而不是死扛, 节省 6-9h.
- **Split-large-scope rule (memory feedback) 实测**: 2463 LOC 单 audit pass 会失焦, 拆 2 chunks 后 A1.4b-i 1557 LOC 单文件足以 pre-fire 深审且 4-commit 拆分清晰可 review.
- **Cross-validate 配对**: A2/C2 Holm缺 / A4/C3 4-fold / A5/C1 adjusted / A8/C5 Pareto tie 都是 Claude+gemini 跨 lineage 验证.
- **Spotcheck 实测**: codex 主动跑 TOST 公式 (+2pp lift → 0.905, zero lift → 0.0) + DL τ² (Q=2 df=2 → 0) 的 strong-claims anti-attack 是 v6 Skill 的明确收益.

### 150.8 B-184 land — canonical Phase 1 prereg gate producer (2026-05-16 evening)

紧接 A1.4b-i 5-commit 收口后, 用户决定提前做 B-184 (原 deferred 0.5-1 day scope) — 因为 B-184 是 paper §1 H1 hero claim 的 source-of-truth, 决定其他 3 个 producer 优先级和 paper prose 走向。

**Spec source**: `preregistration.md` line 68-86 lock:
- estimand = "FE inverse-variance pooled P-SoM drop-one over 6 planned cells"
- per-cell: θ_i = mean over universe of `[t ∈ oracle_6] − [t ∈ oracle_5_drop_PSoM]`, 乘 100 (pp)
- SE_i = paired B=1000 bootstrap (prereg 明锁 1000, 非 analyze_run 的 10000)
- FE pool: `w_i = 1/SE_i²`, `θ_FE = Σw_iθ_i/Σw_i`, `SE_FE = sqrt(1/Σw_i)`
- z-test: `z = (θ_FE − 1.0pp) / SE_FE`, reject H0 当 `p_one_sided < 0.05`

**实施** (`scripts/analysis/aggregate_phase1_prereg_gate.py`):
- Reuse `_build_cells` + `load` + `MIN_EP_FOR_CELL` from `aggregate_phantom_lift.py` 避免 cell enumeration drift
- 自实现 `_norm_cdf` (math.erf, 不依赖 scipy)
- `_cell_drop_one_theta_se`: 验证 6-mode 全在场 → 6-mode 观察 universe 交集 → oracle_6 / oracle_5_no_psom 集合 → per-task diff vector → mean + paired bootstrap SE
- 关键不变量: `oracle_5_no_psom ⊆ oracle_6` (任何 P-SoM 救的 task, 加 P-SoM 后 oracle_6 一定也覆盖), 所以 `diff ∈ {0, 1}` 严格. Code 加 assertion 自检.
- `_fe_pool`: SE=0 floor 防爆; k<2 返 None
- `build_gate` graceful degrade: 0 cell → `INSUFFICIENT_DATA`, 1<k<6 → `PARTIAL_DATA` + pooled FE still emitted, k=6 → `PASS` / `FAIL`

**输出 3 文件**:
- `results/phantom_paper/phase1_prereg_gate.csv` — header + 6 per-cell rows + 1 pooled FE row (paper-table-ready)
- `phase1_prereg_gate.json` — 全 metadata + per_cell array + pooled_fe block
- `phase1_prereg_gate.md` — paper §1 prose 直接可引用 (含 z + p + gate verdict)

**Live status**: 当前 `run_manifest.yaml` 无 cell 含全 6 mode (Phase 1a rerun 仍 in flight) → `INSUFFICIENT_DATA`, producer exit 0 不 block `make analysis`. Phase 1a 数据 land 后自动评估.

**Makefile wire**: `phase1-prereg-gate` 加入 `_aggregate` chain, **在 `phantom-lift` 之前**. `phantom-lift` 保留, 但语义上 demote 为 appendix exploratory (codex B2 catch — 3→5 lift 不同于 prereg drop-one).

**Tests**: `tests/test_phase1_prereg_gate.py` = 17 invariants:
- `_norm_cdf` 在 0/1.96/-1.96 三点对照
- per-task drop-one indicator (oracle_6 ⊇ oracle_5_no_psom invariant)
- 6-mode requirement (missing P-prompt → None)
- bootstrap 确定性 (seed=42 同输入两次同输出)
- SE scales with √n (n=50 vs n=200)
- FE arithmetic equal-weight → 简单 mean
- 反方差加权: SE=0.1 cell 主导 (θ_FE=2.079 vs 朴素平均 6.0)
- θ_FE=δ → z=0 → p=0.5 → 不 PASS
- k=1 → None
- SE=0 floor 防爆
- build_gate 4-state coverage (PASS / FAIL / PARTIAL_DATA / INSUFFICIENT_DATA)
- CSV writer: 6 cell + 1 pool row + header
- JSON / MD round-trips

Pre-B-184: 251 → post-B-184: 268 全 pass.

**Pattern**: prereg lock 锁的 estimand 跟 headline 跑的 estimand 不等价 是 reproducibility-auditor 视角最高 ROI 的攻击类。codex B2 catch 之所以独家是因为它**主动跑 `preregistration.md` ↔ `aggregate_phantom_lift.py` 行对照**, 不是 design / mechinterp lens 能发现的。一旦发现, 修复 (建 canonical producer) 比 patch 容易, 但只有先发现才行动。

**B-185 + B-186 仍 deferred** (per issue file 状态更新) — claim_manifest + hero_metrics 各 0.5-1 day, A1.4b-ii data plane audit 后再做 (schema 可能影响 design).

## 150. Router design v3 + preregistration §C lock + archive diagnostic verdicts (2026-05-16) [design][finding][infra] #design #finding #infra

**Trigger**: §149 router v1→v2 (3-AI cross-stress) 完成后 user 在 conversation 内追加 3 个 OOB catches (P0-8 / P0-9 / P0-10), 全部 Claude+codex+gemini 3 AI 都漏。User instruction: "直接改 preregistration"。这次 session 把 v2 → v3 设计 + preregistration §C 三 patch 落 + archive diagnostic 跑通, 验证 H10 DEFER pre-data triggered。

### 150.1 User-caught 3 OOB findings (v2 → v3)

- **P0-8 (`task.category` provenance)**: v2 P2 Stage 1 候选 pool 列 "manual task category one-hot (4, runtime-known from VWA `task.category`)" — Claude + codex + gemini 都把它当 VWA-native 信任了，但 P79 Cat A/B/C/D 实际是 codex audit derived (`docs/analysis/cross_sites/codex_audit_classifieds.json` + `codex_audit_reddit.json`)。VWA-native 任务对象有 `task_id` / `intent` / `image_url` / `start_url`, **没有**语义 category 字段。Same-class P0 leak as v1 `has_reference_image`。
- **P0-9 OOB (visual hijack 三轴)**: v2 P1+P2 决策没用 §100/§101/§M1 probe data。Probe 显示 hijack trigger = capability (B0 immune / B1 susceptible) × mark density (33→128 marks 时 B1 num_ids 0→446) × site (reddit 74 / cls 111 marks)。`[SOM_MARKS]` text fallback 让 attention bypass screenshot (num_ids 归 0) → Phantom-SoM 4-fold drop-in 的直接 mechanism 解释。
- **P0-10 OOB (`has_ref_image` task-attached)**: v2 P1 L1 rule `if has_ref_image → SoM` 写错了 reason — reference image 是 task-attached, **所有 mode 都看得到** (`docs/analysis/phantom_paper/B0_dom_shopping_diagnostic.md:87`)。正确 reasoning = "task 图需要跟 page 图对比, page 图只在 SoM/Vision screenshot 里", 但 (has_ref_image × model × density) 三轴交互让 hard-rule 不安全 — 笔记 line 2305 实测 B1 reddit Cat B (ref-image) 反转 -3.6pp。

### 150.2 v3 关键变化 (`router_proposals_v3.md`)

- **P1 v3** 真正 capability-blind: 删除 `has_ref_image` L1 rule + 不加 capability-aware hijack guard, 留 single-axis intent regex (`is_search → DOM`) + first-step browser-state escalation + L3 stateful escalation。Effort 1.5 days (-0.5 day vs v2)
- **P2 v3** 删 audit Cat 4-way categorical, 改 5 runtime intent regex binaries (`has_filter/sort/compare/aggregate/account_action`)。Stage 1 候选 pool 53 features (was 50), 含新加 `model` one-hot (P0-9 fix capability axis) + `axtree_element_count_step1` (P0-9 fix density axis); Stage 2 mutual info top-18 selection 让 LR 自动 surface (model × density × has_ref_image) 三轴交互。Effort 6-8 days
- **P1 vs P2 真正 scientific distinction**: P1 = capability-blind handcrafted single-axis baseline; P2 = learned capability-aware multi-axis classifier。Defuses 之前 gemini C1 "phantom renaming" attack (paper-1 P2 vs paper-2 H7 oracle 区分)

### 150.3 Preregistration §C 三 patch 直接落 (commit TBD)

User 指令 "直接改 preregistration"。3 patches 应用到 `docs/checkpoints/pre_run/preregistration.md`:

- **Patch C1**: §2 PRIMARY family 加 H9 + H10 + H9+H10 family-wise correction blocks。Estimand = FE inverse-variance pooled lift over 6 cells (mirror H1 §2.5 decision "3A"), δ=1.0pp, one-sided FE superiority test α=0.05, Holm correction over {H9, H10} pair。H10 DEFER condition embedded: archive G-1 entropy < log(2) → family collapses to {H9}。
- **Patch C2**: §4 best-single-mode row 加 anchor-flicker fallback — Kendall τ < 0.7 → switch 该 cell anchor 到 majority-winner-across-100-resamples。
- **Patch C3**: §4 best-single-mode row 加 outcome convention note — "adjusted-SR" → `success` (post-§139.8 retirement, VWA submodule `f0c835b`)
- Cross-doc updates: §0 outstanding items / §0 gating hypotheses list / §6 lock H-list 全更新为 H1-H3 + H9-H10; Appendix A 加 2026-05-16 entry

### 150.4 Archive diagnostic 跑通 — paper-grade verdict 3 项 land

`scripts/analysis/router_archive_diagnostic.py` 在 B0 cls + reddit × 6 modes 现有 archive 上跑 G-1/G-2/G-4 (G-3/G-5/G-6 标 second pass)。Verdict 落 `docs/checkpoints/router/archive_diagnostic_2026-05-16.{md,json}`:

| Cell | G-1 entropy | P2 viable? | G-2 Kendall τ | Anchor stable? | G-4 SD (pp) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| B0 cls (5-mode partial, phantom_prompt 4ep aborted) | 0.734 | YES (barely) | 0.696 | NO (临界) | 2.23 |
| B0 reddit (6-mode full) | **0.606** | **NO** | 0.841 | YES | 2.17 |

**关键发现**:
1. **H10 DEFER 被 pre-data triggered (reddit cell)**: reddit entropy 0.606 < log(2)=0.693 → per preregistration C1 pre-registered defer condition, **H10 整 family 塌缩为 paper-2 deferred, paper §6 router 只剩 H9 (rule-based)**。Reddit 上 86% tasks (181/210) argmax 在 DOM, learned classifier 退化为 "predict cheap default"。这是 OSF audit-trail-clean 的 pre-data decision, reviewer 不能 accuse HARKing。
2. **Anchor 实际锁定**: cls = `som` (full-cell best + majority winner 100% 一致); reddit = `phantom_som` (full-cell best + majority winner 58.4% — phantom_text 38.0% 第二)
3. **δ calibration rule 需 advisor 决断**: C1 patch 写 "SD > 0.5pp → δ → max(1.0, 2×SD)" but observed SD=2.2pp → would 提到 4.4pp。但 SD 是 sampling SE of lift estimator, 不是 null-distribution noise floor; δ 应该是 minimum-meaningful-effect floor (mirror H1 δ=1.0pp logic)。建议 **保留 δ=1.0pp + 显式 disclose 低 power (~12% at observed effects)**, 不 raise δ。这条 rule wording 需 advisor sync 修。

### 150.5 Paper §6 router framing pivot

H10 pre-data DEFER 触发后, paper §6 router section 从 "rule-based + learned" 双路线变成 **rule-based-only (H9) single-route**:
- Contribution-2 在 paper-1 变 single-claim (rule-based H9 lift over best-single-mode)
- P2 learned classifier 推 paper-2 (along with H7 oracle 已 deferred)
- Reviewer-defense narrative: "Pre-registered defer condition triggered on archive data BEFORE Phase 1a launch → H10 transparently deferred to paper-2, not HARKed post-hoc"

### 150.6 Distance shift (v2 → v3 → post-diagnostic)

| | v2 (post-3AI stress) | v3 (post-user-3-catches) | v3 + diagnostic (post-G1 H10 defer) |
|---|---|---|---|
| Workshop R3 | 0.85 | 0.90 | 0.88 (slight dip, single-route paper-1 减弱 contribution-2 强度) |
| Mid-tier R2 | 0.45 | 0.55 | 0.50 (同) |
| Top-tier R1 | 0.15 | 0.20 | 0.20 (单 H9 clean pre-data defer 比双 router HARKing 风险 stronger) |

H10 defer 是双面性: contribution count -1 (扣 workshop/mid 概率), 但 OSF audit trail clean (顶 reviewer 见 pre-data lock 加分)。net 持平。

### 150.7 Open question (pending advisor sync)

1. δ=1.0pp 保留 vs raise to 2×SD=4.4pp — 推荐保留 (effect-size floor logic) + disclose 低 power, 需 advisor 确认
2. H10 paper-2 defer 是否就此 declare paper-1 single-route router — 推荐 是 (pre-data G-1 audit clean)
3. cls phantom_prompt archive partial (4ep aborted) — 是否需要 rerun for cls full-6-mode entropy? 当前 cls 5-mode entropy 0.734 已 viable, 但是否 6-mode 加进来后 entropy 会变? 推荐 Phase 1a fresh data 直接验证, 不补 archive rerun

## 151. Router design v4 — Option C methodology fix (archive demoted from preregistration lock substrate to sanity check) (2026-05-16 evening) [design][bug][infra] #design #bug #infra

**Trigger**: §150 落地后 user 一句 "用 archive 测的 router 是不是有问题" — paper-grade reviewer-level OOB attack, **Claude + codex + gemini + Mode A 4 个 AI 整个 v1→v2→v3 stress cycle 都 miss**。User catch 是 P0-11 OOB-after-3-AI-stress。

### 151.1 User catch 本质

`router_archive_diagnostic.py` 跑在 B0 paper-grade-pre-bug archive 上 (cls 0-233 / red 0-209 task IDs)。但:
- Phase 1a 用**完全相同**的 task IDs
- Archive 是 pre-§107/§139.8 fix 数据, `success` 值漂移
- 用 archive 锁 H10 DEFER / anchor fallback / δ rule **等价于 peeking at test set**

3 个 AI 都把 archive 当 black-box pre-data substrate 信任了, 没人质疑 task-ID overlap。这是 OOB attack 只有完整 project context (知道 task ID 跨 run 复用) 的 reviewer 才能 surface。

### 151.2 6 specific methodology issues

1. Archive pre-bug 数据 → `success` 漂移
2. 同 task ID population → pattern leak
3. B0-only → 不能验证 B1/B2 cells
4. Hijack mechanism 在 B0 invisible → P2 设计中心理论 untestable
5. Pre-locked thresholds 用 Brownlee/Hastie defense 在 task-ID overlap 下 fails
6. "Raise δ to 2×SD" rule 统计层就错 (SD 是 estimator SE, δ 是 effect-size floor — 两层不同 quantity 不能 conflate)

### 151.3 Option C fix — archive 改 sanity check, locks 全移 Phase 1a fresh data

3 candidates 用户批 Option C:
- Option A: 完全 nested CV on Phase 1a (~5× compute, 超参不稳)
- Option B: literature-default 锁完全 ungrounded (~不行)
- **Option C ⭐**: archive 改 "correlated-population sanity check" + Phase 1a fresh data 作 primary trigger substrate (中间地带, honest)

### 151.4 5 retract/reframe edits 直接落

1. **preregistration §C1 H10 DEFER trigger 改源**: `archive G-1 entropy` → `Phase 1a fresh-data train-fold entropy` (rule pre-data-locked, trigger evaluated post-launch)
2. **preregistration §C2 anchor-flicker trigger 改源**: `archive G-2 Kendall τ` → `Phase 1a fresh-data train-fold Kendall τ`
3. **preregistration §C1 "raise δ to 2×SD" rule retracted entirely**: 统计错 + archive-dependent 双重错; δ=1.0pp 锁定 effect-size floor, paper §6 显式 disclose ~12-20% per-cell power
4. **v3 P1 threshold framing v4 改**: "pre-locked on archive" → "literature/typical default (VWA WebArena 文献 8-15K range 选 12K; SeeAct/BrowserGym 400-600 range 选 500); archive sanity-check 确认 direction"
5. **`docs/checkpoints/router/archive_diagnostic_2026-05-16.md` 顶部加 SANITY-CHECK ONLY 警告 + verdict 段改 "directional confidence" 不是 "DEFER triggered"

加 Appendix A 2026-05-16 evening entry 记录 chronicle。

### 151.5 v3 H10 DEFER "pre-data triggered" 在 v4 中变成什么?

§150 chronicle 声称 "H10 pre-data DEFER triggered on reddit (archive entropy 0.606)" — **v4 retract 这个声称**。H10 DEFER 是 conditional **rule**, trigger 评估在 Phase 1a fresh data 上, 不在 archive。

Archive verdict (reddit entropy 0.606) 现在是 "directional confidence: 可能 Phase 1a B0 reddit train-fold entropy 也会触发 DEFER, 但不锁定". Phase 1a fresh-data 上 B0/B1/B2 reddit 实际 entropy 才决定 H10 命运。这可能 differ — `success` 漂移 + B1/B2 capability 引入会改 label distribution。

### 151.6 Reviewer-defense narrative shift

| | v3 narrative | v4 narrative |
|---|---|---|
| Pre-data lock substrate | archive | Phase 1a fresh data train folds (rules locked, triggers post-launch) |
| Archive role | "preregistration lock substrate" (claim wrong) | "correlated-population sanity check" (honest) |
| Brownlee/Hastie defense | "thresholds frozen pre-data on archive" (fails on task-ID overlap) | "thresholds set by literature default + archive sanity confirms direction" (passes) |
| H10 DEFER framing | "triggered on archive, pre-data locked" (HARKing risk) | "rule pre-data locked, trigger on fresh data" (honest conditional) |

### 151.7 Distance shift (v3 → v4)

- Workshop R3: 0.88 → 0.90
- Mid-tier R2: 0.50 → 0.55 (honest framing 让 R2 reviewer trust 涨)
- Top-tier R1: 0.20 → 0.22 (G2 / G4 / G7 还限, 但 methodology hole 补)

v4 不加 capability / contribution, 只是补 methodology hole。但 reviewer-defense **net stronger** 即便 pre-data decision artifacts 更少 (eg H10 viability 从 pre-launch 决定 → post-launch 决定)。

### 151.8 Cross-AI value proposition 实证 (cycle 总结)

v1 → v4 共 4 轮 hostile review:
- v1→v2: Claude /stress + codex /codex-stress + gemini /gemini-stress 3-AI 12 findings
- v2→v3: user 3 OOB (P0-8 / P0-9 / P0-10) — 3 AI 都漏
- v3→v4: user 1 OOB (P0-11 archive ≠ independent test) — 3 AI + Claude self-stress 都漏

**4 个 OOB findings 全部 user-caught**, 3 AI 一个没抓到。Pattern: user 持完整 project context (task ID 复用 / Cat audit derivation / has_ref_image task-attached / hijack mechanism evidence) 能 surface 跨-doc OOB attack; 3 AI 即使 read 全部 prompt 的 artifacts 也很难 surface 这种 contextual OOB。

**结构性 takeaway**: cross-AI stress 是 floor, 不是 ceiling — user 持完整 mental context 的 OOB 抓取能力**超过** 3-AI synthesis。所以 design synthesis workflow **不能停在 3-AI 完成**, 必须有 user 介入 review 的 turn。这是 design-mode /stress 跟 audit-mode /stress 的核心 process 区别。

### 151.9 Artifacts

- `docs/checkpoints/router/proposals_v4.md` — v4 final spec (supersedes v3)
- `docs/checkpoints/pre_run/preregistration.md` — 5 edits applied (2 trigger source 改 + 1 δ rule retract + Appendix A entry + 已有 v3 anchor)
- `docs/checkpoints/router/archive_diagnostic_2026-05-16.md` — reframed SANITY-CHECK ONLY
- `docs/checkpoints/router/preregistration_C_patches_applied.md` — audit-trail record (v3 patches, v4 retract sub-text 留 history)

### 152.4 gemini chatter root-cause analysis (Path C 落地)

Gemini CLI `-p` mode 3 failure modes empirically catalogued:
1. **Chatter injection mid-output** — A1.4b-ii direct dispatch lost G1-G3 finding rows
2. **File-write sidestep** — `--yolo` 触发 `write_file`, `--approval-mode plan` 触发 `exit_plan_mode → write`
3. **JSON envelope double-wrap** — gemini 在 response 里包 `{"response": "..."}` 仿照输出格式

**Path C 修复**: `--approval-mode plan` (Pro tier auto-route to `gemini-3.1-pro-preview`) + `--output-format json` + `jq -r '.response'` + 3-rule prompt discipline (无 "Write to" path / 显式 forbid `write_file` / 显式 forbid JSON wrap).

Wrapper committed at `scripts/maintenance/gemini_stress_clean.sh` (149 LOC, header docstring self-documenting). 两 SKILL.md (本地, gitignored) + 2 replica (`docs/checkpoints/process/`, dir gitignored) 也 updated.

### 152.5 Patterns

- **0 caller as strong defuse**: codex B-ii-5/B-ii-8 + user spot-check 拯救 gemini v5 #1+#4 假阳性. Cross-AI 单 AI 高严重攻击需另 2 AI cross-check, 否则浪费 fix cycle.
- **Telemetry vs aggregation gap (B-ii-2)**: 上游 (runner) 加 field 不等于 paper-grade aggregate available. A1.4a 我们花 4 commits stamp `trajectory_incomplete` 等, A1.4b-ii 才发现 condition 级断链. **未来 telemetry add must verify aggregator path is wired**.
- **gemini chatter 3-mode 系统性 catalog**: 比起单次 "fix the bug", 把所有 failure mode 实证 catalog 后建 wrapper, 一次 land 永久 close 所有未来 /gemini-stress invocation.
- **历史归档 = forward-only convention**: user 偏好不 backfill rederive 旧数据, fill_defaults 用 baseline 默认值, paper prose 加一句 disclaimer 即可.

## 153. Router design v3 → v6 + 2-round cross-AI pre-fire stress + Pareto reformulation (2026-05-16) [design][finding][infra] #design #finding #infra

Router design 在一天内 4 个 iteration land (v3 → v4 → v5 → v6), 由 2 轮 cross-AI hostile stress + 用户 OOB catches + empirical sanity sim 共同 drive. **核心 reframe**: paper §6 router 架构从 "rule-based vs learned 二选一" 变成 "**2-layer cascade (LR prior + verbose-reactive)** 上叠 **Pareto (Cost, SR) 非主导 estimand**", 跟 paper-1 title "Cost-Aware Routing for Web Usage Agents" 完全 align.

### 153.1 v3 (rule-based prior threshold cherry-pick) → v4 (Option C: archive demoted)

`router_archive_diagnostic.py` G1/G2/G4 verdicts 原拟作 preregistration §C lock substrate. 用户 catch (OOB 4): archive 用 same task IDs as Phase 1a (cls 0-233 / red 0-209), 是 pattern leak 不是 independent test. v4 demotes archive 为 correlated-population sanity-check; H10 DEFER trigger + anchor-flicker fallback 转用 Phase 1a fresh-data 评估. 5 retract/reframe edits applied to preregistration §C. 详 §151 chronicle.

### 153.2 v4 → v5 (2-layer compose: learned L1 + verbose-reactive L2)

P1 v3 实证 archive simulation 暴露 P1 rule **决策树退化** — `dom_size > 12000` + `dom_complexity > 500` 阈值在 cls/red **全 fire 0%** (cls max 5512, red max 6963 chars). P1 v3 ≡ always_phantom_som on archive. 用户提出 reframe: 把 P2 改成 L1 (learned task-prior), 新增 L2 (verbose reactive runtime trigger) — cascade compose. Anchored to `auroc_cross_condition.csv` published fig0g AUROC (verbalized + max_repeat_streak + url_revisit_count 信号 0.7-0.85 range in 13-19 cells). v5 spec landed `proposals_v5.md` + L1 archive sim `l1_archive_simulation.py` (3 class-imbalance variants 测).

L1 archive 实证: Variant A (uniform LR) collapse to always-predict-dom (predicted); Variant B (balanced) **cls +2.56pp / red -4.76pp** site-asymmetric; Variant C (binary + hand rule) all underperform. v5 §2.5 改 "site-conditional L1": cls LR + red phantom_som hardcoded default (后被 P0-2 标 marketing spin).

### 153.3 v5 → v6 (Pareto + phantom-only verbose + 72-cond)

**第二轮 cross-AI pre-fire stress** (codex 11 findings 3 OOB + gemini 11 findings 4 OOB, 第一轮 75-sec milestone audit 太浅 user flag 后 dispatch). 关键 catches:
- **Codex pre-fire #2 OOB (P0-7)**: `p79/experiment/conditions.py:90` Phase 1a 生成器硬编码 `router_on=False`; v5 §3.4 声称 "Phase 1a measures L2" 但 conditions 不 enable router → execution-substrate hole
- **Codex #1 + Gemini #1 双 AI OOB (P0-8)**: paper title "Cost-Aware Routing" vs H9/H10 SR-only estimand = **false advertising**. 没 Pareto / cost-utility / Lagrangian
- **Codex #10 (P0-9)**: `aggregate_phase1_prereg_gate.py:178` `_fe_pool()` floor SE ≤ 0 → 1e-9, degenerate cells (e.g. P-SoM-only=0) infinite weight 主宰 FE pool — paper §1 hero claim infrastructure 可被任一 cell hijack
- **Gemini #4 (P1-17)**: §1 hook (phantom 4-fold drop-in cost/latency claim) vs §6 mechanism (L2 verbose runtime overhead) **coherence collapse** — L2 destroys §1 latency benefit
- **Codex #11 (P1-13)**: `aggregate_cost_electricity.py:104` else branch silently 把 B2 当 B1 处理, paper §6 B2 cost story 结构缺失

**L2 partial-trajectory AUROC sanity sim** (`l2_partial_traj_auroc.py`) 实证 confirm F1: 8/12 cells k=3 partial AUROC < 0.65; k=5 viable 6/11 cells; **phantom modes all viable at k=5** (5/5), baseline modes only 1-3/6 — surfaced as paper §5 mechanism sub-finding "phantom modes' verbose signal saturates faster due to truncated-input forcing early commitment".

### 153.4 User 4 Q&A 锁定 v6 fundamental decisions

User 选 recommended option 全部 4 个:
- **D1 Pareto framing**: 2D Cost × SR primary + Latency dominance check secondary. H9/H10 reformulated from SR-superiority → Pareto-non-dominance bootstrap; H11 NEW cascade non-dominance vs component
- **D2 L2 trigger**: phantom-only verbose (k≥5) + universal cycle (max_repeat ≥ 3, url_revisit ≥ 4)
- **D3 Phase 1a scope**: 72 conditions = 36 baseline + 36 router-variant sequential. Wall-clock ×2 ~2-4 weeks DGX
- **D4 §C amendment**: 立即 amend preregistration + Appendix A 2026-05-16 v6 entry, OSF DOI commit 推后到 advisor sync 之后

- `docs/checkpoints/pre_run/preregistration.md` — H9/H10 estimand 改 Pareto non-dominance + H11 NEW + Appendix A 2026-05-16 v6 entry

- `scripts/analysis/l1_archive_simulation.py` — Pipeline + StandardScaler 把 z-score 进 train fold 内 (P1-11); missing-mode 改 invalid 不 fallback inflate (P1-2)

### 153.6 Cross-AI value lesson

**Round 1 codex 75 sec audit 太浅**: 8 findings 中 5 个是 confirm Claude F1/F3/F4, 没独立 attack design layer. User 一句 "感觉太浅" 立即 re-dispatch pre-fire scope (≥10 findings ≥3 OOB explicit). **Round 2 codex 4 min 出 11 findings 3 OOB**, 包含 P0-7 execution-substrate hole + P0-9 FE zero-SE hijack 等 paper-grade fatal catches. Empirical lesson: round-1 confirmation-heavy → re-dispatch with explicit "find new attacks not in {<list>}" instruction is high-leverage move.

**Gemini value 独立**: round 1 + round 2 共 8 OOB catches 在 prose / rhetorical / preregistration coherence 层 (P0-3 fig0g "external" rhetorical trick / P0-4 H9-H10 hypothesis mismatch / P0-8 false advertising / P1-17 §1-§6 coherence collapse). Codex evidence-pipeline catches + Gemini design-rhetoric catches 互补.

## 154. Router v6 → v7 walk-back: learned-only paper-1 + repeated CV (2026-05-16 late) [design][finding] #design #finding

§153 land 几小时后 user 提出关键简化 (Q3): "我们只保留 learned router 在 paper-1?" + (Q4) "5-fold 不够数据量, 换种方式训练吗?". 两个问题各自 trigger paper §6 重大简化 + L1 sim 训练方式升级.

### 154.1 Q3 walk-back: drop cascade, learned-only paper-1

v6 设计 2-layer cascade (L1 LR + L2 cycle + L2 phantom-verbose), 加 3-test Holm 家族 {H9, H10, H11}. 实际 archive simulation + cross-AI stress 已 surface:
- P1 rule-based router (v3 决策树) 实证 archive degenerate (P1 v3 ≡ always_phantom_som)
- L2 verbose anchor AUROC category error: full-episode vs step-3 partial 不能 transfer (L2 sanity sim 8/12 cells 不达 anchor)
- L2 cascade L2 phantom-verbose 跟 L2 cycle 本质上是 Phase 3 deferred M3 retry / M4 two-stage 的 paper-1 absorption, 需 advisor sync overhead
- gemini #4 catch: cascade vs FrugalGPT rebrand risk; gemini #5: phantom_som 14.29% red ceiling fallback dead-end
- Paper §1 hook (phantom 4-fold drop-in cost/latency claim) vs §6 mechanism (L2 verbose overhead) coherence collapse

User Q3 reframe: paper-1 §6 contribution **只**保留 learned router (LR over phantom-augmented mode set + Pareto non-dominance H10). Cascade + L2 + 规则 router 全留 paper-2.

**v7 walk-back 5 edits applied**:
- (a) `proposals_v6.md` → `proposals_v7.md` (cascade scope reduced to paper-2; site-asymmetric viability narrative explicit)
- (b) `preregistration.md` §C: H9 + H11 标 paper-2 DEFERRED (estimand specs 保留为 forward stubs); Holm 家族从 {H9, H10, H11} 缩到 {H10} singleton
- (c) `preregistration.md` Appendix A 2026-05-16 v7 entry written
- (d) `p79/experiment/conditions.py` 加 `phase1.router_kind: "learned"|"cascade"` 子选项; learned (v7+ default) emits 1 router cond/cell with `obs_mode="learned"` sentinel; cascade 保留 6 cond/cell 作 paper-2 forward use
- (e) paper title 简化: "Phantom Routing Space and Cost-Aware Cascade Routing for VLM Web Agents" → "Phantom Routing Space and Cost-Aware Routing for VLM Web Agents" (drop "Cascade")

**Phase 1a condition count 大幅缩**: cascade v6 = 72 cond (36 baseline + 36 router); learned v7 = **42 cond (36 baseline + 6 router)**. 6 cells × 1 learned router cond/cell. Wall-clock A100 从 2-4 weeks 缩到 1.5-2.5 weeks (router pass 3-5 days vs 1-2 weeks).

User Q2 prediction "router cell 还是那么多对吧" verified: cells (statistical strata) unchanged at 6; per-cell router conditions drop 6 → 1.

### 154.2 Q4 walk-back: repeated stratified 5-fold × 10 repeats

cls archive N=234, 5-fold = 47 test/fold + balanced LR + 8 features → 单 5-fold variance 大. v7 改用 **repeated stratified 5-fold × 10 repeats** (50 train-test pairs per variant). 1h script change.

实际数字对比 (cls Variant B vs always_phantom_som 15.81%):

| Method | cls Variant B SR | Δ vs phantom_som | CI |
|---|---|---|---|
| Single 5-fold (v6) | 18.38% | +2.56pp | [13.68, 23.50] (47-task fold variance) |
| **Repeated 5-fold × 10 (v7)** | **17.84%** | **+2.02pp** | **[16.30, 19.42]** (50-pair pooled) |

CI lower bound 16.30 > baseline 15.81, **Variant B cls 仍 statistically beat phantom_som default**. Magnitude 衰减 0.54pp 反映 single-fold optimism.

红 Variant B 仍 -3.95pp (从 -4.76pp 略升), 但 lower bound 9.05 < 14.29 = 仍 fail Pareto vs phantom_som. **Red learned router 仍 collapse to majority on text-dominated archive**, paper §6 site-asymmetric viability finding 保留.

**Paper §6 framing v7-locked**:
- Archive sim 数字 (repeated 5-fold × 10) 作为 development sanity supplementary table
- Paper-grade main number 锁 **Phase 1a LOCO** (Leave-One-Cell-Out: train 5 cells, test 1, repeat 6 times) — Phase 1a 6 cells × N=200-240 = ~1200-1400 task pool 之后可做
- 双层 honest disclosure: "archive +2.02pp cls development; Phase 1a LOCO +Y.YYpp paper-grade"

|---|---|---|

| Phantom 4-fold drop-in §1 hook | coherence at risk | **fully consistent** |
| Cross-AI surfaced P0 still open | 4 (P0-1/2/3/4 partial defuse) | **0 (全 N/A)** |

Walk-back **极大简化 paper §6 contribution shape** + 给 paper-2 加厚 (cascade + mechanism + M3/M4 + cross-family). Reviewer-defense surface 缩窄, contribution sharper.

### §157.2 7-fix remediation (B-230~B-236)

User decision Option **A** (P0 全 4 + P1-2 + P1-3 ≈ 3h):

- **B-230 P0-1** auth gate hard-fail propagation: B-224 (queue_baseline.sh) didn't propagate to 3 phantom scripts. 50% (18/36) Phase 1a conditions shipped pre-fix soft-warn → first 1-3 phantom tasks NOT-LOGGED-IN. Fix: `reset_and_auth_gate()` helper in new `_lib_paper_grade_gates.sh`.
- **B-231 P0-2** BUG-2 URL-locality preflight propagation: `queue_baseline.sh:114-122` had `assert all site URLs local on A100`, phantom siblings missing. Fix: `assert_a100_url_locality()` helper.
- **B-232 P0-3** queue_chain sentinel content-validity: pre-fix file-existence only. FORCE_NEW same-second + mid-write SIGKILL + stale prior-run dir 3 failure modes. Fix: inline Python validates JSON parse + condition_id match + total_tasks > 0.
- **B-233 P0-4** watchdog liveness in chain wait loop: launch-time FATAL invariant not enforced at runtime. Watchdog mid-run death silent → multi-day chain weekend runs lose auth refresh → adjusted_success bias. Fix: `wait_for_runner_done` polls watchdog PID too; death → kill runner + ntfy + abort (Q2 A decision).
- **B-234 P1-2** RUN_ID same-second collision: 1-sec precision + master orchestrator parallel chains. Fix: `mint_run_id` appends `${PID}_R${RANDOM}` — same-second 3-call smoke produced 3 distinct IDs.
- **B-235 P1-3** preflight VWA submodule pin: `evaluator_router` import passes on any branch → B-91 fix loss silent. Fix: new `check_vwa_submodule_lock()` verifies branch `p79-patches` + SHA `f0c835b` + `grep -cP 'if not pred or not pred\.strip\(\):'` = 2.
- **B-236 P1-4** QUARK_TZ vestigial dead code removal: client-side export doesn't influence docker container TZ. Original "3-AI agree 2026-05-16 fix" was cargo cult; A100 self-hosted docker path doesn't go through quark Windows host at all. Fix: 5-line delete + 8-line `BUG-6 NOTE` audit comment retained for future readers.

User pushback during /stress audit: "我的 paper grade run 应该是在 A100 起 docker 啊,这个是不是你的 memory 没有更新"。Audit 发现:
- Memory `reference_compute_resources.md:24` 已写 "**self-host VWA Docker stack ON the A100 VM** — Phase 1 paper-grade runs use A100's own dockerized VWA (cls/red/shop), NOT quark's Docker via Tailscale. Standing decision" (set 2026-05-14)

- 新 memory `project_paper_grade_target_host.md`: 高优先级 surface paper-grade A100 standing decision + 3 vestigial markers (QUARK_TZ / vwa_env_remote.sh Tailscale endpoints / queue script `quark` comments) 警示列表
- CLAUDE.md `运行环境` section: 顶部 disambiguate "Dev session host (this conversation) = DGX Spark; Paper-grade target host = A100 self-hosted docker"

- MEMORY.md index: 新增 1-line pointer 指向 `project_paper_grade_target_host.md`

教训:**memory presence ≠ memory surfaced**. `reference_compute_resources.md:24` 写在中文表格里第三行,我读 memory 时优先看 MEMORY.md index summary,长 reference file 跳过细节 → 知识在 memory 里但没 load 到 active context。修复 = high-freq loader (CLAUDE.md + MEMORY.md index) surface 关键 decision。

### 158.6 Why "hard-delete" 而非 "selective-retain alias"

2026-05-14 §139.8 时 design 是 "alias 保留 == raw counterpart 保 output-schema 稳定"(memory L49)。User overrule 2026-05-16 = 这套 selective-retain 反而是 reviewer-trip vector:
- 字段名 KEY 携带历史语义 (`adjusted_success` 暗示 post-hoc adjusted),VALUE 是 canonical → reviewer 拿 JSON 看到 `adjusted_success` 与 `success` 恒等会困惑 "为啥两字段一样?"
- `fig0b_fp_rate_per_mode` 整 figure 围绕已退役 FP rate (post-§139.8 always 0) → paper figure 画全 0 条 → reviewer "为啥所有 mode FP=0?"
- `_mark_false_positives` 函数 docstring 自己说 "thin alias-setter" → reviewer 读 grep 找 FP detection 找到一个 "always returns 0" 的函数 → cargo-cult perception
- 3-AI 互验 P0-1 denominator transition gap = 同款问题:retire 时只 sweep code path,narrative footprint (docstring / report prose / paper §4 hero table) 没跟上 → 3 处 surface 数字不自洽

Hard-delete 比 alias-retain 更诚实:retire 就 retire,output schema break 一次性发生,fresh aggregator run 写新 schema,legacy `docs/archive/analysis_pre_2026-05-15/` 永久存档不再混用。
