### 162.2 P0-4 codex unique catch — empirically verified LAUNCH BLOCKER

`queue_chain.sh:217-219` pre-fix sentinel:
```python
total = d.get('total_tasks', d.get('num_tasks', d.get('scored_task_count', 0)))
if not isinstance(total, int) or total <= 0: ...
```
Codex Mode B 跑 sample=20 实测 `condition_summary_v2.json` top-level schema — **三个 fallback key 全 None**, 实际 canonical field 是 `episodes: int` (234 cls / 210 red)。Pre-fix 任何 100% completion summary 全 fail validation → chain 跑完 cell 1 立刻 abort → user 被迫 `--no-reset` manual relaunch → 跨 condition contam 风险 soar。

Claude + gemini 都 missed 这条 (gemini 60% file coverage 漏读 queue_chain.sh schema branch; Claude Mode A 没 empirically sample 验)。**Cross-AI Mode B ROI 这一条就回本** — 没 codex Phase 1a fire 直接卡死 cell 1 之后。

Fix B-302: 改用 `episodes` field + hardcode `expected_n` per site (cls=234/red=210/shop=466/wa_*) parsed from run_id pattern + 90% completion threshold。3 case smoke 全 expected: valid 234→exit 0, ep=0→exit 3, partial 100/234=43%→exit 4。

### 162.3 P1-5-B 3-AI brainstorm — alternative solutions

User 主动触发二轮 brainstorm "P1-5-B 你觉得还有哪些方案?可以 fire codex gemini 来想"。Mode A 已知 3 方案 (A discard / B no-reset / C docker snapshot); 用户授权 fire codex+gemini 找 unconventional alternatives。

Codex 出 6 code/protocol-layer options 含 (D-codex) **Prefix Action Replay** (replay 0-119 actions sans-model via Playwright env.step, GPU near zero ~5-20% prefix wallclock) — game-changing 想法; (F-codex) **Per-Cell Disposable Site Namespace** (volume isolation between cells, 0 compute waste, 1-2w engineering); (G-codex) **Logical Mutation Ledger Replay** (semantic mutation replay via API)。

Gemini 出 6 statistical/disclosure-layer options 含 (1-gemini) **GLMM with `is_after_reset` covariate** (4h analysis, absorb reset bias 进 fixed effect); (2-gemini) **Multi-Epoch Sequential Benchmark §3 reframe** (1h paper-§3 文档, transform bug 成 intended protocol); (4-gemini) **Fisher's exact homogeneity rebuttal** (3h reviewer武器准备)。

### 162.4 User cross-talk insight — auto-auth/auto-clear 同构性

User: "auto auth 这个是不是也能够有效果, 现在经常会丢失 login 然后就自动清除 task"。

Cross-talk surface 同构性: P1-5-B (cell interrupt → relaunch+reset) 跟 auth-loss/auto-clear 是同 bug class 两个 layer 的 mid-trajectory perturbation:
- P1-5-B: JSONL 干净, site state 错 (reset 后清掉前 episodes mutation)
- auth-loss/auto-clear: JSONL 干净 (清掉 contaminated attempt), site state 错 (前 attempt partial mutation 残留)

两者 JSONL ↔ site state 不一致方向相反但 bug class 同。Tier 1 analysis-layer fixes 自然 generalize (just add `had_auth_clear` covariate alongside `is_after_reset`)。Option (K) Trajectory Event Log = unified perturbation event schema cover BOTH 类 at zero analysis-cost。

User accept α' = Tier 1 stack + Option K = ~11h total cover BOTH P1-5-B + auth-loss perturbation。Chunk 1 lands code fix (B) `--no-reset` (5min, 立即用); (2-gemini)/(1-gemini)/(4-gemini) paper §3 + GLMM + Fisher 留 Chunk 2 + advisor sync。

### 163.3 Option K Trajectory Event Log (B-313 + B-314, user cross-talk insight)

User §162 之后提出 generalize Tier 1 stack 到 auth-loss/auto-clear class — 同 bug class 两个 layer。设计 Option K 统一 schema:

**Schema (B-313)**: Append-only JSONL `condition_dir/trajectory_events.jsonl`, 每行 = `{event_type, task_index, wallclock_ts, metadata}`. event_type ∈ {`reset_post_interrupt`, `task_auto_cleared`, `auth_refresh_no_clear`, `runner_restart`, `watchdog_intervention`}。

**API (B-313)**:
- `LoggerV2.log_trajectory_event(event_type, task_index, metadata)` — instance method
- `log_trajectory_event_external(condition_dir, event_type, task_index, metadata)` — module helper for out-of-band callers; graceful no-op when dir missing

**Hooks (B-314)**:
1. **`experiment_watchdog.py:1402+`** auto-clean path: log `task_auto_cleared` event, metadata 含 `reason` + `retry_attempt` + `is_noise` + **`is_auth_loss` bool flag** (差异化 auth-loss subset) + `purged_digest_records`。
2. **`_lib_paper_grade_gates.sh:reset_and_auth_gate`** post-reset success: 写 `reset_post_interrupt` 到 staging file `${repo_dir}/logs/trajectory_events_staging/RUN_${RUN_ID}.jsonl` (condition_dir 不存在还); runner-side pickup + merge 留 follow-up。

**Smoke verified end-to-end**:
- missing dir → graceful no-op ✓
- existing dir → 2 events JSONL written, schema correct ✓
- watchdog hook try/except 包裹, 失败非 fatal ✓

### 163.4 Generalization 影响 — analysis layer 自然 cover auth-loss

User cross-talk insight 同构性:
- P1-5-B (cell interrupt → relaunch+reset): JSONL 干净, site state 错 (reset 后清前 episodes mutation)
- auth-loss/auto-clear (watchdog detect+clear+rerun): JSONL 干净 (清掉 contaminated attempt), site state 错 (前 attempt partial mutation 残留)

两者方向相反但 corrupt within-cell single trajectory assumption。Option K 统一 event log → aggregator emits per-episode `is_after_reset` + `had_auth_clear` + `prior_event_count` covariates → paper §4 GLMM (1-gemini Tier 1) absorb BOTH classes 的 bias 进 fixed effect, main treatment effect clean。

**ROI 加成**: Tier 1 stack 原本只 cover P1-5-B reset events (Chunk 1 已 land code fix B-304); Option K (Chunk 2) extension 让同样 analysis investment 同时 cover auth-loss class — 实际更高频 perturbation source。

### 167.7 实操 ROI: spot-check w/o cross-AI dispatch 的边界

按 v7.7 spec 默认 milestone+ scope 必 chain Mode B + C, 但 user 显式打断 → Mode A only。

**Mode A only 的代价**: 没 cross-AI 复审, blind spot 风险高。例如 cross-AI typical 还会 catch (a) prereg.md ↔ plan §A2 内部 cross-ref drift / (b) N=6 cells × DL meta k=6 边界 power 数学 detail / (c) `gemma-3-4b-it` 是否真 "matched-capability" 跟 Qwen3-VL-4B 训练数据对齐细节。这三类 Claude self-review 不容易自抓。

**Mode A only 的边界**: 当 stress target = checklist 体系 (元 audit) 而非 code / claim 时, cross-AI 边际收益 ↓ (Claude self-review 已 catch 主要结构性 gap, cross-AI catch 应该 ≤2 个 marginal finding)。这次 user 判断正确, 节约 ~15-25 min cross-AI dispatch 时间。

**未来同类元 audit 规则提议**: plan-meta / process-artifact / checklist-体系 类 stress → 默认 Mode A only, user 可显式 +B+C upgrade。已经写入 `feedback_*` memory? **暂未** — 这是一个 calibration data point, 待重复出现 2-3 次后才考虑 memorialize 防 over-generalize。

## 167. /stress A1.10 `p79/experiment/{router, modules, state_change, checklist_module, tasks, config}` + consumers + paper §1/§3/§4 prose — 3-AI cycle + 17-fix paper-grade substrate + 8-prose-fix batch (2026-05-16 late) [infra][bug][design][finding] #infra #bug #design #finding

### 167.1 3-AI cycle

## 168. /stress A1.15 watchdog stack + 6-layer auto-clean — Pre-fire 闭环 11-fix batch + Option K critical path closure (2026-05-16 late-night) [infra][bug][design][finding] #infra #bug #design #finding

A1.x ladder 接续 A1.10。Scope = watchdog stack 5 files (`experiment_watchdog.py` 1773 LOC + 3 control scripts ≈ 2111 LOC total). Pre-fire scope per skill v7.6 (8-12 files/side, ≥7 findings, ≥3 OOB). Worktree isolation `../p79-a1.15` branch `a1.15-stress` 防 parallel A1.9/A1.10/A1.12 race。User strategic decision T8=(a) "Pre-fire 闭环" — 1-1.5 day fire delay 换 trajectory_events.jsonl → aggregator covariate trail 在 fire 前完整通端到端。

### 168.1 3-AI cycle output

- **Mode A Claude self**: 11 findings (3 OOB)。Empirical spot-check on 5 reddit DOM 实测 catch B-387 reddit regex inert (5/5 logged-in 0 detect with OLD regex)。OOB strengths: paper-grade bias direction analysis (outcome-dependent retry SR upward), session refresh flag persistence, empirical artifact verification (v7.5 hard rule)。
- **Mode B codex `/codex-stress`**: 9 findings (6 OOB), 362s runtime, 433KB raw output (verbose reasoning trace + final consolidated findings at L5038+)。Unique catches: B-385 session auto-clean missing trajectory hook (NOT just ordering — entirely different code path missing) + B-384 + B-391 `_run_auto_digest` path bug + B-393 corrupt state silent reset + condition_finalize race。Strengths: race-free audit trail thinking, sibling-grep code-bug detection, runner ↔ watchdog race semantics。
- **Mode C gemini `/gemini-stress`**: 10 findings (5 OOB), ~5min runtime, 15KB clean bilingual output, sentinel `=== GEMINI_AUDIT_DONE ===`。Unique catches: O(N·M) purge_digest death spiral (B-392) + 8400-file scan_summaries glob (P2) + 5000-char DOM read limit edge + restart_watchdog /proc race + step_000-only session check mid-trajectory loss。Strengths: scalability instinct, edge-case operational fragility。**Caveat**: line number citations off ~50-70 lines in 3/5 spot checks — use Mode A line numbers when consolidating。

**3-AI互补性 empirically validated**: Mode A unique catches require empirical artifact reading (v7.5 reddit DOM spot-check); Mode B unique catches require whole-pipeline thinking (auto-clean path NOT in Option K = different code path); Mode C unique catches require scalability instinct (NFS I/O, 8400-file glob). 3 lineage 加起来才有 paper-grade coverage — 单 lineage 必漏 ≥30% paper-grade-critical attacks。

### 172.4 P0-1 SE floor data-grounded re-design (user "详细想想" 触发)

User 不直接 pick top-tier option (A) const 1.0pp / (B) N-aware / (C) exclude degenerate, 而要求 "查 archive 数据 + prereg pending 现在是重设计窗口". Grep `meta_phantom_lift.md` 2026-05-09 archive:

| Cell | Lift | SE |
|---|---:|---:|
| B0 cls | +2.56pp | 0.981pp |
| B0 red | +3.33pp | 1.096pp |
| B1 cls | +1.71pp | 0.766pp |

Median archive SE ≈ 0.98pp ≈ const 1.0pp. **0 degenerate cells observed** in 3-cell archive sample. → const 1.0pp is **empirically calibrated**, 不是 arbitrary. Recommend (A) keep const + add Agresti-Coull-style prereg disclosure paragraph anchored to archive median. N-aware (B) 各 cell 不同 floor 引入额外 noise; exclude (C) 违 "fixed cells as design" principle (prereg L586). User implicit confirm via "fix P0+P1 完整" — proceed (A) with explicit prereg disclosure.

### 172.5 P0-3 reframe — Cost Falsification gate location

Gemini Mode C 原 claim: `aggregate_phase1_prereg_gate.py` 缺 cost ratio check. Verify path: `preregistration_decision_test.py:517-557` 已有 `evaluate_h2_cost` reading `cost_dom/cost_psom` per task + `apply_framing_rule()` L601- combines H1+H2+H3 → R1-R5. Gemini 把 H1-only gate `gate_status=PASS` 误读为 paper-§1-全 PASS.

**真 bug** found via grep: (a) `cost_margin_pct: float = 10.0` (L517) + CLI `--H2-cost-margin-pct` default L796 = 10.0 vs prereg.md L131-132 lock 20.0 (1.20× ratio = +20% relative) → margin 2× 严格 → false-falsification 膨胀; (b) `consistent: pass_count >= transparency_K_h2` (L551) = K-of-N transparency, vs prereg L131-132 + L368 "if **ANY** condition violated → falsified" 严格 ALL-pass semantics → K-of-N 错把 H1/H3 transparency 移植到 H2(a) falsification.

Fix landed: `cost_margin_pct` default 10.0 → 20.0; `transparency_K_h2` arg DeprecationWarning + ignored; `consistent` 改 `== n_cells_total`; new fields `n_cells_falsified` + `semantics: "strict_all_pass_falsification_check"` + `prereg_anchor`. CLI default fixed. Smoke synthetic r1_pass: consistent=True, margin=20.0, R1 framing pass.

Paper §1 SR systematically inflated.

### 178.4 User reframing learning — B-91 precedent argument

User initially said "其他 vwa paper 也是这样" (don't fix, match upstream). My first response leaned (a) disclose-only. User then countered: "但是 llm fuzzy match 会有 na fp, 这个我就是上游 patch 的, 所以理论上来说, 这个严格 string 是不是也应该修复?" — point: B-91 patching upstream is precedent that "P79 patches upstream evaluator bugs + discloses divergence". My (a) was inconsistent with B-91 stance. **Lesson**: when applying "match upstream" defense, check existing P79 precedents — if any prior fix patched upstream + disclosed, the precedent IS to patch + disclose, not to preserve buggy upstream. **Recovery**: revise to (b-fix) with user's "no ambiguous middle" constraint.

### 178.5 Cross-paper SR comparability — paper §3.5 evaluator-patch policy

Post-B-535 fix, P79 evaluator is strict-negative-first while VWA / WebArena-Verified / PAE / Aviator-Web etc continue to use upstream's `"correct" in response` substring matcher. This adds a 3rd P79 evaluator divergence point (after `gpt-4o-mini` vs `gpt-4-1106-preview` judge MODEL choice + B-91 empty-pred guard). Paper §3.5 should articulate the "P79 evaluator-patch policy: patch upstream evaluator bugs + disclose divergence; do NOT preserve buggy upstream for direct comparability when bug systematically inflates SR; rely on within-paper paired comparisons for internal validity, NOT cross-paper SR absolute comparison". This policy was implicit in B-91 fix but should be explicit prose for reviewer audit.

### 178.6 Cross-session inline-reference state

This Chunk 4 didn't suffer the cross-session collision from Chunk 3 (B-485~B-490 draft) because:
1. Mode A pre-read at 11:07 re-grep'd "Next available B-number" → B-512+ (parallel A1.21 sed renumber commit `5b08d79`).
2. By the time I committed (TBD timestamp), parallel session's latest claim was B-534 (no further claims in window).
3. My fix B-535-B-541 inline references in submodule + main repo files are stable canonical IDs from the start.

**Lesson institutionalized**: re-grep "Next available" IMMEDIATELY BEFORE inline tagging works in practice when there's no concurrent parallel-session commit between recon and commit. For cross-session collisions like Chunk 3, the only true fix is single-session reservation or post-hoc renumber. /stress SKILL.md should add a step "Phase 0.5: cross-session B-number reservation atomic claim".

(Note for future audits.)
2. **Parallel B-ID collision**: during Chunk 1 commit, parallel session committed B-535~B-541 (A1.25 Chunk 4). My initial B-535 collided → sed renumber B-535→B-542, B-552→B-543. Per CLAUDE.md memory `他session也在工作，你只commit自己的+每次append grep最新序号` — implemented re-grep IMMEDIATELY-before-commit pattern. Catalog reservation note `B-534 left unclaimed for any concurrent parallel-session reservation` proved its value — fits hand-in-glove gap-fill.
3. **Paper-prose chunk defer**: parallel session has uncommitted `section1_intro.md` + `section3_*.md` + `section4_*.md` edits → my Chunk 4 catalog/chronicle/plan-tick proceeds (no overlap with parallel), but paper §3.5 disclosure update for B-545 + paper §4.X.6 strengthen for B-512 + paper §1 hero TBD for B-545 (gemini original) all DEFERRED to post-parallel-reconcile to avoid working-tree collision. **Institutional rule**: identify parallel session's M list at start of chunked work, partition chunks by file scope, defer overlap to user-reconcile slot.
4. **Codex scheduled-fire 限额 pattern carry-over from Phase 1**: This session codex available immediately (no quota wait), but lesson from Phase 1 (`sleep 3767 && codex_cmd &` pattern) remains useful precedent for future quota-bound dispatches.

**Phase 0 self-audit catch**: Mode A initial published 4 findings during cold-read; foreground verification batch (post-B+C dispatch) surfaced 3 additional fresh attack vectors (B2 gallery alias gap, empty URL silent pass, stale resume fingerprint) that landed in unified bug list before B+C returned.

**Workflow lessons** (encoded for future audit cycles):
1. **Comment-vs-code drift in claim verification** — B-640 mint_run_id comment promised `%N nanoseconds` but `date +%Y%m%d_%H%M%S` had no `%N`. Always grep the actual `date` format string against the comment claim.
2. **Bash parameter expansion is NOT python f-string** — `${var!r}` is `${var@op}` form with invalid op `r` → bad substitution. Python heredoc embedded in bash needs env-var pass-through not literal interpolation.
3. **`set -e` mid-script flip changes errexit for ALL subsequent lines** — explicit guard logic written assuming no errexit becomes dead code. Decide errexit policy at script top once.
4. **Substring break-first ordering is structurally wrong** — longest-prefix-first or structural parse beats `for site in $LIST; do if substring; then; fi; done` (WA shopping site-key disaster).
5. **Empty string in case "OK" branch silently passes failures** — `case ... in ...|"") ;; *) FATAL` lets `vwa_env_remote.sh` source failure (empty URLs) pretend everything's fine. Separate explicit "" → FATAL branch.
6. **Sibling propagation across 4-queue-script + lib pattern** — any new gate / env / arg must propagate to all 5 callers. Today's audit caught 6+ propagation defects (B-638 B2 alias / B-644 ntfy env / B-645 positional args / B-647 WA reset / earlier B-230 series).
7. **Silent skip on benchmark-specific branch is paper-grade-fatal** — replace with hard-fail until proper gate exists (B-647 WA skip ≠ "supported on WA, just different mechanism").
8. **TOCTOU pgrep collision check needs lock** — flock-based per-(site,benchmark) lock spans check + launch + sentinel; non-blocking acquire → second-chain FATAL exit better than infinite-wait.
9. **History data is paper-grade evidence**: 23/24 historical paper-grade fires were 100% exact (cls/red 234/210 + B0_dom_shopping 99.78% pre-fix). Sentinel "90% threshold" was lazy default not reality-driven. Empirical history > developer intuition.

**Test net**: pytest 428 passed / 8 skipped at chunks (a)/(b)/(c)/(e) boundaries; 2 failed at chunk (d) boundary but **unrelated** — VWA submodule SHA drift from parallel A1.5b/A1.22 session committed pin without updating `EXPECTED_SUBMODULE_SHA` constant in `tests/test_stress_a1_4b_ii_g4_fixes.py`. A1.13 introduced 0 test regression.

**Closing**: A1.13 launch+collision substrate audit **closed**. 19 fixes consume B-630~B-648; B-647 is scaffold pending (a) A100 WA Docker state verification (b) Phase 1b WA scope advisor sync (c) auth_refresh WA benchmark extension. Phase 1a fire green-light **STILL ON** post-A1.13 for VWA (the 18 non-scaffold fixes close all VWA paper-grade paths). Push deferred per `feedback_git_push_requires_confirm`.
